                    0013  kConfMinor: .EQU '0'            ;Config: 1 to 9 = official, 0 = user
                    0014  kCode:      .EQU 0x0000         ;Typically 0x0000 or 0xE000
                    0015  kData:      .EQU 0xFC00         ;Typically 0xFC00 (to 0xFFFF)
                    0016  kROMBanks:  .EQU 1              ;Number of software selectable ROM banks
                    0017  kROMTop:    .EQU 0x1F           ;Top of banked ROM (hi byte only)
                    0018  #ENDIF
                    0019

                    0021  ; All official RC2014 systems can run this
                    0022  ; Paged ROM board set for 8k page size
                    0023  ; 64k RAM board set to start at 8k (not page enabled)
                    0024  #IF         BUILD = "R1"
                    0025  kConfMinor: .EQU '1'            ;Config: 1 to 9 = official, 0 = user

                    0027  kData:      .EQU 0xFC00         ;Typically 0xFC00 (to 0xFFFF)
                    0028  kROMBanks:  .EQU 1              ;Number of software selectable ROM banks
                    0029  kROMTop:    .EQU 0x1F           ;Top of banked ROM (hi byte only)
                    0030  #ENDIF

4000:               0036  kConfMinor:

0000:               0037  kCode:      .EQU 0x0000         ;Typically 0x0000 or 0xE000
FC00:               0038  kData:      .EQU 0xFC00         ;Typically 0xFC00 (to 0xFFFF)

                    0041  #ENDIF

                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  Include SCM core modules                  by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **********************************************************************
                    0004  
                    0005  ; Source code version number: Major.Minor.Revision (.Touch)
                    0006  ; This is the version number of the core code and does not include
                    0007  ; the hardware drivers. 
                    0008  ; This version number is only changed when the source code no longer
                    0009  ; produces the same binary code for all existing builds.
                    0010  ; Cosmetic changes to the source and changes only affecting new
                    0011  ; hardware do not require the version number to change, only the
                    0012  ; 'Touch' date (which is an invisible part of the version number).
                    0013  ; Hardware drivers have their own configuration code and revision
                    0014  ; numbers: kConfMajor, kConfMinor and kConfRevis
4000:               0015  kVersMajor:
                    0015  kVersMajor: .EQU 1
4000:               0016  kVersMinor:
                    0016  kVersMinor: .EQU 0
4000:               0017  kVersRevis:
                    0017  kVersRevis: .EQU 0
                    0018  ;VersTouch: .EQU 20190124       ;Last date core code touched
                    0019  
                    0020  
                    0021  
4000:               0022  StartOfMonitor:
                    0023              .EQU kCode          ;Start of monitor code
                    0024  
                    0025  ; Essential modules
                    0026  
                    0027  ; Core OS functions
                    0028  #INCLUDE    Monitor\Alpha.asm   ;This must be the first #include
                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  Alpha module                              by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **********************************************************************
                    0004  
                    0005  ; This module provides the following:
                    0006  ;   Defines the memory map (except kCode and kData)
                    0007  ;   Reset code / Cold start command line interpreter
                    0008  ;   Warm start command line interpreter
                    0009  ;
                    0010  ; Public functions provided:
                    0011  ;   ColdStart             Cold start monitor
                    0012  ;   WarmStart             Warm start monitor
                    0013  ;   InitJumps             Initialise jump table with vector list
                    0014  ;   ClaimJump             Claim jump table entry
                    0015  ;   ReadJump              Read jump table entry
                    0016  ;   MemAPI                Call API with parameters in RAM
                    0017  ;   SelConDev             Select console in/out device
                    0018  ;   SelConDevI            Select console input device
                    0019  ;   SelConDevO            Select console output device
                    0020  ;   DevInput              Input from specified console device
                    0021  ;   DevOutput             Output to specified console device
                    0022  ;   Delay                 Delay by sepecified number of millseconds
                    0023  ;   GetConDev             Get current console device numbers
                    0024  ;   GetMemTop             Get top of free memory
                    0025  ;   SetMemTop             Set top of free memory
                    0026  ;   GetVersion            Get version and configuration details
                    0027  ;   OutputMessage         Output specified embedded message
                    0028  ;   SetBaud               Set baud rate for console devices
                    0029  ;   SysReset              System reset
                    0030  
                    0031  
                    0032  ; **********************************************************************
                    0033  ; **  Constants                                                       **
                    0034  ; **********************************************************************
                    0035  
                    0036  ; Memory map (ROM or RAM)
0000:               0037  Reset:      .EQU 0x0000         ;Z80 reset location
                    0038  
                    0039  ; Memory map (RAM)
                    0040  ; 0xFC00 to 0xFCBF  User stack
                    0041  ; 0xFCC0 to 0xFCFF  System stack
                    0042  ; 0xFD00 to 0xFD7F  Line input buffer
                    0043  ; 0xFD80 to 0xFDFF  String buffer
                    0044  ; 0xFE00 to 0xFE5F  Jump table
                    0045  ; 0xFE60 to 0xFEFF  Workspace (currently using to about 0xFEAF)
                    0046  ; 0xFF00 to 0xFFFF  Pass info between apps and memory banks:
                    0047  ; 0xFF00 to 0xFF7F    Transient data area
                    0048  ; 0xFF80 to 0xFFEF    Transient code area
                    0049  ; 0xFFD0 to 0xFFDF    ROMFS file info block 2
                    0050  ; 0xFFE0 to 0xFFEF    ROMFS file info block 1
                    0051  ; 0xFFF0 to 0xFFFF    System variables
FCC0:               0052  kSPUsr:     .EQU kData+0x00C0   ;Top of stack for user program
FD00:               0053  kSPSys:     .EQU kData+0x0100   ;Top of stack for system
4000:               0054  kInputBuff:
                    0054  kInputBuff: .EQU kData+0x0100   ;Line input buffer start    (to +0x017F)
4000:               0055  kInputSize:
                    0055  kInputSize: .EQU 128            ;Size of input buffer
4000:               0056  kStrBuffer:
                    0056  kStrBuffer: .EQU kData+0x0180   ;String buffer              (to +0x01FF)
0080:               0057  kStrSize:   .EQU 128            ;Size of string buffer
FE00:               0058  kJumpTab:   .EQU kData+0x0200   ;Redirection jump table     (to +0x025F)
                    0059  ;kWorkspace:                    .EQU kData+0x0260;Space for data & variables (to +0x02FF)
                    0060  ; Pass information between apps and memory banks 0xFF00 to 0xFFFF
FF00:               0061  kPassData   .EQU 0xFF00         ;0xFF00 to 0xFF7F Transient data area
FF80:               0062  kPassCode:  .EQU 0xFF80         ;0xFF80 to 0xFFEF Transient code area
FFF0:               0063  kPassInfo:  .EQU 0xFFF0         ;0xFFF0 to 0xFFFF Variable passing area
FFF0:               0064  kPassCtrl:  .EQU kPassInfo+0x00 ;Pass control / paging information
FFF2:               0065  kPassAF:    .EQU kPassInfo+0x02 ;Pass AF to/from API
FFF4:               0066  kPassBC:    .EQU kPassInfo+0x04 ;Pass BC to/from API
FFF6:               0067  kPassDE:    .EQU kPassInfo+0x06 ;Pass DE to/from API
FFF8:               0068  kPassHL:    .EQU kPassInfo+0x08 ;Pass HL --/from API
FFFA:               0069  kPassDevI:  .EQU kPassInfo+0x0A ;Pass current input device
FFFB:               0070  kPassDevO:  .EQU kPassInfo+0x0B ;Pass current output device
                    0071  
                    0072  
                    0073  ; Common constants
0000:               0074  kNull       .EQU 0              ;Null character/byte (0x00)
0005:               0075  kNewLine    .EQU 5              ;New line character (0x05)
4000:               0076  kBackspace:
                    0076  kBackspace: .EQU 8              ;Backspace character (0x08)
000A:               0077  kLinefeed:  .EQU 10             ;Line feed character (0x0A)
000D:               0078  kReturn:    .EQU 13             ;Return character (0x0D)
001B:               0079  kEscape:    .EQU 27             ;Escape character (0x1B)
0020:               0080  kSpace:     .EQU 32             ;Space character (0x20)
4000:               0081  kApostroph:
                    0081  kApostroph: .EQU 39             ;Apostrophe character (0x27)
002C:               0082  kComma:     .EQU 44             ;Comma character (0x2C)
002E:               0083  kPeriod:    .EQU 46             ;Period character (0x2E)
003A:               0084  kColon:     .EQU 58             ;Colon character (0x3A)
4000:               0085  kSemicolon:
                    0085  kSemicolon: .EQU 59             ;Semicolon character (0x3B)
007F:               0086  kDelete:    .EQU 127            ;Delete character (0x7F)
                    0087  
                    0088  ; The jump table contains a number of "JP nn" instructions which are
                    0089  ; used to redirect functions. Each entry in the table takes 3 bytes.
                    0090  ; The jump table is created in RAM on cold start of the monitor.
                    0091  ; Jump table constants - jump number (0 to n)
0000:               0092  kFnNMI:     .EQU 0x00           ;Fn 0x00: non-maskable interrupt handler
0001:               0093  kFnRST08:   .EQU 0x01           ;Fn 0x01: restart 08 handler
0002:               0094  kFnRST10:   .EQU 0x02           ;Fn 0x02: restart 10 handler
0003:               0095  kFnRST18:   .EQU 0x03           ;Fn 0x03: restart 18 handler
0004:               0096  kFnRST20:   .EQU 0x04           ;Fn 0x04: restart 20 handler
0005:               0097  kFnRST28:   .EQU 0x05           ;Fn 0x05: restart 18 breakpoint
0006:               0098  kFnRST30:   .EQU 0x06           ;Fn 0x06: restart 30 API handler
0007:               0099  kFnINT:     .EQU 0x07           ;Fn 0x07: restart 38 interrupt handler
0008:               0100  kFnConIn:   .EQU 0x08           ;Fn 0x08: console input character
0009:               0101  kFnConOut:  .EQU 0x09           ;Fn 0x09: console output character
                    0102  ;FnConISta: .EQU 0x0A           ;Fn 0x0A: console get input status
                    0103  ;FnConOSta: .EQU 0x0B           ;Fn 0x0B: console get output status
000C:               0104  kFnIdle:    .EQU 0x0C           ;Fn 0x0C: Jump to idle handler
000D:               0105  kFnTimer1:  .EQU 0x0D           ;Fn 0x0D: Jump to timer 1 handler
000E:               0106  kFnTimer2:  .EQU 0x0E           ;Fn 0x0E: Jump to timer 2 handler
000F:               0107  kFnTimer3:  .EQU 0x0F           ;Fn 0x0F: Jump to timer 3 handler
                    0108  ;FnDevN:    .EQU 0x10           ;Fn 0x10: device 1 to n input & output
0010:               0109  kFnDev1In:  .EQU 0x10           ;Fn 0x10: device 1 input
4000:               0110  kFnDev1Out:
                    0110  kFnDev1Out: .EQU 0x11           ;Fn 0x11: device 1 output
                    0111  ;kFnDev2In: .EQU 0x12           ;Fn 0x12: device 2 input
                    0112  ;FnDev2Out: .EQU 0x13           ;Fn 0x13: device 2 output
0014:               0113  kFnDev3In:  .EQU 0x14           ;Fn 0x14: device 3 input
                    0114  ;FnDev3Out: .EQU 0x15           ;Fn 0x15: device 3 output
                    0115  ;FnDev4In:  .EQU 0x16           ;Fn 0x16: device 4 input
                    0116  ;FnDev4Out: .EQU 0x17           ;Fn 0x17: device 4 output
                    0117  ;FnDev5In:  .EQU 0x18           ;Fn 0x18: device 5 input
                    0118  ;FnDev5Out: .EQU 0x19           ;Fn 0x19: device 5 output
001A:               0119  kFnDev6In:  .EQU 0x1A           ;Fn 0x1A: device 6 input
                    0120  ;FnDev6Out: .EQU 0x1B           ;Fn 0x1B: device 6 output
                    0121  
                    0122  ; Message numbers
0000:               0123  kMsgNull:   .EQU 0              ;Null message
4000:               0124  kMsgProdID:
                    0124  kMsgProdID: .EQU 1              ;Product identifier
4000:               0125  kMsgDevice:
                    0125  kMsgDevice: .EQU 2              ;="Devices:"
0003:               0126  kMsgAbout:  .EQU 3              ;About SCMonitor inc version
4000:               0127  kMsgDevLst:
                    0127  kMsgDevLst: .EQU 4              ;Device list
4000:               0128  kMsgLstSys:
                    0128  kMsgLstSys: .EQU 4              ;Last system message number
                    0129  
                    0130  ; Page zero use 
                    0131  ; SCMonitor: page zero can be in RAM or ROM
                    0132  ; CP/M: page zero must be in RAM
                    0133  ; <Address>   <Z80 function>   <Monitor>   <CP/M 2>
                    0134  ; 0000-0002   RST 00 / Reset   Cold start  Warm boot
                    0135  ; 0003-0004                    Warm start  IOBYTE / drive & user
                    0136  ; 0005-0007                    As CP/M     FDOS entry point
                    0137  ; 0008-000B   RST 08           Char out    Not used
                    0138  ; 000C-000F                    CstartOld   Not used
                    0139  ; 0010-0013   RST 10           Char in     Not used
                    0140  ; 0014-0017                    WstartOld   Not used
                    0141  ; 0018-001F   RST 18           In status   Not used
                    0142  ; 0020-0027   RST 20           Not used    Not used
                    0143  ; 0028-002F   RST 28           Breakpoint  Debugging
                    0144  ; 0030-0037   RST 30           API entry   Not used
                    0145  ; 0038-003F   RST 38 / INT     Interrupt   Interrupt mode 1 handler
                    0146  ; 0040-005B                    Options     Not used
                    0147  ; 005C-007F                    As CP/M     Default FCB
                    0148  ; 0066-0068   NMI              or Non-maskable interrupt (NMI) handler
                    0149  ; 0080-00FF                    As CP/M     Default DMA
                    0150  
                    0151  
                    0152  ; **********************************************************************
                    0153  ; **  Initialise memory sections                                      **
                    0154  ; **********************************************************************
                    0155  
                    0156  ; Initialise data section
                    0157              .DATA
                    0158  
                    0159              .ORG  kData
                    0160  
                    0161              .ORG kJumpTab
                    0162  
FE00: C3 00 00      0163  JpNMI:      JP   0              ;Fn 0x00: Jump to non-maskable interrupt
FE03: C3 00 00      0164  JpRST08:    JP   0              ;Fn 0x01: Jump to restart 08 handler
FE06: C3 00 00      0165  JpRST10:    JP   0              ;Fn 0x02: Jump to restart 10 handler
FE09: C3 00 00      0166  JpRST18:    JP   0              ;Fn 0x03: Jump to restart 18 handler
FE0C: C3 00 00      0167  JpRST20:    JP   0              ;Fn 0x04: Jump to restart 20 handler
FE0F: C3 00 00      0168  JpBP:       JP   0              ;Fn 0x05: Jump to restart 28 breakpoint
FE12: C3 00 00      0169  JpAPI:      JP   0              ;Fn 0x06: Jump to restart 30 API handler
FE15: C3 00 00      0170  JpINT:      JP   0              ;Fn 0x07: Jump to restart 38 interrupt handler
FE18: C3 00 00      0171  JpConIn:    JP   0              ;Fn 0x08: Jump to console input character
FE1B: C3 00 00      0172  JpConOut:   JP   0              ;Fn 0x09: Jump to console output character
FE1E: C3 00 00      0173              JP   0              ;Fn 0x0A: Jump to console get input status
FE21: C3 00 00      0174              JP   0              ;Fn 0x0B: Jump to console get output status
FE24: C3 00 00      0175  JpIdle:     JP   0              ;Fn 0x0C: Jump to idle handler
FE27: C3 00 00      0176  JpTimer1:   JP   0              ;Fn 0x0D: Jump to timer 1 handler
FE2A: C3 00 00      0177  JpTimer2:   JP   0              ;Fn 0x0E: Jump to timer 2 handler
FE2D: C3 00 00      0178  JpTimer3:   JP   0              ;Fn 0x0F: Jump to timer 3 handler
                    0179              ;Fn 0x10: Start of console device jumps
FE30: C3 00 00      0180              JP   0              ;Jump to device 1 input character
FE33: C3 00 00      0181              JP   0              ;Jump to device 1 output character
FE36: C3 00 00      0182              JP   0              ;Jump to device 2 input character
FE39: C3 00 00      0183              JP   0              ;Jump to device 2 output character
FE3C: C3 00 00      0184              JP   0              ;Jump to device 3 input character
FE3F: C3 00 00      0185              JP   0              ;Jump to device 3 output character
FE42: C3 00 00      0186              JP   0              ;Jump to device 4 input character
FE45: C3 00 00      0187              JP   0              ;Jump to device 4 output character
FE48: C3 00 00      0188              JP   0              ;Jump to device 5 input character
FE4B: C3 00 00      0189              JP   0              ;Jump to device 5 output character
FE4E: C3 00 00      0190              JP   0              ;Jump to device 6 input character
FE51: C3 00 00      0191              JP   0              ;Jump to device 6 output character
                    0192  
FE54: FF FF FF FF   0193              .DS   12            ;Workspace starts at kJumpTab + 0x60
FE58: FF FF ...     0415  
                    0194  ;           .ORG  kWorkspace
                    0195  
                    0196  ; Initialise code section
                    0197              .CODE
                    0198              .ORG  kCode
                    0199  
                    0200  
                    0201  ; **********************************************************************
                    0202  ; **  Page zero default vectors etc, copied to RAM if appropriate     **
                    0203  ; **********************************************************************
                    0204  
                    0205  ; Reset / power up here
0000:               0206  Page0Strt:
0000: C3 BD 00      0207  ColdStart:  JP   ColdStrt       ;0x0000  CP/M 2 Warm boot
0003: 18 0F         0208  WarmStart:  JR   WStrt          ;0x0003  CP/M 2 IOBYTE / drive & user
0005: C3 64 05      0209              JP   FDOS           ;0x0005  CP/M 2 FDOS entry point
0008: C3 03 FE      0210              JP   JpRST08        ;0x0008  RST 08 Console character out
000B: 00            0211              .DB  0              ;0x000B

000F: 00            0213              .DB  0              ;0x000F

0013: 00            0215              .DB  0              ;0x0013
0014: C3 4B 01      0216  WStrt:      JP   WarmStrt       ;0x0014  Warm start (unofficial entry)
0017: 00            0217              .DB  0              ;0x0017
0018: C3 09 FE      0218              JP   JpRST18        ;0x0018  RST 18 Console input status
001B: 00 00 00 00   0219              .DB  0,0,0,0,0      ;0x001B
001F: 00            0441  
0020: C3 0C FE      0220              JP   JpRST20        ;0x0020  RST 20 Not used
0023: 00 00 00 00   0221              .DB  0,0,0,0,0      ;0x0023
0027: 00            0443  
0028: C3 0F FE      0222              JP   JpBP           ;0x0028  RST 28 Our debugging breakpoint
002B: 00 00 00 00   0223              .DB  0,0,0,0,0      ;0x002B         and CP/M debugging tools
002F: 00            0445  
0030: C3 12 FE      0224              JP   JpAPI          ;0x0030  RST 30 API entry point
0033: 00            0225              .DB  0              ;0x0033         parameters in registers

003F: 00            0451

004C: 00 00 00 00   0457

                    0283  ; Cold start Command Line Interpreter
00BD: F3            0284  ColdStrt:   DI                  ;Disable interrupts
00BE: 31 00 FD      0285              LD   SP,kSPSys      ;Initialise system stack pointer
                    0286  ; Self test included?
                    0287  #IFDEF      IncludeSelftest
                    0288  ; This indicates status on the default output port (LEDs)
                    0289  ; At the end of a sucessful self test the default output port is cleared 
                    0290  ; to zero, otherwise the default output port indicates the failure
                    0291  #IF         BUILD = "S2"
                    0292  #INCLUDE    Hardware\SC_S2\Selftest.asm
                    0293  #DEFINE     CUSTOM_SELFTEST
                    0294  #ENDIF
                    0295  #IFNDEF     CUSTOM_SELFTEST
                    0296  #INCLUDE    Monitor\Selftest.asm  ;Include self test functions
                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  Self test at reset                        by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **********************************************************************
                    0004  
                    0005  ; This module provides a self test function that runs at reset.
                    0006  ;
                    0007  ; The code is written to be INCLUDED in-line during the early stages
                    0008  ; following a reset. It therefore does not end in a RET instruction.
                    0009  ;
                    0010  ; Initially the digital status port LEDs each flash in turn. This 
                    0011  ; will run even if there is no RAM.
                    0012  ;
                    0013  ; A very simple RAM test using just location 0xFFFE and 0xFFFF is then
                    0014  ; performed. If it fails the self test repeats from the beginning, so
                    0015  ; the LEDs keep flashing if the RAM fails.
                    0016  ;
                    0017  
                    0018  ; Must be defined in hardware manager module. eg:
                    0019  ;kPrtOut:    .EQU 0              ;Assume digital status port is present
                    0020  
                    0021  
                    0022  ; **********************************************************************
                    0023  ; **  Public functions                                                **
                    0024  ; **********************************************************************
                    0025  
                    0026              .CODE
                    0027  
                    0028  ; Initialially we assume that there is no RAM so we avoid subroutines.
                    0029  
                    0030  ; Flash LEDs in turn to show we get as far as running code
00C1: 11 01 00      0031  Selftest:   LD   DE,1           ;Prepared for delay loop
00C4: 7B            0032              LD   A,E            ;First byte to write to LEDs = 0x01
00C5: D3 00         0033  @Loop1:     OUT  (kPrtOut),A    ;Write to LEDs
00C7: 21 C0 E1      0034              LD   HL,0xE1C0      ;Set delay time (approx 8000 loops)
00CA: 19            0035  @Delay1:    ADD  HL,DE          ;Delay loop increments HL until
00CB: 30 FD         0036              JR   NC,@Delay1     ;  it reaches zero
00CD: 07            0037              RLCA                ;Rotate LED bit left
00CE: 30 F5         0038              JR   NC,@Loop1      ;Repeat until last LED cleared
00D0: AF            0039              XOR  A              ;Clear A
00D1: D3 00         0040              OUT  (kPrtOut),A    ;All LEDs off
                    0041  
                    0042  ; Very brief RAM test
00D3: 21 FF FF      0043              LD   HL,0xFFFF      ;Location to be tested
00D6: 3E 55         0044              LD   A,0x55         ;Test pattern 01010101
00D8: 77            0045              LD   (HL),A         ;Store 01010101 at 0xFFFF
00D9: 2B            0046              DEC  HL             ;HL = 0xFFFE
00DA: 2F            0047              CPL                 ;Invert bits to 10101010
00DB: 77            0048              LD   (HL),A         ;Store 10101010 at 0xFFFE
00DC: 23            0049              INC  HL             ;HL = 0xFFFF
00DD: 2F            0050              CPL                 ;Invert bits to 01010101
00DE: BE            0051              CP   (HL)           ;Test 01010101 at 0xFFFF
00DF: 20 E0         0052              JR   NZ,Selftest    ;Failed, so restart
00E1: 2B            0053              DEC  HL             ;HL = 0xFFFE
00E2: 2F            0054              CPL                 ;Invert bits to 10101010
00E3: BE            0055              CP   (HL)           ;Test 10101010 at 0xFFFE
00E4: 20 DB         0056              JR   NZ,Selftest    ;Failed so restart
                    0057  ; Repeat with all tests inverted
00E6: 2F            0058              CPL                 ;Invert bits to 01010101
00E7: 77            0059              LD   (HL),A         ;Store 01010101 at 0xFFFE
00E8: 23            0060              INC  HL             ;HL = 0xFFFF
00E9: 2F            0061              CPL                 ;Invert bits to 10101010
00EA: 77            0062              LD   (HL),A         ;Store 10101010 at 0xFFFF
00EB: 2B            0063              DEC  HL             ;HL = 0xFFFE
00EC: 2F            0064              CPL                 ;Invert bits to 01010101
00ED: BE            0065              CP   (HL)           ;Test 01010101 at 0xFFFE
00EE: 20 D1         0066              JR   NZ,Selftest    ;Failed, so restart
00F0: 23            0067              INC  HL             ;HL = 0xFFFF
00F1: 2F            0068              CPL                 ;Invert bits to 10101010
00F2: BE            0069              CP   (HL)           ;Test 10101010 at 0xFFFF
00F3: 20 CC         0070              JR   NZ,Selftest    ;Failed, so restart
                    0071  
00F5:               0072  SelftestEnd:
00F5: AF            0073              XOR  A
00F6: D3 00         0074              OUT  (kPrtOut),A    ;All LEDs off
                    0075  
                    0076  
                    0077  ; **********************************************************************
                    0078  ; **  End of Self test module                                         **
                    0079  ; **********************************************************************
                    0080  
                    0081  
                    0082  
                    0297  #ENDIF
                    0298  #ENDIF
                    0299  ; Copy vectors etc to page zero in case code is elsewhere
00F8: 11 00 00      0300              LD   DE,0x0000      ;Copy vectors etc to here
00FB: 21 00 00      0301              LD   HL,Page0Strt   ;Copy vectors etc from here
00FE: 01 69 00      0302              LD   BC,Page0End-Page0Strt  ;Number of bytes to copy
0101: ED B0         0303              LDIR                ;Copy bytes
                    0304  ; Initialise jump table, other than console devices
0103: 11 00 FE      0305              LD   DE,kJumpTab    ;Copy jump table to here
0106: 21 69 00      0306              LD   HL,JumpStrt    ;Copy jump table from here
0109: 01 54 00      0307              LD   BC,JumpEnd-JumpStrt  ;Number of bytes to copy
010C: ED B0         0308              LDIR                ;Copy bytes
                    0309  ; Initialise top of memory value
010E: 21 FF FB      0310              LD   HL,kData-1     ;Top of free memory
0111: 22 60 FE      0311              LD   (iMemTop),HL   ;Set top of free memory
                    0312  ; Initialise ports module for default I/O ports
                    0313  ; This will turn off all outputs at the default output port (LEDs)
0114: 3E 00         0314              LD   A,kPrtOut      ;Default output port address
0116: CD A5 04      0315              CALL PrtOInit       ;Initialise output port
0119: 3E 00         0316              LD   A,kPrtIn       ;Default input port address
011B: CD D3 04      0317              CALL PrtIInit       ;Initialise input port
                    0318  ; Initialise hardware and set up required jump table entries
                    0319  ; This may indicate an error at the default output port (LEDs)
011E: CD AE 1D      0320              CALL Hardware_Initialise
                    0321  ; Initialise default console device to first physical device
0121: 3A 40 00      0322              LD   A,(kaConDev)   ;Default device number
0124: CD B3 01      0323              CALL SelConDev      ;Select console device
                    0324  ; Initialise rest of system
0127: CD EE 02      0325              CALL ConInitialise  ;Initialise the console
                    0326  #IFDEF      IncludeScripting
                    0327              CALL ScrInitialise  ;Initialise script language
                    0328  #ENDIF
                    0329  #IFDEF      IncludeRomFS
012A: CD E3 1A      0330              CALL RomInitialise  ;Initialise ROM filing system
                    0331  #ENDIF
                    0332  ; Output sign-on message
012D: CD 20 04      0333              CALL OutputNewLine  ;Output new line
0130: CD 20 04      0334              CALL OutputNewLine  ;Output new line
0133: 3E 01         0335              LD   A,kMsgProdID   ;="Small Computer Monitor"
0135: CD 23 02      0336              CALL OutputMessage  ;Output message
0138: 3E 2D         0337              LD   A,'-'          ;="-"
013A: CD 0B 04      0338              CALL OutputChar     ;Output character
013D: 3E 20         0339              LD   A,kSpace       ;=" "
013F: CD 0B 04      0340              CALL OutputChar     ;Output character
0142: 11 A7 1D      0341              LD   DE,szStartup   ;="<hardware>"
0145: CD 28 04      0342              CALL OutputZString  ;Output message at DE
0148: CD 20 04      0343              CALL OutputNewLine  ;Output new line
                    0344  #IFNDEF     IncludeCommands
                    0345              CALL OutputNewLine  ;Output new line
                    0346              LD   A,kMsgAbout    ;="Small Computer Monitor ..."
                    0347              CALL OutputMessage  ;Output message A
                    0348              CALL OutputNewLine  ;Output new line
                    0349              LD   A,kMsgDevice   ;="Devices:"
                    0350              CALL OutputMessage  ;Output message A
                    0351              LD   A,kMsgDevLst   ;="<device list>"
                    0352              CALL OutputMessage  ;Output message A
                    0353  #ENDIF
                    0354  
                    0355  ; Warm start Command Line Interpreter
0000:               0356  WarmStrt:
014B: 31 00 FD      0357              LD   SP,kSPSys      ;Initialise system stack pointer
                    0358  #IFDEF      IncludeBreakpoint
014E: CD FF 0D      0359              CALL BPInitialise   ;Initialise breakpoint module
                    0360  #ENDIF
                    0361  #IFDEF      IncludeCommands
0151: C3 72 0F      0362              JP   CLILoop        ;Command Line Interpreter main loop
                    0363  #ELSE
                    0364  @Halt:      JR   @Halt          ;Halt here if no CLI
                    0365  #ENDIF
                    0366  
                    0367  ; Trap unused entry points
                    0368  #IFNDEF     IncludeAPI
                    0369  API:
                    0370  #ENDIF
                    0371  #IFNDEF     IncludeFDOS
                    0372  FDOS:
                    0373  #ENDIF
                    0374  #IFNDEF     IncludeBreakpoint
                    0375  BPHandler:
                    0376  #ENDIF
0154: C9            0377  TrapCALL:   RET                 ;Return from entry point
                    0378  
                    0379  ; Trap unused mode 1 interrupt
0155: ED 4D         0380  TrapINT:    RETI                ;Return from interrupt
                    0381  
                    0382  ; Trap unused non-maskabler interrupt
0157: ED 45         0383  TrapNMI:    RETN                ;Return from interrupt
                    0384  
                    0385  ; Default console device routines
0000:               0386  DevNoIn:
0159: AF            0387  DevNoOut:   XOR   A             ;Z flagged as no input or output
015A: C9            0388              RET                 ;Return have done nothing
                    0389  
                    0390  
                    0391  ; **********************************************************************
                    0392  ; **  Public functions                                                **
                    0393  ; **********************************************************************
                    0394  
                    0395  
                    0396  ; System: Initialise jump table entries
                    0397  ;   On entry: A = First jump table entry to initialise
                    0398  ;             B = Number of entries to be initialised
                    0399  ;             HL = Pointer to list of vectors
                    0400  ;   On exit:  C IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
015B: 5E            0401  InitJumps:  LD   E,(HL)         ;Get lo byte of vector
015C: 23            0402              INC  HL             ;Point to hi byte of vector
015D: 56            0403              LD   D,(HL)         ;Get lo byte of vector
015E: 23            0404              INC  HL             ;Point to next vector
015F: CD 66 01      0405              CALL ClaimJump      ;Claim jump table entry
0162: 3C            0406              INC  A              ;Increment entry number
0163: 10 F6         0407              DJNZ InitJumps      ;Repeat until done
0165: C9            0408              RET
                    0409  
                    0410  
                    0411  ; System: Claim system jump table entry
                    0412  ;   On entry: A = Entry number (0 to n)
                    0413  ;             DE = Address of function
                    0414  ;   On exit:  No parameters returned
                    0415  ;             AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0416  ; Some system functions, such as console in and console out, are 
                    0417  ; redirected through a jump table. By claiming a jump table entry the
                    0418  ; function can be handled by any required code. This might allow
                    0419  ; swapping output to a different device, such as a printer.
0166: F5            0420  ClaimJump:  PUSH AF
0167: C5            0421              PUSH BC
0168: E5            0422              PUSH HL
0169: 21 00 FE      0423              LD   HL,kJumpTab    ;Start of jump table
016C: 06 00         0424              LD   B,0            ;Calculate offset and store in BC..
016E: 4F            0425              LD   C,A            ;C = 3 times A..
016F: 87            0426              ADD  A              ;x2
0170: 81            0427              ADD  C              ;x3
0171: 4F            0428              LD   C,A
0172: 09            0429              ADD  HL,BC          ;Find location in table...
                    0430  ; Write jump table entry to memory
0173: 36 C3         0431              LD   (HL),0xC3      ;Store jump instruction
0175: 23            0432              INC  HL
0176: 73            0433              LD   (HL),E         ;Store routine address lo byte
0177: 23            0434              INC  HL
0178: 72            0435              LD   (HL),D         ;Store routine address hi byte
0179: E1            0436              POP  HL
017A: C1            0437              POP  BC
017B: F1            0438              POP  AF
017C: C9            0439              RET
                    0440  
                    0441  
                    0442  ; System: API call with parameters passed via memory
                    0443  ;   On entry: Memory locations kPassXX contain register values
                    0444  ;   On exit:  Memory locations kPassXX contain register values
017D: 2A F2 FF      0445  MemAPI:     LD   HL,(kPassAF)   ;Get AF parameter from RAM
0180: E5            0446              PUSH HL             ;Pass AF parameter via stack
0181: F1            0447              POP  AF             ;Get AF parameter from stack
0182: ED 4B F4 FF   0448              LD   BC,(kPassBC)   ;Get BC parameter from RAM
0186: ED 5B F6 FF   0449              LD   DE,(kPassDE)   ;Get DE parameter from RAM
018A: 2A F8 FF      0450              LD   HL,(kPassHL)   ;Get HL parameter from RAM
018D: F7            0451              RST  0x30           ;Call API
018E: F5            0452              PUSH AF             ;Pass AF result via stack
018F: E1            0453              POP  HL             ;Get AF result from stack
0190: 22 F2 FF      0454              LD   (kPassAF),HL   ;Store AF result in RAM
0193: 22 F8 FF      0455              LD   (kPassHL),HL   ;Store HL result in RAM
0196: ED 53 F6 FF   0456              LD   (kPassDE),DE   ;Store DE result in RAM
019A: ED 43 F4 FF   0457              LD   (kPassBC),BC   ;Store BC result in RAM
019E: C9            0458              RET
                    0459  
                    0460  
                    0461  ; System: Read system jump table entry
                    0462  ;   On entry: A = Entry number (0 to n)
                    0463  ;   On exit:  DE = Address of function
                    0464  ;             AF BC HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0465  ; Some system functions, such as console in and console out, are 
                    0466  ; redirected through a jump table. By claiming a jump table entry the
                    0467  ; function can be handled by any required code. This might allow
                    0468  ; swapping output to a different device, such as a printer.
019F: F5            0469  ReadJump:   PUSH AF
01A0: C5            0470              PUSH BC
01A1: E5            0471              PUSH HL
01A2: 21 01 FE      0472              LD   HL,kJumpTab+1  ;Start of jump table + 1
01A5: 06 00         0473              LD   B,0            ;Calculate offset and store in BC..
01A7: 4F            0474              LD   C,A            ;C = 3 times A..
01A8: 87            0475              ADD  A              ;x2
01A9: 81            0476              ADD  C              ;x3
01AA: 4F            0477              LD   C,A
01AB: 09            0478              ADD  HL,BC          ;Find location in table...
                    0479  ; Write jump table entry to memory
01AC: 5E            0480              LD   E,(HL)         ;Store routine address lo byte
01AD: 23            0481              INC  HL
01AE: 56            0482              LD   D,(HL)         ;Store routine address hi byte
01AF: E1            0483              POP  HL
01B0: C1            0484              POP  BC
01B1: F1            0485              POP  AF
01B2: C9            0486              RET
                    0487  
                    0488  
                    0489  ; System: Select console device
                    0490  ;   On entry: A = New console device number (1 to n)
                    0491  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0492  ; The console device list starts at jump table entry kFnDevN.
                    0493  ; Each device has two entries: input and output
                    0494  ; SelConDev  = Select both input and output device
                    0495  ; SelConDevO = Select output device only
                    0496  ; SelConDevI = Select input device only
01B3: CD C9 01      0497  SelConDev:  CALL SelConDevI     ;Select console input device
                    0498  ;           JP   SelConDevO     ;Select console output device
                    0499  ; Select output device
01B6:               0500  SelConDevO:
01B6: F5            0500  SelConDevO: PUSH AF
01B7: D5            0501              PUSH DE
01B8: 32 FB FF      0502              LD   (kPassDevO),A  ;Store output device number
01BB: 87            0503              ADD  A,A            ;Double as two entries each
01BC: C6 0F         0504              ADD  kFnDev1Out-2   ;Function number for device zero
01BE: CD 9F 01      0505              CALL ReadJump       ;Read source entry
01C1: 3E 09         0506              LD   A,kFnConOut    ;Destination device entry number
01C3: CD 66 01      0507              CALL ClaimJump      ;Write destination entry
01C6: D1            0508              POP  DE
01C7: F1            0509              POP  AF
01C8: C9            0510              RET
                    0511  ; Select input device
01C9:               0512  SelConDevI:
01C9: F5            0512  SelConDevI: PUSH AF
01CA: D5            0513              PUSH DE
01CB: 32 FA FF      0514              LD   (kPassDevI),A  ;Store input device number
01CE: 87            0515              ADD  A,A            ;Double as two entries each
01CF: C6 0E         0516              ADD  kFnDev1In-2    ;Function number for device zero
01D1: CD 9F 01      0517              CALL ReadJump       ;Read source entry
01D4: 3E 08         0518              LD   A,kFnConIn     ;Destination device entry number
01D6: CD 66 01      0519              CALL ClaimJump      ;Write destination entry
01D9: D1            0520              POP  DE
01DA: F1            0521              POP  AF
01DB: C9            0522              RET
                    0523  
                    0524  
                    0525  ; System: Input from specified console device
                    0526  ;   On entry: E = Console device number (1 to n)
                    0527  ;   On exit:  A = Character input 9if there is one ready)
                    0528  ;             NZ flagged if character has been input
                    0529  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
01DC: 7B            0530  DevInput:   LD   A,E            ;Get console device number
01DD: 87            0531              ADD  A,A            ;Double as two entries each
01DE: C6 0E         0532              ADD  kFnDev1In-2    ;Function number for device zero
01E0: CD 9F 01      0533              CALL ReadJump       ;Read table entry
01E3: EB            0534              EX   DE,HL          ;Get routine address in HL
01E4: E9            0535              JP   (HL)           ;Jump to input routine
                    0536  
                    0537  
                    0538  ; System: Output to specified console device
                    0539  ;   On entry: A = Character to be output
                    0540  ;             E = Console device number (1 to n)
                    0541  ;   On exit:  IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
01E5: F5            0542  DevOutput:  PUSH AF             ;Store character to be output
01E6: 7B            0543              LD   A,E            ;Get console device number
01E7: 87            0544              ADD  A,A            ;Double as two entries each
01E8: C6 0F         0545              ADD  kFnDev1Out-2   ;Function number for device zero
01EA: CD 9F 01      0546              CALL ReadJump       ;Read table entry
01ED: EB            0547              EX   DE,HL          ;Get routine address in HL
01EE: F1            0548              POP  AF             ;Restore character to be output
01EF: E9            0549              JP   (HL)           ;Jump to output routine
                    0550  
                    0551  
                    0552  ; System: Delay by specified number of millseconds
                    0553  ;   On entry: DE = Delay time in milliseconds
                    0554  ;   On exit:  BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0555  ; Clock =  1.0000 MHz,  1 ms =  1,000 TCy =  40 * 24 - 36 
                    0556  ; Clock =  4.0000 MHz,  1 ms =  4,000 TCy = 165 * 24 - 36
                    0557  ; Clock =  7.3728 MHz,  1 ms =  7,373 TCy = 306 * 24 - 36
                    0558  ; Clock = 12.0000 MHz,  1 ms = 12,000 TCy = 498 * 24 - 36
                    0559  ; Clock = 20.0000 MHz,  1 ms = 20,000 TCy = 831 * 24 - 36
01F0: C5            0560  Delay:      PUSH BC
01F1: D5            0561              PUSH DE
                    0562  ; 1 ms loop, DE times... (overhead = 36 TCy)
01F2: 01 32 01      0563  @LoopDE:    LD   BC,kDelayCnt   ;[10]  Loop counter
                    0564  ; 26 TCy loop, BC times...
01F5: 0B            0565  @LoopBC:    DEC  BC             ;[6]
01F6: 79            0566              LD   A,C            ;[4]
01F7: B0            0567              OR   B              ;[4]
01F8: C2 F5 01      0568              JP   NZ,@LoopBC     ;[10]
01FB: 1B            0569              DEC  DE             ;[6]
01FC: 7B            0570              LD   A,E            ;[4]
01FD: B2            0571              OR   D              ;[4]
01FE: 20 F2         0572              JR   NZ,@LoopDE     ;[12/7]
0200: D1            0573              POP  DE
0201: C1            0574              POP  BC
0202: C9            0575              RET
                    0576  
                    0577  
                    0578  ; System: Get current console device numbers
                    0579  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0580  ;   On exit:  D = Current console output device number
                    0581  ;             E = Current console input device number
                    0582  ;   On exit:  AF BC HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
0203: ED 5B FA FF   0583  GetConDev:  LD   DE,(kPassDevI) ;Get console device numbers
0207: C9            0584              RET
                    0585  
                    0586  
                    0587  ; System: Get top of free memory
                    0588  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0589  ;   On exit:  DE = Top of free memory
                    0590  ;   On exit:  AF BC HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
0208: ED 5B 60 FE   0591  GetMemTop:  LD   DE,(iMemTop)   ;Get top of free memory
020C: C9            0592              RET
                    0593  
                    0594  
                    0595  ; System: Set top of free memory
                    0596  ;   On entry: DE = Top of free memory
                    0597  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
020D: ED 53 60 FE   0598  SetMemTop:  LD   (iMemTop),DE   ;Set top of free memory
0211: C9            0599              RET
                    0600  
                    0601  
                    0602  ; System: Get version details
                    0603  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0604  ;   On exit:  D,E and A = Monitor code version
                    0605  ;               D = kVersMajor
                    0606  ;               E = kVersMinor
                    0607  ;               A = kVersRevis(ion)
                    0608  ;             B,C = Configuration ID
                    0609  ;               B = kConfMajor ('R'=RC2014, 'L'=LiNC80, etc)
                    0610  ;               C = kConfMinor (sub-type '1', '2', etc)
                    0611  ;             H,L = Target hardware ID
                    0612  ;               H = kHardID (1=Simulator, 2=,SCDevKt, 3=RC2014, etc)
                    0613  ;               L = Hardware option flags (hardware specific)
                    0614  ;             IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
0212:               0615  GetVersion:
0212: 26 03         0616              LD  H,kHardID       ;H = Hardware ID
0214: 3A AF FE      0617              LD  A,(iHwFlags)    ;Get hardware option flags
0217: 6F            0618              LD  L,A             ;L = Hardware option flags
0218: 06 52         0619              LD  B,kConfMajor    ;B = Major configuration
021A: 0E 32         0620              LD  C,kConfMinor    ;C = Minor configuration 
021C: 16 01         0621              LD  D,kVersMajor    ;D = Major version number
021E: 1E 00         0622              LD  E,kVersMinor    ;E = Minor version number
0220: 3E 00         0623              LD  A,kVersRevis    ;A = Revision number
0222: C9            0624              RET
                    0625  
                    0626  
                    0627  ; System: Output message
                    0628  ;  On entry:  A = Message number (0 to n)
                    0629  ;   On exit:  BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
0223:               0630  OutputMessage:
0223: B7            0631              OR   A              ;Null message?
0224: C8            0632              RET  Z              ;Yes, so abort
0225: D5            0633              PUSH DE             ;Preserve DE
0226: E5            0634              PUSH HL             ;Preserve HL
                    0635  ; Monitor message?
                    0636  #IFDEF      IncludeMonitor
0227: CD 69 08      0637              CALL MonOutputMsg   ;Offer message number to monitor
022A: B7            0638              OR   A              ;Message still needs handling?
022B: 28 31         0639              JR   Z,@Exit        ;No, so exit
                    0640  #ENDIF
                    0641  ; Add any other message generating modules here
                    0642  ; ...........
                    0643  ; System message?
022D: FE 05         0644              CP   kMsgLstSys+1   ;Valid system message number?
022F: 30 2D         0645              JR   NC,@Exit       ;No, so abort
                    0646  ; About message?
0231: FE 03         0647              CP   kMsgAbout      ;About message?
0233: 20 11         0648              JR   NZ,@NotAbout   ;No, so skip
0235: 11 73 02      0649              LD   DE,szProduct   ;="Small Computer Monitor"
0238: CD 28 04      0650              CALL OutputZString  ;Output message at DE
023B: 11 9E 02      0651              LD   DE,szAbout     ;="<about this configuration>"
023E: CD 28 04      0652              CALL OutputZString  ;Output message at DE
0241: CD 4F 1E      0653              CALL Hardware_Signon  ;Hardware signon message
0244: 18 18         0654              JR   @Exit
0000:               0655  @NotAbout:
                    0656  ; Device list message?
0246: FE 04         0657              CP   kMsgDevLst     ;Device list message?
0248: 20 05         0658              JR   NZ,@NotDevLst  ;No, so skip
                    0659  ;           LD   DE,szDevices   ;="Devices:"
                    0660  ;           CALL OutputZString  ;Output message at DE
024A: CD 6F 1E      0661              CALL Hardware_Devices ;Output device list
024D: 18 0F         0662              JR   @Exit
024F:               0663  @NotDevLst:
                    0664  ; Other system message?
024F: 5F            0665              LD   E,A            ;Get message number
0250: 16 00         0666              LD   D,0
0252: 21 E8 02      0667              LD   HL,MsgTabSys   ;Get start of message table
0255: 19            0668              ADD  HL,DE          ;Calculate location in table
0256: 19            0669              ADD  HL,DE
0257: 7E            0670              LD   A,(HL)         ;Get address from table...
0258: 23            0671              INC  HL
0259: 56            0672              LD   D,(HL)
025A: 5F            0673              LD   E,A
025B: CD 28 04      0674              CALL OutputZString  ;Output message as DE
025E: E1            0675  @Exit:      POP  HL             ;Restore HL
025F: D1            0676              POP  DE             ;Restore DE
0260: C9            0677              RET
                    0678  
                    0679  
                    0680  ; System: Set baud rate
                    0681  ;  On entry:  A = Device identifier (0x01 to 0x06, or 0x0A to 0x0B)
                    0682  ;             E = Baud rate code 
                    0683  ;   On exit:  IF successful: (ie. valid device and baud code)
                    0684  ;               A != 0 and NZ flagged
                    0685  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
0261: FE 0A         0686  SetBaud:    CP   0x0A           ;Identifier is a hex letter?
0263: 38 02         0687              JR   C,@GotNum      ;No, so skip
0265: D6 09         0688              SUB  0x09           ;Convert 0x0A/B to 0x01/2
0267: 4F            0689  @GotNum:    LD   C,A            ;Get device identifier (0x01 to 0x06)
0268: 7B            0690              LD   A,E            ;Get baud rate code
                    0691  ; Set baud rate for device C (1 to 6) to baud code A
0269: C3 33 1E      0692              JP   Hardware_BaudSet ;Failure: A=0 and Z flagged
                    0693  
                    0694  
                    0695  ; System: System reset
                    0696  ;  On entry:  A = Reset type: 
                    0697  ;               0 = Cold start monitor
                    0698  ;               1 = Warm start monitor
                    0699  ;   On exit:  System resets
026C: FE 01         0700  SysReset:   CP   0x01           ;Warm start monitor
026E: CA 03 00      0701              JP   Z,WarmStart    ;Yes, so warm start monitor
0271: C7            0702              RST  0              ;Cold start monitor
                    0703  
                    0704  
                    0705  ; **********************************************************************
                    0706  ; **  Constant data                                                   **
                    0707  ; **********************************************************************
                    0708  
                    0709  ; Message strings (zero terminated)
0272: 00            0710  szNull:     .DB  kNull
0273: 53 6D 61 6C   0711  szProduct:  .DB  "Small Computer Monitor ",kNull
0277: 6C 20 ...     1015  
028B: 44 65 76 69   0712  szDevices:  .DB  "Devices detected:",kNewLine,kNull
028F: 63 65 ...     1016  
0000:               0713  szAbout:
029E: 62 79 20 53   0714              .DB  "by Stephen C Cousins (www.scc.me.uk)",kNewLine
02A2: 74 65 ...     1018

02C7: 69 6F 6E 20   1019

02CD: 30 2E         0717              .DB  '0'+kVersMinor,'.'

02EC: 8B 02         0729              .DW  szDevices
                    0730  ;           .DW  szAbout        ;Handled in code
                    0731  ;           .DW  szDevList      ;Handled in code
