                    0029  #INCLUDE    Monitor\Console.asm ;Console support
                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  Console support (input and output)        by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **********************************************************************
                    0004  
                    0005  ; This module handles console input and output. The console is normally
                    0006  ; a serial terminal or terminal emulation program.
                    0007  ;
                    0008  ; Public functions provided
                    0009  ;   ConInitialise         Initialise console module
                    0010  ;   InputInsert           Insert character to character waiting buffer
                    0011  ;   InputMore             Ask if user wants to output more
                    0012  ;   InputPreview          Await one character but leave it to be input
                    0013  ;   InputChar             Input character from console input device
                    0014  ;   InputBufConvUpper     Convert contents of input buffer to upper case
                    0015  ;   InputLineEdit         Input line where existing line is replaced
                    0016  ;   InputLine             Input line to system line buffer
                    0017  ;   InputLineTo           Input line to specified line buffer
                    0018  ;   InputStatus           Check if input character is available
                    0019  ;   OutputChar            Output character to console output device
                    0020  ;   OutputNewLine         Output new line character(s)
                    0021  ;   OutputZString         Output a zero (null) terminated string
                    0022  
                    0023  
                    0024  ; **********************************************************************
                    0025  ; **  Public functions                                                **
                    0026  ; **********************************************************************
                    0027  
                    0028              .CODE
                    0029  
                    0030  ; Console: Initialise console module
                    0031  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0032  ;   On exit:  BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
02EE:               0033  ConInitialise:
02EE: AF            0034              XOR  A
02EF: 32 62 FE      0035              LD   (iConInChar),A
02F2: C9            0036              RET
                    0037  
                    0038  
                    0039  ; **********************************************************************
                    0040  ; **  Input form console / input device, typically a terminal         **
                    0041  ; **********************************************************************
                    0042  
                    0043  
                    0044  ; Console input: Insert character into character waiting buffer
                    0045  ;   On entry: A = ASCII character
                    0046  ;   On exit:  A BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
02F3:               0047  InputInsert:
02F3: 32 62 FE      0048              LD   (iConInChar),A ;Store as character waiting
02F6: C9            0049              RET
                    0050  
                    0051  
                    0052  ; Console input: Ask if user wants to output more
                    0053  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0054  ;   On exit:  A = ASCII character
                    0055  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0056  ; No prompt is given, it just waits until character is available. 
                    0057  ; If the character is the request for 'more' character (eg. Return) the 
                    0058  ; routine exits with the character in A and the Z flag set. 
                    0059  ; If the character is Escape then the routine exits with A = 0 and
                    0060  ; NZ flagged.
                    0061  ; Otherwise the character is stored in iConInChar and will be picked up 
                    0062  ; on next call to InputGetWaiting.
02F7: CD 24 FE      0063  InputMore:  CALL JpIdle         ;Process idle events
02FA: CD 18 FE      0064              CALL JpConIn        ;Wait for input character
02FD: 28 F8         0065              JR   Z,InputMore    ;Repeat until we get a character
02FF: FE 0A         0066              CP   kLinefeed      ;More (linefeed) ?
0301: 28 F4         0067              JR   Z,InputMore    ;Ignore line feed
                    0068  ;           RET  Z              ;Yes, so return with Z flagged
0303: FE 0D         0069              CP   kReturn        ;More (carriage return) ? 
0305: C8            0070              RET  Z              ;Yes, so return with Z flagged
0306: FE 1B         0071              CP   kEscape        ;Escape character ?
0308: 28 04         0072              JR   Z,@Escape      ;Yes, so skip
030A: 32 62 FE      0073              LD   (iConInChar),A ;Store as character waiting
030D: C9            0074              RET                 ;  and return with NZ flagged
030E: AF            0075  @Escape:    XOR  A              ;Clear A
030F: FE 01         0076              CP   1              ;Flag NZ as not a request for more
0311: C9            0077              RET
                    0078  
                    0079  
                    0080  ; Console input: Await one character but leave it waiting to be input
                    0081  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0082  ;   On exit:  A = ASCII character
                    0083  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
0312:               0084  InputPreview:
0312: 3A 62 FE      0085              LD   A,(iConInChar) ;Read from character waiting buffer
0315: B7            0086              OR   A              ;Is there a character waiting?
0316: C0            0087              RET  NZ             ;If character is waiting then return it
0317: CD 18 FE      0088  @Wait:      CALL JpConIn        ;Wait for input character
031A: 20 05         0089              JR   NZ,@GotOne     ;Skip if we get a character
031C: CD 24 FE      0090              CALL JpIdle         ;Process idle events
031F: 18 F6         0091              JR   @Wait          ;Repeat until we get a character
0321: 32 62 FE      0092  @GotOne:    LD   (iConInChar),A ;Store as character waiting
0324: C9            0093              RET
                    0094  
                    0095  
                    0096  ; Console input: Input character from console input device
                    0097  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0098  ;   On exit:  A = ASCII character
                    0099  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0100  ; Waits until character is available
0000:               0101  InputChar:
0325: 3A 62 FE      0102              LD   A,(iConInChar) ;Read from character waiting buffer
0328: B7            0103              OR   A              ;Is there a character waiting?
0329: 28 07         0104              JR   Z,@Input       ;No, so go get new character..
                    0105  ; Return character which is waiting to be read
032B: F5            0106              PUSH AF             ;Preserve character
032C: AF            0107              XOR  A              ;Flush waiting buffer by
032D: 32 62 FE      0108              LD   (iConInChar),A ;  storing zero in to
0330: F1            0109              POP  AF             ;Restore character 
0331: C9            0110              RET
                    0111  ; Get new character from console input device
0332: CD 18 FE      0112  @Input:     CALL JpConIn        ;Look for input character
0335: C0            0113              RET  NZ             ;Exit if we have a character
0336: CD 24 FE      0114              CALL JpIdle         ;Process idle events
0339: 18 F7         0115              JR   @Input         ;No character so keep looking
                    0116  
                    0117  
                    0118  ; Console input: Check if input character is available
                    0119  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0120  ;   On exit:  NZ flagged if character is available
                    0121  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0122  ; Does not wait for a character to become available
033B:               0123  InputStatus:
033B: 3A 62 FE      0124              LD   A,(iConInChar) ;Read from character waiting buffer
033E: B7            0125              OR   A              ;Is there a character waiting?
033F: C0            0126              RET  NZ             ;If character is waiting return
0340: CD 18 FE      0127              CALL JpConIn        ;Look for input character
0343: C8            0128              RET  Z              ;No character to exit
0344: 32 62 FE      0129              LD   (iConInChar),A ;Store character to read later
0347: C9            0130              RET
                    0131  
                    0132  
                    0133  ; Console input: Convert contents of input buffer to upper case
                    0134  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0135  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0136  ; One exception is the character following an apostrophe
0348:               0137  InputBufConvUpper:
0348: F5            0138              PUSH AF
0349: E5            0139              PUSH HL
034A: 2A 63 FE      0140              LD   HL,(iConInBuf) ;Get start of current input buffer
034D: 7E            0141  @Loop:      LD   A,(HL)         ;Get character from input buffer
034E: B7            0142              OR   A              ;Null string?
034F: 28 12         0143              JR   Z,@Done        ;Yes, so we're done here
                    0144  ; Do not convert character immediately following an apostrophe
0351: FE 27         0145              CP   kApostroph     ;Apostrophe?
0353: 20 07         0146              JR   NZ,@NotApos    ;No, so skip
0355: 23            0147              INC  HL             ;Skip apostrophe
0356: 7E            0148              LD   A,(HL)         ;Get character from input buffer
0357: B7            0149              OR   A              ;Null string?
0358: 28 09         0150              JR   Z,@Done        ;Yes, so we're done here
035A: 18 04         0151              JR   @Next          ;Go to next character
0000:               0152  @NotApos:
                    0153  ; Convert this character to upper case
035C: CD DE 06      0154              CALL ConvertCharToUCase
035F: 77            0155              LD   (HL),A         ;Write upper case char to string
0360: 23            0156  @Next:      INC  HL             ;Point ot next character in string
0361: 18 EA         0157              JR   @Loop          ;Loop until end of string
0363: E1            0158  @Done:      POP  HL
0364: F1            0159              POP  AF
0365: C9            0160              RET
                    0161  
                    0162  
                    0163  ; Console input: Input line to system line buffer
                    0164  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0165  ;   On exit:  DE = Start location of buffer
                    0166  ;             A = Number of characters in buffer
                    0167  ;             Z flagged if no characters in bufffer
                    0168  ;             BC HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0169  ;             Interrupts enabled during this call ???
                    0170  ; The number of characters returned does not include the null terminator.
0366: 11 00 FD      0171  InputLine:  LD   DE,kInputBuff  ;Start of system line buffer
0369: AF            0172              XOR  A
036A: 12            0173              LD   (DE),A         ;Clear input buffer
036B: 3E 7F         0174              LD   A,kInputSize-1 ;Length of system line buffer
036D: C3 7F 03      0175              JP   InputLineNow   ;Input line
                    0176  ;           CALL InputLineNow   ;Input line
                    0177  ;           RET
                    0178  
                    0179  
                    0180  ; Console input: Edit line in default string buffer
                    0181  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0182  ;   On exit:  DE = Start location of buffer
                    0183  ;             A = Number of characters in buffer
                    0184  ;             Z flagged if no characters in bufffer
                    0185  ;             BC HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0186  ;             Interrupts enabled during this call ???
                    0187  ; The number of characters returned does not include the null terminator.
                    0188  ; DE points to a null terminated string to be edited
0370:               0189  InputLineEdit:
0370: 11 00 FD      0190              LD   DE,kInputBuff  ;Start of system line buffer
0373: CD C8 05      0191              CALL StrCopyToZ     ;Copy current string to input buffer
0376: 3E 7F         0192              LD   A,kInputSize-1 ;Length of system line buffer
0378: C3 7F 03      0193              JP   InputLineNow   ;Input line
                    0194  ;           CALL InputLineNow   ;Input line
                    0195  ;           RET
                    0196  
                    0197  
                    0198  ; Console input: Input line to user defined buffer
                    0199  ;   On entry: DE = Start of buffer
                    0200  ;             A = Size of input buffer in bytes
                    0201  ;   On exit:  A = Number of characters in input buffer
                    0202  ;             Z flagged if no characters in bufffer ???
                    0203  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0204  ;             Interrupts enabled during this call ???
                    0205  ; Maximum buffer length 255 bytes.
                    0206  ; The buffer length includes the string termination character (null).
                    0207  ; The number of characters returned does not include the null terminator.
037B:               0208  InputLineTo:
037B: F5            0209              PUSH AF
037C: AF            0210              XOR  A
037D: 12            0211              LD   (DE),A         ;Clear input buffer
037E: F1            0212              POP  AF
                    0213  ;           JP   InputLineNow   ;Input line
                    0214  
                    0215  
                    0216  ; Console input: Input or edit line at specified location 
                    0217  ;   On entry: DE = Start of buffer
                    0218  ;             A = Size of input buffer in bytes
                    0219  ;   On exit:  A = Number of characters in input buffer
                    0220  ;             Z flagged if no characters in bufffer ???
                    0221  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0222  ;             Interrupts enabled during this call ???
                    0223  ; Maximum buffer length 255 bytes.
                    0224  ; The buffer length includes the string termination character (null).
                    0225  ; The number of characters returned does not include the null terminator.
037F:               0226  InputLineNow:
037F: D5            0227              PUSH DE
0380: E5            0228              PUSH HL
0381: ED 53 63 FE   0229              LD (iConInBuf),DE   ;Store start of buffer
0385: 6F            0230              LD   L,A            ;Store end address of buffer lo byte
0386: 2D            0231              DEC  L              ;Allow space for return character
0387: 63            0232              LD   H,E            ;Store start address of buffer lo byte
                    0233              ;XOR  A
                    0234              ;LD   (DE),A        ;Clear input buffer
                    0235  ; Check if we are overtyping an existing string
0388: 1A            0236              LD   A,(DE)         ;Get first character in buffer
0389: B7            0237              OR   A              ;Null (zero)?
038A: 28 12         0238              JR   Z,@Input       ;Yes, so skip
                    0239  ; Output current string in buffer
038C: CD 0B 04      0240  @Loop1:     CALL OutputChar     ;Output character
038F: 13            0241              INC  DE             ;Point to next character
0390: 1A            0242              LD   A,(DE)         ;Get character from buffer
0391: B7            0243              OR   A              ;Null (zero)?
0392: 20 F8         0244              JR   NZ,@Loop1      ;No, so go output it
                    0245  ; Backspace to start of string
0394: 3E 08         0246  @Loop2:     LD   A,kBackspace   ;ASCII backspace
0396: CD 0B 04      0247              CALL OutputChar     ;Output backspace
0399: 1B            0248              DEC  DE             ;Back one character
039A: 7B            0249              LD   A,E            ;Get start address of buffer lo byte
039B: BC            0250              CP   H              ;Start of buffer?
039C: 20 F6         0251              JR   NZ,@Loop2      ;No, so go backspace again
                    0252  ; Input line to buffer
039E: CD 25 03      0253  @Input:     CALL InputChar      ;Wait for input character
03A1: CB 7F         0254              BIT  7,A            ;Reject if bit 7 set
03A3: 20 F9         0255              JR   NZ,@Input
03A5: FE 1B         0256              CP   kEscape        ;Test if Escape character
03A7: 28 38         0257              JR   Z,@Escape
03A9: FE 08         0258              CP   kBackspace     ;Test if Delete character
03AB: 28 3C         0259              JR   Z,@Backspace
03AD: FE 0D         0260              CP   kReturn        ;Test if carriage return
03AF: 28 50         0261              JR   Z,@Return
03B1: FE 20         0262              CP   kSpace         ;Reject if control char
03B3: 38 E9         0263              JR   C,@Input
                    0264  ; Normal character (ASCII 32 to 126), consider edit mode
                    0265  ; If start of line and in edit mode, erase the line
03B5: F5            0266              PUSH AF
03B6: 7B            0267              LD   A,E            ;Get start address of buffer lo byte
03B7: BC            0268              CP   H              ;Start of buffer?
03B8: 20 1A         0269              JR   NZ,@EndErase   ;No, so skip
03BA: 1A            0270              LD   A,(DE)         ;Get first character in line
03BB: B7            0271              OR   A              ;Anything to edit?
03BC: 28 16         0272              JR   Z,@EndErase    ;No, so skip
                    0273  ; Erase string being edited
03BE: 3E 20         0274  @Loop3:     LD   A,kSpace       ;ASCII space
03C0: CD 0B 04      0275              CALL OutputChar     ;Erase character from terminal
03C3: AF            0276              XOR  A
03C4: 12            0277              LD   (DE),A         ;Erase character from buffer
03C5: 13            0278              INC  DE             ;Point to next character
03C6: 1A            0279              LD   A,(DE)         ;Get character from buffer
03C7: B7            0280              OR   A              ;Null (zero)?
03C8: 20 F4         0281              JR   NZ,@Loop3      ;No, so go output it
03CA: 3E 08         0282  @Loop4:     LD   A,kBackspace   ;ASCII backspace
03CC: CD 0B 04      0283              CALL OutputChar     ;Output backspace
03CF: 1B            0284              DEC  DE             ;Back one character
03D0: 7B            0285              LD   A,E            ;Get start address of buffer lo byte
03D1: BC            0286              CP   H              ;Start of buffer?
03D2: 20 F6         0287              JR   NZ,@Loop4      ;No, so go backspace again
03D4: F1            0288  @EndErase:  POP  AF
                    0289  ; Normal character (ASCII 32 to 126), write to buffer
03D5: 12            0290              LD   (DE),A         ;Store character in buffer
03D6: 7B            0291              LD   A,E            ;Get current address lo byte
03D7: BD            0292              CP   L              ;Buffer full?
03D8: 28 C4         0293              JR   Z,@Input       ;Yes, so don't increment pointer
03DA: 1A            0294              LD   A,(DE)         ;No, so..
03DB: CD 0B 04      0295              CALL OutputChar     ;  echo character just input
03DE: 13            0296              INC  DE             ;  and increment buffer pointer
03DF: 18 BD         0297              JR   @Input
                    0298  ; Escape character
03E1: CD 20 04      0299  @Escape:    CALL OutputNewLine  ;Output new line character(s)
03E4: 3E 1B         0300              LD   A,kEscape
03E6: BF            0301              CP   A              ;Set zero flag but with A = kEscape
03E7: 18 1F         0302              JR   @Exit
                    0303  ; Delete character
03E9:               0304  @Backspace:
03E9: 7B            0304  @Backspace: LD   A,E            ;Get start address of buffer lo byte
03EA: BC            0305              CP   H              ;Start of buffer?
03EB: 28 B1         0306              JR   Z,@Input       ;Yes, so nothing to delete
03ED: 3E 08         0307              LD   A,kBackspace   ;ASCII backspace
03EF: CD 0B 04      0308              CALL OutputChar     ;Output backspace
03F2: 3E 20         0309              LD   A,kSpace       ;ASCII space
03F4: CD 0B 04      0310              CALL OutputChar     ;Output space
03F7: 3E 08         0311              LD   A,kBackspace   ;ASCII backspace
03F9: CD 0B 04      0312              CALL OutputChar     ;Output backspace
03FC: 1B            0313              DEC  DE             ;Decrement buffer pointer
03FD: AF            0314              XOR  A
03FE: 12            0315              LD   (DE),A         ;Mark end of string with null
03FF: 18 9D         0316              JR   @Input
                    0317  ; Carriage return
0401: AF            0318  @Return:    XOR  A              ;Clear A to a null character (zero)
0402: 12            0319              LD   (DE),A         ;Store null to terminate string in buffer
0403: CD 20 04      0320              CALL OutputNewLine  ;Output new line character(s)
0406: 7B            0321              LD   A,E            ;Calculate number of characters
0407: 94            0322              SUB  A,H            ;  in input buffer
0408: E1            0323  @Exit:      POP  HL
0409: D1            0324              POP  DE
040A: C9            0325              RET
                    0326  
                    0327  
                    0328  ; **********************************************************************
                    0329  ; **  Output to console / output device, typically a terminal         **
                    0330  ; **********************************************************************
                    0331  
                    0332  
                    0333  ; Console output: Output character to console output device
                    0334  ;   On entry: A = Character to output
                    0335  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0336  ; This is the only place the actual new line codes (eg. CR/LF) are used
040B:               0337  OutputChar:
040B: F5            0338              PUSH AF
040C: FE 05         0339              CP   kNewLine       ;New line character?
040E: 20 09         0340              JR   NZ,@NotNL      ;No, so skip
0410: 3E 0D         0341              LD   A,kReturn      ;Yes, so output physical new line
0412: CD 1B FE      0342  @Wait1:     CALL JpConOut       ;  to console..
0415: 28 FB         0343              JR   Z,@Wait1
0417: 3E 0A         0344              LD   A,kLinefeed
0000:               0345  @NotNL:
0419: CD 1B FE      0346  @Wait2:     CALL JpConOut       ;Output character to console
041C: 28 FB         0347              JR   Z,@Wait2
041E: F1            0348  @Exit:      POP  AF
041F: C9            0349              RET
                    0350  
                    0351  
                    0352  ; Console output: Output new line character(s)
                    0353  ;   On entry: No parameters
                    0354  ;   On exit:  A BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0355  ; This is the only place the actual new line codes (eg. CR/LF) are used
0420:               0356  OutputNewLine:
0420: F5            0357              PUSH AF
0421: 3E 05         0358              LD   A,kNewLine     ;Get new line character
0423: CD 0B 04      0359              CALL OutputChar     ;Output carriage return character
0426: F1            0360              POP  AF
0427: C9            0361              RET
                    0362  
                    0363  
                    0364  ; Console output: Output a zero (null) terminated string
                    0365  ;   On entry: DE= Start address of string
                    0366  ;   On exit:  DE= Address after null
                    0367  ;             AF BC HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0368  ;             Interrupts enabled during this call
                    0369  ; Supports \n for new line
0428:               0370  OutputZString:
0428: F5            0371              PUSH AF
0429: 1A            0372  @Next:      LD   A,(DE)         ;Get character from string
042A: 13            0373              INC  DE             ;Point to next character
042B: B7            0374              OR   A              ;Null terminator?
042C: 28 05         0375              JR   Z,@Finished    ;Yes, so we've finished
042E: CD 0B 04      0376              CALL OutputChar     ;Output character
0431: 18 F6         0377              JR   @Next          ;Go process next character
0433: F1            0378  @Finished:  POP  AF
0434: C9            0379              RET
                    0380  
                    0381  
                    0382  ; **********************************************************************
                    0383  ; **  Private functions                                                **
                    0384  ; **********************************************************************
                    0385  
                    0386  
                    0387  #IFNDEF     IncludeUtilities
                    0388  ; This function is normal provided by the Utilities module, but if this 
                    0389  ; build does not include Utilities then assemble the function here.
                    0390  ; Utility: Convert character to upper case
                    0391  ;   On entry: A = Character in either case
                    0392  ;   On exit:  A = Character in upper case
                    0393  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0394  ConvertCharToUCase:
                    0395              CP   'a'            ;Character less than 'a'?
                    0396              RET  C              ;Yes, so finished
                    0397              CP   'z'+1          ;Character greater than 'z'?
                    0398              RET  NC             ;Yes, so finished
                    0399              SUB  'a'-'A'        ;Convert case
                    0400              RET
                    0401  #ENDIF
                    0402  
                    0403  
                    0404  ; **********************************************************************
                    0405  ; **  Private workspace (in RAM)                                      **
                    0406  ; **********************************************************************
                    0407  
                    0408              .DATA
                    0409  
FE62:               0410  iConInChar:
FE62: 00            0410  iConInChar: .DB  0x00           ;Console input character waiting
FE63: 00 00         0411  iConInBuf:  .DW  0x0000         ;Console input buffer start
                    0412

                    0415  ; **  End of Console support module                                   **
