                    0421  
                    0030  #INCLUDE    Monitor\Idle.asm    ;Idle events
                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  Idle events                               by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **********************************************************************
                    0004  
                    0005  ; This module handles the idle events.
                    0006  ;
                    0007  ; When the processor is not busy it repeatedly calls the idle handler.
                    0008  ; By default this is just a return instruction, but the system can be 
                    0009  ; configured to call IdlePoll instead. See API function 0x13.
                    0010  ;
                    0011  ; IdlePoll tries to emulate a timer interrupt and issues timer events
                    0012  ; at specified multiples 1 ms, 10 ms or 100 ms. Due to the lack of 
                    0013  ; hardware timer as standard in a typical small computer system (such 
                    0014  ; as RC2014) this function does not produce accurately timed events.
                    0015  ; Systems with a hardware timer, such as LiNC80, sync the idle events
                    0016  ; to the timer for better accuracy.
                    0017  ;
                    0018  ; Timer events are issued via the jump table to allow them to be
                    0019  ; directed anywhere required. Events are NOT issued from within an
                    0020  ; interrupt routine, but are simply subroutine calls made from within 
                    0021  ; system functions like Console Input. 
                    0022  ;
                    0023  ; Timer event handlers must preserve all registers except AF and HL,
                    0024  ; which are already preserved on the stack.
                    0025  ;
                    0026  ;
                    0027  ; Public functions provided
                    0028  ;   IdleConfig            Configure idle event handler
                    0029  ;   IdlePoll              Poll idle events
                    0030  ;   IdleTimer1            Set up Timer 1 event in multiples of 1 ms
                    0031  ;   IdleTimer2            Set up Timer 1 event in multiples of 10 ms
                    0032  ;   IdleTimer3            Set up Timer 1 event in multiples of 100 ms
                    0033  
                    0034  
                    0035  ; **********************************************************************
                    0036  ; **  Public functions                                                **
                    0037  ; **********************************************************************
                    0038  
                    0039              .CODE
                    0040  
                    0041  ; Idle: Configure idle events
                    0042  ;   On entry: A = Configuration:
                    0043  ;                 0 = Off (just execute RET instruction)
                    0044  ;                 1 = Software generated timer events
                    0045  ;                 2+ = Future expansion
                    0046  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
0435:               0047  IdleConfig:
0435: 11 43 04      0047  IdleConfig: LD   DE,@Return     ;Idle mode 0 routine
0438: B7            0048              OR   A              ;A=0?
0439: 28 03         0049              JR   Z,@IdleSet     ;Yes, so skip
043B: 11 44 04      0050              LD   DE,IdlePoll    ;Idle mode 1 routine
                    0051  ; Set up event handler by writing to jump table
043E: 3E 0C         0052  @IdleSet:   LD   A,kFnIdle      ;Jump table 0x0C = idle handler
0440: C3 66 01      0053              JP   ClaimJump      ;Write jump table entry A
0443: C9            0054  @Return:    RET                 ;Idle mode zero routine
                    0055  
                    0056  
                    0057  ; Idle: Poll idle events
                    0058  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0059  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0060  ; Registers must be preserved by this function
0444: F5            0061  IdlePoll:   PUSH AF
0445: CD 40 1E      0062              CALL Hardware_PollTimer
0448: 20 02         0063              JR   NZ,@Event1ms   ;Skip if 1ms event to process
044A: F1            0064              POP  AF             ;  otherwise exit now
044B: C9            0065              RET
                    0066  ; 1 ms tick (we arrive here approximately 1000 times each second)
044C: C5            0067  @Event1ms:  PUSH BC             ;Preserve BC
044D: D5            0068              PUSH DE             ;Preserve DE
044E: E5            0069              PUSH HL             ;Preserve HL
044F: 21 67 FE      0070              LD   HL,iIdleT1     ;Point to timer 1
0452: 35            0071              DEC  (HL)           ;Decrement timer 1
0453: 20 07         0072              JR   NZ,@IdleT1end  ;Skip if not zero
0455: 3A 68 FE      0073              LD   A,(iIdleP1)    ;Get period for timer 1
0458: 77            0074              LD   (HL),A         ;Reset timer 1
0459: CD 27 FE      0075              CALL JpTimer1       ;Call n x 1 ms timer event handler
045C:               0076  @IdleT1end:
045C: 21 65 FE      0076  @IdleT1end: LD   HL,iIdleMS     ;Point to millisecond counter
045F: 35            0077              DEC  (HL)           ;Decrement millisecond counter
0460: 20 24         0078              JR   NZ,@IdleExit   ;Not zero so exit
                    0079  ; 10 ms tick (we arrive here approximately 100 times each second)
0462: 36 0A         0080              LD   (HL),10        ;Reset millisecond counter to 10
0464: 21 69 FE      0081              LD   HL,iIdleT2     ;Point to timer 2
0467: 35            0082              DEC  (HL)           ;Decrement timer 2
0468: 20 07         0083              JR   NZ,@IdleT2end  ;Skip if not zero
046A: 3A 6A FE      0084              LD   A,(iIdleP2)    ;Get period for timer 2
046D: 77            0085              LD   (HL),A         ;Reset timer 2
046E: CD 2A FE      0086              CALL JpTimer2       ;Call n x 10 ms timer event handler
0471:               0087  @IdleT2end:
0471: 21 66 FE      0087  @IdleT2end: LD   HL,iIdleCS     ;Point to centisecond counter
0474: 35            0088              DEC  (HL)           ;Decrement centisecond counter
0475: 20 0F         0089              JR   NZ,@IdleExit   ;Not zero so exit
                    0090  ; 100 ms tick (we arrive here approximately 10 times each second)
0477: 36 0A         0091              LD   (HL),10        ;Reset centisecond counter to 10
0479: 21 6B FE      0092              LD   HL,iIdleT3     ;Point to timer 3
047C: 35            0093              DEC  (HL)           ;Decrement timer 3
047D: 20 07         0094              JR   NZ,@IdleExit   ;Skip if not zero
047F: 3A 6C FE      0095              LD   A,(iIdleP3)    ;Get period for timer 3
0482: 77            0096              LD   (HL),A         ;Reset timer 3
0483: CD 2D FE      0097              CALL JpTimer3       ;Call n x 100 ms timer event handler
0486: E1            0098  @IdleExit:  POP  HL             ;Restore HL
0487: D1            0099              POP  DE             ;Restore DE
0488: C1            0100              POP  BC             ;Restore BC
0489: F1            0101              POP  AF
048A: C9            0102              RET
                    0103  
                    0104  
                    0105  ; Idle: Set up timer 1 / 2 / 3
                    0106  ;   On entry: A = Time period in units of 1 ms / 10 ms / 100 ms
                    0107  ;             DE = Address of timer event handler
                    0108  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
048B:               0109  IdleTimer1:
048B: 0E 01         0109  IdleTimer1: LD   C,1            ;Timer 1 (resolution 1 ms) 
048D: 18 06         0110              JR   IdleSetUp
048F:               0111  IdleTimer2:
048F: 0E 02         0111  IdleTimer2: LD   C,2            ;Timer 2 (resolution 10 ms) 
0491: 18 02         0112              JR   IdleSetUp
0493:               0113  IdleTimer3:
0493: 0E 03         0113  IdleTimer3: LD   C,3            ;Timer 3 (resolution 100 ms) 
                    0114  ; Set up timer number C (1 to 3)
0495: 06 00         0115  IdleSetUp:  LD   B,0
0497: 21 65 FE      0116              LD   HL,iIdleT1-2   ;Start of timer and period data
049A: 09            0117              ADD  HL,BC          ;Calculate address of timer data
049B: 09            0118              ADD  HL,BC          ;  which take two bytes each
049C: 77            0119              LD   (HL),A         ;Store timer value
049D: 23            0120              INC  HL             ;Point to period value
049E: 77            0121              LD   (HL),A         ;Store period value
049F: 79            0122              LD   A,C            ;Get timer number
04A0: C6 0C         0123              ADD  A,kFnTimer1-1  ;Calculate jump table entry number
04A2: C3 66 01      0124              JP   ClaimJump      ;Write handler address to jump table
                    0125  
                    0126  
                    0127  ; **********************************************************************
                    0128  ; **  Private functions                                               **
                    0129  ; **********************************************************************
                    0130  
                    0131  
                    0132  ; **********************************************************************
                    0133  ; **  Private workspace (in RAM)                                      **
                    0134  ; **********************************************************************
                    0135  
                    0136              .DATA
                    0137  
                    0138  ; Cycle counting variables
FE65: 00            0139  iIdleMS:    .DB  0              ;Millisecond counter
FE66: 00            0140  iIdleCS:    .DB  0              ;Centisecond counter
                    0141  
                    0142  ; Periods and timers
                    0143  ; Each timer has a period value and a count down timer value
                    0144  ; WARNING: Do not change order as hard coded (above)
FE67: 00            0145  iIdleT1:    .DB  0              ;Timer 1 (resolution = 1 ms)
FE68: 00            0146  iIdleP1:    .DB  0              ;Period 1 (resolution = 1 ms)
FE69: 00            0147  iIdleT2:    .DB  0              ;Timer 2 (resolution = 10 ms)
FE6A: 00            0148  iIdleP2:    .DB  0              ;Period 2 (resolution = 10 ms)
FE6B: 00            0149  iIdleT3:    .DB  0              ;Timer 3 (resolution = 100 ms)
FE6C: 00            0150  iIdleP3:    .DB  0              ;Period 3 (resolution = 100 ms)
                    0151  
                    0152  
                    0153  ; **********************************************************************
                    0154  ; **  End of Idle events module                                       **
                    0155  ; **********************************************************************
                    0156  
                    0031  #INCLUDE    Monitor\Ports.asm   ;Port functions
                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  Port functions                            by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **********************************************************************
                    0004  
                    0005  ; This module provides functions to manipulate I/O ports.
                    0006  ;
                    0007  ; Public functions provided
                    0008  ;   PrtOInit              Initialise output port
                    0009  ;   PrtOWr                Write to output port
                    0010  ;   PrtORd                Read from output port
                    0011  ;   PrtOTst               Test output port bit
                    0012  ;   PrtOSet               Set output port bit
                    0013  ;   PrtOClr               Clear output port bit
                    0014  ;   PrtOInv               Invert output port bit
                    0015  ;   PrtIInit              Initialise input port
                    0016  ;   PrtIRd                Read from input port
                    0017  ;   PrtITst               Test input port bit
                    0018  
                    0019  
                    0020  ; **********************************************************************
                    0021  ; **  Public functions                                                **
                    0022  ; **********************************************************************
                    0023  
                    0024              .CODE
                    0025  
                    0026  ; Ports: Initialise output port
                    0027  ;   On entry: A = Output port address
                    0028  ;   On exit:  A = Output port data byte (which will be zero)
                    0029  ;             DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
04A5: 32 6E FE      0030  PrtOInit:   LD   (iPrtOutA),A   ;Store port address
04A8: AF            0031              XOR  A              ;Clear A (data)
04A9: 18 1C         0032              JR   PrtOWr         ;Write A to output port
                    0033  
                    0034  ; Ports: Read output port data
                    0035  ;   On entry: no parameters required
                    0036  ;   On exit:  A = Output port data
                    0037  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
04AB: 3A 6F FE      0038  PrtORd:     LD   A,(iPrtOutD)   ;Read output port
04AE: C9            0039              RET
                    0040  
                    0041  ; Ports: Test output port bit
                    0042  ;   On entry: A = Bit number 0 to 7
                    0043  ;   On exit:  A = 0 and Z flagged if bit low
                    0044  ;             A !=0 and NZ flagged if bit high
                    0045  ;             DE IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
04AF: CD E9 04      0046  PrtOTst:    CALL PortMask       ;Get bit mask for bit A
04B2: A0            0047              AND  B              ;Test against bit masked in B
04B3: C9            0048              RET                 ;Flag NZ if bit set
                    0049  
                    0050  ; Ports: Set output port bit
                    0051  ;   On entry: A = Bit number 0 to 7
                    0052  ;   On exit:  A = Output port data
                    0053  ;             DE IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
04B4: CD E9 04      0054  PrtOSet:    CALL PortMask       ;Get bit mask for bit A
04B7: B0            0055              OR   B              ;Set bit masked in B
04B8: 18 0D         0056              JR   PrtOWr         ;Write to port
                    0057  
                    0058  ; Ports: Clear output port bit
                    0059  ;   On entry: A = Bit number 0 to 7
                    0060  ;   On exit:  A = Output port data
                    0061  ;             DE IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
04BA: CD E9 04      0062  PrtOClr:    CALL PortMask       ;Get bit mask for bit A
04BD: 4F            0063              LD   C,A            ;Remember output port data
04BE: 78            0064              LD   A,B            ;Get bit mask
04BF: 2F            0065              CPL                 ;Complement mask (invert bits)
04C0: A1            0066              AND  C              ;Invert bit masked in A
04C1: 18 04         0067              JR   PrtOWr         ;Write to port
                    0068  
                    0069  ; Ports: Invert output port bit
                    0070  ;   On entry: A = Bit number 0 to 7
                    0071  ;   On exit:  A = Output port data
                    0072  ;             DE IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
04C3: CD E9 04      0073  PrtOInv:    CALL PortMask       ;Get bit mask for bit A
04C6: A8            0074              XOR  B              ;Invert bit masked in B
                    0075              ;JR   PrtOWr        ;Write to port
                    0076  
                    0077  ; Ports: Write to output port 
                    0078  ;   On entry: A = Output data byte
                    0079  ;   On exit:  A = Output port data
                    0080  ;             DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
04C7: 47            0081  PrtOWr:     LD   B,A            ;Remember port data
04C8: 3A 6E FE      0082              LD   A,(iPrtOutA)   ;Get port address 
04CB: 4F            0083              LD   C,A            ;Remember port address
04CC: 78            0084              LD   A,B            ;Get port data
04CD: 32 6F FE      0085              LD   (iPrtOutD),A   ;Store port data
04D0: ED 79         0086              OUT  (C),A          ;Write to port
04D2: C9            0087              RET
                    0088  
                    0089  
                    0090  ; Ports: Initialise input port
                    0091  ;   On entry: A = Input port address
                    0092  ;   On exit:  A = Input port data
                    0093  ;             DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
04D3: 32 6D FE      0094  PrtIInit:   LD   (iPrtInA),A    ;Store port address
04D6: AF            0095              XOR  A              ;Clear A (data)
                    0096              ;JR   PrtIRd        ;Write A to output port
                    0097  
                    0098  ; Ports: Read input port data
                    0099  ;   On entry: no parameters required
                    0100  ;   On exit:  A = Input port data
                    0101  ;             B DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
04D7: 3A 6D FE      0102  PrtIRd:     LD   A,(iPrtInA)    ;Get input port address 
04DA: 4F            0103              LD   C,A            ;Remember port address
04DB: ED 78         0104              IN   A,(C)          ;Read input port data
04DD: C9            0105              RET
                    0106  
                    0107  ; Ports: Test input port bit
                    0108  ;   On entry: A = Bit number 0 to 7
                    0109  ;   On exit:  A = 0 and Z flagged if bit low
                    0110  ;             A !=0 and NZ flagged if bit high
                    0111  ;             DE IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
04DE: CD E9 04      0112  PrtITst:    CALL PortMask       ;Get bit mask for bit A
04E1: 3A 6D FE      0113              LD   A,(iPrtInA)    ;Get input port address 
04E4: 4F            0114              LD   C,A            ;Remember port address
04E5: ED 78         0115              IN   A,(C)          ;Read input port data
04E7: A0            0116              AND  B              ;Test against bit masked in B
04E8: C9            0117              RET                 ;Flag NZ if bit set
                    0118  
                    0119  
                    0120  ; **********************************************************************
                    0121  ; **  Private functions                                               **
                    0122  ; **********************************************************************
                    0123  
                    0124  ; Get bit mask for bit A
                    0125  ;   On entry: A = Output port address
                    0126  ;   On exit:  B = Bit mask
                    0127  ;             A = Current output port value
                    0128  ;             DE IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
04E9: 21 F5 04      0129  PortMask:   LD   HL,@MaskTab    ;Start of bit mask table
04EC: 4F            0130              LD   C,A            ;Get bit number
04ED: 06 00         0131              LD   B,0            ;Clear B
04EF: 09            0132              ADD  HL,BC          ;Calculate location of bit mask in table
04F0: 46            0133              LD   B,(HL)         ;Get bit mask
04F1: 3A 6F FE      0134              LD   A,(iPrtOutD)   ;Get output data
04F4: C9            0135              RET
                    0136  ; Bit mask table: bit 0 mask, bit 1 mask, ...
04F5: 01 02 04 08   0137  @MaskTab:   .DB  1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128
04F9: 10 20 40 80   1782  
                    0138  
                    0139  
                    0140  ; **********************************************************************
                    0141  ; **  Private workspace (in RAM)                                      **
                    0142  ; **********************************************************************
                    0143  
                    0144              .DATA
                    0145  
FE6D: 00            0146  iPrtInA:    .DB  0              ;Input port address
FE6E: 00            0147  iPrtOutA:   .DB  0              ;Output port address
FE6F: 00            0148  iPrtOutD:   .DB  0              ;Output port data
                    0149  
                    0150  
                    0151  ; **********************************************************************
                    0152  ; **  End of Port functions module                                    **
                    0153  ; **********************************************************************
                    0154  
                    0032  
                    0033  ; Optional modules (see #DEFINEs above)
                    0034  
                    0035  ; Exporting functions
                    0036  #IFDEF      IncludeAPI
                    0037  #INCLUDE    Monitor\API.asm     ;Application Programming Interface (API)
                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  Application Programming Interface (API)   by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **********************************************************************
                    0004  
                    0005  ; This module provides a means of external software accessing some of
                    0006  ; the features of the Small Computer Monitor.
                    0007  ;
                    0008  ; API functions are accessed by:
                    0009  ; Loading C register with the function number
                    0010  ; Loading registers as required by the selected function
                    0011  ; Calling address 0x0030 (either with CALL or RST instruction)
                    0012  ;
                    0013  ; Public functions provided:
                    0014  ;   APIHandler            API main entry point
                    0015  
                    0016  
                    0017  ; **********************************************************************
                    0018  ; **  Public functions                                                **
                    0019  ; **********************************************************************
                    0020  
                    0021              .CODE
                    0022  
                    0023  ; API: Main entry point
                    0024  ;   On entry: C = Function number
                    0025  ;             A, DE = Parameters (as specified by function)
                    0026  ;   On exit:  AF,BC,DE,HL = Return values (as specified by function)
                    0027  ;             IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0028  ; This handler modifies: F, B, HL but preserves A, C, DE
                    0029  ; Other registers depend on API function called
04FD:               0030  APIHandler:
04FD: 21 0F 05      0030  APIHandler: LD   HL,APITable    ;Start of function address table
0500: 47            0031              LD   B,A            ;Preserve A
0501: 79            0032              LD   A,C            ;Get function number
0502: FE 2A         0033              CP   kAPILast+1     ;Supported function?
0504: D0            0034              RET  NC             ;No, so abort
0505: 78            0035              LD   A,B            ;Restore A
0506: 06 00         0036              LD   B,0
0508: 09            0037              ADD  HL,BC          ;Calculate table pointer..
0509: 09            0038              ADD  HL,BC
050A: 46            0039              LD   B,(HL)         ;Read function address from table..
050B: 23            0040              INC  HL
050C: 66            0041              LD   H,(HL)
050D: 68            0042              LD   L,B
050E: E9            0043              JP   (HL)           ;Jump to function address
                    0044  
                    0045  
                    0046  ; API: Function address table
                    0047  ; This table contains a list of addresses, one for each API function. 
                    0048  ; Each is the address of the subroutine for the relevant function.
050F: 6C 02         0049  APITable:   .DW  SysReset       ; 0x00 = System reset
0511: 25 03         0050              .DW  InputChar      ; 0x01 = Input character
0513: 0B 04         0051              .DW  OutputChar     ; 0x02 = Output character
0515: 3B 03         0052              .DW  InputStatus    ; 0x03 = Input status
0517: 7B 03         0053              .DW  InputLineTo    ; 0x04 = Input line
0519: 66 03         0054              .DW  InputLine      ; 0x05 = Input line default
051B: 28 04         0055              .DW  OutputZString  ; 0x06 = Output line
051D: 20 04         0056              .DW  OutputNewLine  ; 0x07 = Output new line
051F: 12 02         0057              .DW  GetVersion     ; 0x08 = Get version details
0521: 66 01         0058              .DW  ClaimJump      ; 0x09 = Claim jump table entry
0523: F0 01         0059              .DW  Delay          ; 0x0A = Delay in milliseconds
0525: 23 02         0060              .DW  OutputMessage  ; 0x0B = Output system message
0527: 9F 01         0061              .DW  ReadJump       ; 0x0C = Read jump table entry
0529: B3 01         0062              .DW  SelConDev      ; 0x0D = Select console in/out device
052B: C9 01         0063              .DW  SelConDevI     ; 0x0E = Select console input device
052D: B6 01         0064              .DW  SelConDevO     ; 0x0F = Select console output device
052F: DC 01         0065              .DW  DevInput       ; 0x10 = Input from specified device
0531: E5 01         0066              .DW  DevOutput      ; 0x11 = Output to specifiec device
0533: 24 FE         0067              .DW  JpIdle         ; 0x12 = Poll idle events
0535: 35 04         0068              .DW  IdleConfig     ; 0x13 = Configure idle events
0537: 8B 04         0069              .DW  IdleTimer1     ; 0x14 = Timer 1 control
0539: 8F 04         0070              .DW  IdleTimer2     ; 0x15 = Timer 2 control
053B: 93 04         0071              .DW  IdleTimer3     ; 0x16 = Timer 3 control
053D: A5 04         0072              .DW  PrtOInit       ; 0x17 = Output port initialise
053F: C7 04         0073              .DW  PrtOWr         ; 0x18 = Write to output port
0541: AB 04         0074              .DW  PrtORd         ; 0x19 = Read from output port
0543: AF 04         0075              .DW  PrtOTst        ; 0x1A = Test output port bit
0545: B4 04         0076              .DW  PrtOSet        ; 0x1B = Set output port bit
0547: BA 04         0077              .DW  PrtOClr        ; 0x1C = Clear output port bit
0549: C3 04         0078              .DW  PrtOInv        ; 0x1D = Invert output port bit
054B: D3 04         0079              .DW  PrtIInit       ; 0x1E = Input port initialise
054D: D7 04         0080              .DW  PrtIRd         ; 0x1F = Read from input port
054F: DE 04         0081              .DW  PrtITst        ; 0x20 = Test input port bit
0551: 61 02         0082              .DW  SetBaud        ; 0x21 = Set baud rate
0553: A2 0F         0083              .DW  CLIExecute     ; 0x22 = Execute command line
0555: 81 1B         0084              .DW  RomGetPtr      ; 0x23 = Get pointer to command line
0557: F4 0F         0085              .DW  CLISkipDelim   ; 0x24 = Skip delimiter
0559: EB 0F         0086              .DW  CLISkipNonDeli ; 0x25 = Skip non-delimiter
055B: FD 0F         0087              .DW  CLIGetHexParam ; 0x26 = Get hex parameter
055D: 03 02         0088              .DW  GetConDev      ; 0x27 = Get console in/out devices
055F: 08 02         0089              .DW  GetMemTop      ; 0x28 = Get top of free memory
0561: 0D 02         0090              .DW  SetMemTop      ; 0x29 = Set top of free memory
                    0091  
                    0092  #IFDEF      RAM_BANK_SUPPORT
                    0093              .DW  RdBankedRAM    ; 0x2A = Read banked RAM
                    0094              .DW  WrBankedRAM    ; 0x2B = Write banked RAM
                    0095  kAPILast:   .EQU 0x2B           ;Last API function number
                    0096  #ELSE
0029:               0097  kAPILast:   .EQU 0x29           ;Last API function number
                    0098  #ENDIF
                    0099  
                    0100  
                    0101  ; Dummy entry points for unsupported features
                    0102  #IFNDEF     IncludeRomFS
                    0103  RomGetPtr:
                    0104  #ENDIF
0563: C9            0105              RET
                    0106  
                    0107  
                    0108  ; **********************************************************************
                    0109  ; **  End of Application Programming Interface (API) module           **
                    0110  ; **********************************************************************
                    0111  
                    0112  
                    0113  
                    0038  #ENDIF
                    0039  #IFDEF      IncludeFDOS
                    0040  #INCLUDE    Monitor\FDOS.asm    ;Very limited CP/M style FDOS support
                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  Limited CP/M style FDOS support           by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **********************************************************************
                    0004  
                    0005  ; Currently this is just a proff of concept!
                    0006  
                    0007              .CODE
                    0008  
                    0009  ; FDOS: Main entry point
                    0010  ;   On entry: C = Function number
                    0011  ;             DE = Parameter (as specified by function)
                    0012  ;   On exit:  A = Single byte value (as specified by function)
                    0013  ;             HL = Double byte value (as specified by function)
                    0014  ;             IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0015  ; For compatibility functions return with A=L and B=H.
                    0016  ; Unsupported or out of range functions return A,B,H and L = zero.
0564: 21 81 05      0017  FDOS:       LD   HL,FDOSTable   ;Start of function address table
                    0018  ;           LD   B,A            ;Preserve A
0567: 79            0019              LD   A,C            ;Get function number
0568: FE 03         0020              CP   kFDOSLast+1    ;Supported function?
056A: 30 09         0021              JR   NC,FDOSExit    ;No, so go..
                    0022  ;           LD   A,B            ;Restore A
056C: 06 00         0023              LD   B,0
056E: 09            0024              ADD  HL,BC          ;Calculate table pointer..
056F: 09            0025              ADD  HL,BC
0570: 46            0026              LD   B,(HL)         ;Read function address from table..
0571: 23            0027              INC  HL
0572: 66            0028              LD   H,(HL)
0573: 68            0029              LD   L,B
0574: E9            0030              JP   (HL)           ;Jump to function address
                    0031  ; Exit for unsupported or out of range functions
0575: AF            0032  FDOSExit:   XOR  A              ;A=0
0576: 47            0033              LD   B,A            ;B=0
0577: 67            0034              LD   H,A            ;H=0
0578: 6F            0035              LD   L,A            ;L=0
0579: C9            0036              RET
                    0037  
                    0038  
                    0039  ; FDOS: Function 1: Console input
                    0040  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0041  ;   On exit:  A = ASCII character from input
                    0042  ;             IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0043  ; WARNING: Only partially implemented
057A: C3 25 03      0044  FDOSConIn:  JP   InputChar
                    0045  
                    0046  
                    0047  ; FDOS: Function 2: Console output
                    0048  ;   On entry: E = ASCII character to be output
                    0049  ;   On exit:  IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0050  ; WARNING: Only partially implemented
057D:               0051  FDOSConOut:
057D: 7B            0051  FDOSConOut: LD   A,E
057E: C3 0B 04      0052              JP   OutputChar
                    0053  
                    0054  
                    0055  ; FDOS: Function address table
                    0056  ; This table contains a list of addresses, one for each FDOS function. 
                    0057  ; Each is the address of the subroutine for the relevant function.
0581: 00 00         0058  FDOSTable:  .DW  Reset          ;  0 = System reset
0583: 7A 05         0059              .DW  FDOSConIn      ;  1 = Console input
0585: 7D 05         0060              .DW  FDOSConOut     ;  2 = Console output
                    0061  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ;  3 = Reader input
                    0062  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ;  4 = Punch output
                    0063  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ;  5 = List output
                    0064  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ;  6 = Direct console I/O
                    0065  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ;  7 = Get I/O byte
                    0066  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ;  8 = Set I/O byte
                    0067  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ;  9 = Read string
                    0068  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 10 = Read console buffer
                    0069  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 11 = Get console status
                    0070  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 12 = Return version number
                    0071  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 13 = Reset disk system
                    0072  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 14 = Select disk
                    0073  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 15 = Open file
                    0074  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 16 = Close file
                    0075  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 17 = Search for first
                    0076  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 18 = Search for next
                    0077  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 19 = Delete file
                    0078  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 20 = Read sequential
                    0079  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 21 = Write sequential
                    0080  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 22 = Make file
                    0081  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 23 = Rename file
                    0082  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 24 = Return login vector
                    0083  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 25 = Return current disk
                    0084  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 26 = Set DMA address
                    0085  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 27 = Get addr (alloc)
                    0086  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 28 = Write protect disk
                    0087  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 29 = Get R/O vector
                    0088  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 30 = Set file attributes
                    0089  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 31 = Get addr (disk params)
                    0090  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 32 = Set/get user code
                    0091  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 33 = Read random
                    0092  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 34 = Write random
                    0093  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 35 = Compute file size
                    0094  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 36 = Set random record
                    0095  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 37 = Reset drive
                    0096  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 38 = Unspecified
                    0097  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 39 = Unspecified
                    0098  ;           .DW  FDOSExit       ; 40 = Write random with zero fill
                    0099  
0002:               0100  kFDOSLast:  .EQU 2              ;Last FDOS function number
                    0101  
                    0102  
                    0103  ; **********************************************************************
                    0104  ; **  End of CP/M style FDOS support module                           **
                    0105  ; **********************************************************************
                    0106  
                    0041  #ENDIF
                    0042  
                    0043  ; Support functions
                    0044  #IFDEF      IncludeStrings
                    0045  #INCLUDE    Monitor\Strings.asm ;String support
                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  String support                            by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **********************************************************************
                    0004  
                    0005  ; This module provides a group of functions to handle strings. Strings
                    0006  ; are build in buffers using various functions, such as StrWrChar,
                    0007  ; which writes the specified character to the end of the currently 
                    0008  ; selected string. The string can then be 'printed' to the current 
                    0009  ; output device with the StrPrint function.
                    0010  ;
                    0011  ; Ensure StrInitialise or StrInitDefault is called before any other 
                    0012  ; string function as these finctions select and initialise a string 
                    0013  ; buffer.
                    0014  ;
                    0015  ; Strings are stored in buffers where the first byte of the buffer
                    0016  ; contains the string length. A value of zero therefore indicates
                    0017  ; an empty (or null) string. 
                    0018  ;
                    0019  ; Public functions provided
                    0020  ;   StrAppend             Append specified string to current buffer
                    0021  ;   StrAppendZ            Append specified zero terminated string
                    0022  ;   StrClear              Clear the current string buffer
                    0023  ;   StrConvUpper          Convert string to upper case
                    0024  ;   StrCopyToZ            Copy to zero (null) terminated string
                    0025  ;   StrGetLength          Get length of string in current string buffer
                    0026  ;   StrInitDefault        Initialise and select the default buffer
                    0027  ;   StrInitialise         Initialise default or supplied string buffer
                    0028  ;   StrPrint              Print string in current string buffer
                    0029  ;   StrPrintDE            Print string in string buffer at DE
                    0030  ;   StrWrAddress          Write address, colon, space to buffer
                    0031  ;   StrWrAsciiChar        Write ascii character to string buffer
                    0032  ;   StrWrBackspace        Write backspace to string buffer
                    0033  ;   StrWrBinaryByte       Write binary byte to string buffer
                    0034  ;   StrWrBinaryWord       TODO write binary byte
                    0035  ;   StrWrChar             Write character to string buffer
                    0036  ;   StrWrDecByte          TODO write decimal byte
                    0037  ;   StrWrDecWord          TODO write decimal word
                    0038  ;   StrWrHexByte          Write byte to buffer as 2 hex characters
                    0039  ;   StrWrHexNibble        Write nibble to buffer as 1 hex character
                    0040  ;   StrWrHexWord          Write word to buffer as 4 hex characters
                    0041  ;   StrWrNewLine          Write new line to string buffer
                    0042  ;   StrWrPadding          Write padding (spaces) to specified length
                    0043  ;   StrWrSpace            Write space character to string buffer
                    0044  ;   StrWrSpaces           Write specified spaces to string buffer
                    0045  ; Unless otherwise stated these functions have no return values and 
                    0046  ; preserved the registers: AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL'
                    0047  
                    0048  
                    0049  ; **********************************************************************
                    0050  ; **  Public functions                                                **
                    0051  ; **********************************************************************
                    0052  
                    0053              .CODE
                    0054  
                    0055  ; String: Append specified string to current string buffer
                    0056  ;   On entry: DE = Start of string to be appended
                    0057  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
0000:               0058  StrAppend:
0587: F5            0059              PUSH AF
0588: C5            0060              PUSH BC
0589: D5            0061              PUSH DE
058A: 1A            0062              LD   A,(DE)         ;Get length of specified string
058B: B7            0063              OR   A              ;Null string?
058C: 28 08         0064              JR   Z,@Done        ;Yes, so we're done
058E: 47            0065              LD   B,A            ;Store length of string
058F: 13            0066  @Next:      INC  DE             ;Point to next character to append
0590: 1A            0067              LD   A,(DE)         ;Get character from specified string
0591: CD 38 06      0068              CALL StrWrChar      ;Write character to current string
0594: 10 F9         0069              DJNZ @Next          ;Loop back if more character
0596: D1            0070  @Done:      POP  DE
0597: C1            0071              POP  BC
0598: F1            0072              POP  AF
0599: C9            0073              RET
                    0074  
                    0075  
                    0076  ; String: Append specified zero (null) terminated string
                    0077  ;   On entry: DE = Start of string to be appended
                    0078  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0079  ; Appends specified zero (null) terminated string to current string 
                    0080  ; buffer. The string does not have the usual length prefix but 
                    0081  ; instead is terminated with a zero (null).
059A:               0082  StrAppendZ:
059A: F5            0083              PUSH AF
059B: D5            0084              PUSH DE
059C: 1A            0085  @Next:      LD   A,(DE)         ;Get length of specified string
059D: B7            0086              OR   A              ;Null string?
059E: 28 06         0087              JR   Z,@Done        ;Yes, so we're done
05A0: CD 38 06      0088              CALL StrWrChar      ;Write character to current string
05A3: 13            0089              INC  DE             ;Point to next character
05A4: 18 F6         0090              JR   @Next          ;Loop back if more character
05A6: D1            0091  @Done:      POP  DE
05A7: F1            0092              POP  AF
05A8: C9            0093              RET
                    0094  
                    0095  
                    0096  ; String: Clear string in current string buffer
                    0097  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0098  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
0000:               0099  StrClear:
05A9: E5            0100              PUSH HL
05AA: 2A 70 FE      0101              LD   HL,(iStrStart) ;Get start of current string buffer
05AD: 36 00         0102              LD   (HL),0         ;Initialise string with length zero
05AF: E1            0103              POP  HL
05B0: C9            0104              RET
                    0105  
                    0106  
                    0107  ; String: Convert string to upper case
                    0108  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0109  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
05B1:               0110  StrConvUpper:
05B1: F5            0111              PUSH AF
05B2: E5            0112              PUSH HL
05B3: 2A 70 FE      0113              LD   HL,(iStrStart) ;Get start of current string buffer
05B6: 7E            0114              LD   A,(HL)         ;Get length of string
05B7: B7            0115              OR   A              ;Null string?
05B8: 28 0B         0116              JR   Z,@Done        ;Yes, so we're done here
05BA: C5            0117              PUSH BC
05BB: 47            0118              LD   B,A            ;Store length of string
05BC: 23            0119  @Loop:      INC  HL             ;Point to next character in string
05BD: 7E            0120              LD   A,(HL)         ;Get character from string
05BE: CD DE 06      0121              CALL ConvertCharToUCase
05C1: 77            0122              LD   (HL),A         ;Write upper case char to string
05C2: 10 F8         0123              DJNZ @Loop          ;Loop until end of string
05C4: C1            0124              POP  BC
05C5: E1            0125  @Done:      POP  HL
05C6: F1            0126              POP  AF
05C7: C9            0127              RET
                    0128  
                    0129  
                    0130  ; String: Copy to zero (null) terminated string
                    0131  ;   On entry: DE = Location to store Z string
                    0132  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
05C8:               0133  StrCopyToZ:
05C8: F5            0134              PUSH AF
05C9: D5            0135              PUSH DE
05CA: E5            0136              PUSH HL
05CB: 2A 70 FE      0137              LD   HL,(iStrStart) ;Get start of current string buffer
05CE: 7E            0138              LD   A,(HL)         ;Get length of string
05CF: B7            0139              OR   A              ;Null string?
05D0: 28 08         0140              JR   Z,@Done        ;Yes, so we're done here
05D2: 23            0141              INC  HL             ;Point to first character in string
05D3: C5            0142              PUSH BC
05D4: 4F            0143              LD   C,A            ;Store length of string
05D5: 06 00         0144              LD   B,0
05D7: ED B0         0145              LDIR                ;Copy string from HL to DE
05D9: C1            0146              POP  BC
05DA: AF            0147  @Done:      XOR  A
05DB: 12            0148              LD   (DE),A         ;Terminate string with null
05DC: E1            0149              POP  HL
05DD: D1            0150              POP  DE
05DE: F1            0151              POP  AF
05DF: C9            0152              RET
                    0153  
                    0154  
                    0155  ; String: Get length of string in current string buffer
                    0156  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0157  ;   On exit:  A = Length in characters
                    0158  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
05E0:               0159  StrGetLength:
05E0: E5            0160              PUSH HL
05E1: 2A 70 FE      0161              LD   HL,(iStrStart) ;Get start of current string buffer
05E4: 7E            0162              LD   A,(HL)         ;Get length of string in buffer
05E5: E1            0163              POP  HL
05E6: C9            0164              RET
                    0165  
                    0166  
                    0167  ; String: Initialise and select default string buffer
                    0168  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0169  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
05E7:               0170  StrInitDefault:
05E7: F5            0171              PUSH AF
05E8: AF            0172              XOR  A              ;Select default string buffer (0)
05E9: CD EE 05      0173              CALL StrInitialise  ;Select and initialise buffer
05EC: F1            0174              POP  AF
05ED: C9            0175              RET
                    0176  
                    0177  
                    0178  ; String: Initialise default or supplied string buffer
                    0179  ;   On entry: A = Size of buffer or zero to restore defaults
                    0180  ;             DE = Start address of string buffer
                    0181  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0182  ; Size includes the string's length byte so needs to be one byte
                    0183  ; longer than the largest string it can hold.
05EE:               0184  StrInitialise:
05EE: F5            0185              PUSH AF
05EF: D5            0186              PUSH DE
05F0: B7            0187              OR   A              ;Buffer length zero?
05F1: 20 05         0188              JR   NZ,@Init       ;No, so go use supplied values
05F3: 11 80 FD      0189              LD   DE,kStrBuffer  ;Get start of default buffer
05F6: 3E 80         0190              LD   A,kStrSize     ;Get size of default buffer
05F8: ED 53 70 FE   0191  @Init:      LD   (iStrStart),DE ;Store start of string buffer
05FC: 32 72 FE      0192              LD   (iStrSize),A   ;Store size of string buffer
05FF: AF            0193              XOR  A              ;Prepare for length zero
0600: 12            0194              LD   (DE),A         ;Initialise string with length zero
0601: D1            0195              POP  DE
0602: F1            0196              POP  AF
0603: C9            0197              RET
                    0198  
                    0199  
                    0200  ; String: Print string in current string buffer
                    0201  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0202  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0203  ; The string is printed to the current output device
                    0204  ; Supports \n for new line
0000:               0205  StrPrint:
0604: D5            0206              PUSH DE
0605: ED 5B 70 FE   0207              LD   DE,(iStrStart) ;Get start of current string buffer
0609: CD 0E 06      0208              CALL StrPrintDE     ;Print string at DE
060C: D1            0209  @Done:      POP  DE
060D: C9            0210              RET
                    0211  
                    0212  
                    0213  ; String: Print string in current string buffer
                    0214  ;   On entry: DE = Address of string
                    0215  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0216  ; The string is printed to the current output device
060E:               0217  StrPrintDE:
060E: F5            0218              PUSH AF
060F: C5            0219              PUSH BC
0610: D5            0220              PUSH DE
0611: 1A            0221              LD   A,(DE)         ;Get length of specified string
0612: B7            0222              OR   A              ;Null string?
0613: 28 08         0223              JR   Z,@Done        ;Yes, so we're done
0615: 47            0224              LD   B,A            ;Store length of string
0616: 13            0225  @Next:      INC  DE             ;Point to next character to append
0617: 1A            0226              LD   A,(DE)         ;Get character from specified string
0618: CD 0B 04      0227              CALL OutputChar     ;Output character to output device
061B: 10 F9         0228              DJNZ @Next          ;Loop back if more character
061D: D1            0229  @Done:      POP  DE
061E: C1            0230              POP  BC
061F: F1            0231              POP  AF
0620: C9            0232              RET
                    0233  
                    0234  
                    0235  ; String: Write address, colon, space to string buffer
                    0236  ;   On entry: DE = Address
                    0237  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0238  ; Example output: "1234: "
0621:               0239  StrWrAddress:
0621: F5            0240              PUSH AF
0622: CD 6C 06      0241              CALL StrWrHexWord   ;Write start address of this line
0625: 3E 3A         0242              LD   A,':'
0627: CD 38 06      0243              CALL StrWrChar      ;Write colon
062A: CD 92 06      0244              CALL StrWrSpace     ;Write space
062D: F1            0245              POP  AF
062E: C9            0246              RET
                    0247  
                    0248  
                    0249  ; String: Write ascii character to string buffer
                    0250  ;   On entry: A = ASCII character
                    0251  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0252  ; If the character is not printable then a dot is written instead
062F:               0253  StrWrAsciiChar:
062F: F5            0254              PUSH AF
0630: CD A3 06      0255              CALL ConvertByteToAscii
0633: CD 38 06      0256              CALL StrWrChar      ;Write character or a dot
0636: F1            0257              POP  AF
0637: C9            0258              RET
                    0259  
                    0260  
                    0261  #IFDEF      kIncludeUnusedCode
                    0262  ; String: Write backspace to string buffer
                    0263  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0264  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0265  ; Writeing backspace deletes the last character in the buffer
                    0266  StrWrBackspace:
                    0267              PUSH AF
                    0268              PUSH HL
                    0269              LD   HL,(iStrStart) ;Pointer to start of string buffer
                    0270              LD   A,(HL)         ;Get length of string in buffer
                    0271              OR   A              ;Null terminator?
                    0272              JR   Z,@Skip        ;Yes, so skip as null string
                    0273              DEC  HL             ;Decrement string length
                    0274  @Skip:      POP  HL
                    0275              POP  AF
                    0276              RET
                    0277  #ENDIF
                    0278  
                    0279  
                    0280  #IFDEF      kIncludeUnusedCode
                    0281  ; String: Write binary byte
                    0282  ;   On entry: A = Binary byte
                    0283  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0284  StrWrBinaryByte:
                    0285              PUSH AF
                    0286              PUSH BC
                    0287              LD   B,8            ;Set for 8-bits
                    0288              LD   C,A            ;Store binary byte
                    0289  @NextBit:   LD   A,'1'          ;Default to '1'
                    0290              RL   C              ;Rotate data byte
                    0291              JR   C,@One         ; result in Carry
                    0292              LD   A,'0'          ;Select '0'
                    0293  @One:       CALL StrWrChar      ;Output '1' or '0'
                    0294              DJNZ @NextBit       ;Loop until done
                    0295              POP  BC
                    0296              POP  AF
                    0297              RET
                    0298  #ENDIF
                    0299  
                    0300  
                    0301  ; String: Write character
                    0302  ;   On entry: A = Character to write to string buffer
                    0303  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0304  ; The specified character is writted to the string buffer and a null
                    0305  ; terminator added.
0000:               0306  StrWrChar:
0638: F5            0307              PUSH AF
0639: D5            0308              PUSH DE
063A: E5            0309              PUSH HL
063B: 5F            0310              LD   E,A            ;Store character to write
063C: 2A 70 FE      0311              LD   HL,(iStrStart) ;Start of current string buffer
063F: 7E            0312              LD   A,(HL)         ;Get length of string in buffer
                    0313  ; TODO >>>>> Trap strings too long for the buffer
0640: 34            0314              INC  (HL)           ;Increment string length
0641: 3C            0315              INC  A              ;Inc to skip length byte
0642: 85            0316              ADD  A,L            ;Add A to start of buffer...
0643: 6F            0317              LD   L,A            ;  to get address for next character
0644: 30 01         0318              JR   NC,@Store
0646: 24            0319              INC  H
0647: 73            0320  @Store:     LD   (HL),E         ;Store character in buffer
0648: E1            0321              POP  HL
0649: D1            0322              POP  DE
064A: F1            0323              POP  AF
064B: C9            0324              RET
                    0325

064C:               0336  StrWrHexByte:

0651: 7A            0340              LD   A,D
0652: CD 5C 06      0341              CALL StrWrHexNibble
0655: 7B            0342              LD   A,E
0656: CD 5C 06      0343              CALL StrWrHexNibble
0659: D1            0344              POP  DE
065A: F1            0345              POP  AF
065B: C9            0346              RET
                    0347

                    0349  ; String: Write hex nibble to string buffer
                    0350  ;   On entry: A = Hex nibble
                    0351  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
065C:               0352  StrWrHexNibble:
065C: F5            0353              PUSH AF
065D: E6 0F         0354              AND  0x0F           ;Mask off nibble
065F: FE 0A         0355              CP   0x0A           ;Nibble > 10 ?
0661: 38 02         0356              JR   C,@Skip        ;No, so skip
0663: C6 07         0357              ADD  A,7            ;Yes, so add 7
0665: C6 30         0358  @Skip:      ADD  A,0x30         ;Add ASCII '0'
0667: CD 38 06      0359              CALL StrWrChar      ;Write character
066A: F1            0360              POP  AF
066B: C9            0361              RET
                    0362  
                    0363

066C:               0367  StrWrHexWord:

066E: CD 4C 06      0370              CALL StrWrHexByte   ;Write as two hex digits
0671: 7B            0371              LD   A,E            ;Get lo byte
0672: CD 4C 06      0372              CALL StrWrHexByte   ;Write as two hex digits
0675: F1            0373              POP  AF
0676: C9            0374              RET
                    0375  
                    0376  
                    0377  ; String: Write new line to string buffer
                    0378  ;   On entry: No parameters
                    0379  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
0677:               0380  StrWrNewLine:
0677: F5            0381              PUSH AF
0678: 3E 05         0382              LD   A,kNewLine     ;Get new line character
067A: CD 38 06      0383              CALL StrWrChar      ;Write character to string
067D: F1            0384              POP  AF
067E: C9            0385              RET
                    0386  
                    0387  
                    0388  ; String:  Write padding (spaces) to specified length
                    0389  ;   On entry: A = Required length of string
                    0390  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
067F:               0391  StrWrPadding:
067F: F5            0392              PUSH AF
0680: C5            0393              PUSH BC
0681: E5            0394              PUSH HL
0682: 47            0395              LD   B,A
0683: 2A 70 FE      0396              LD   HL,(iStrStart) ;Get start of current string buffer
0686: 96            0397              SUB  (HL)           ;Compare required length to current
0687: 38 05         0398              JR   C,@End         ;End now if already too long
0689: 28 03         0399              JR   Z,@End         ;End now if already required length
068B: CD 9A 06      0400              CALL StrWrSpaces    ;Write required number of spaces
068E: E1            0401  @End:       POP  HL

0690: F1            0403              POP  AF

                    0405

0692:               0410  StrWrSpace:

0695: CD 38 06      0413              CALL StrWrChar      ;Write space character
0698: F1            0414              POP  AF

                    0416  
                    0417  
                    0418  ; String: Write spaces to string buffer
                    0419  ;   On entry: A = Number of spaces to write
                    0420  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
069A:               0421  StrWrSpaces:
069A: F5            0422              PUSH AF
069B: CD 92 06      0423  @Loop:      CALL StrWrSpace     ;Print one space character
069E: 3D            0424              DEC  A              ;Written all required spaces?
069F: 20 FA         0425              JR   NZ,@Loop       ;No, so go write another
06A1: F1            0426              POP  AF
06A2: C9            0427              RET
