                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  Command Line Interpreter                  by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **********************************************************************
                    0004  
                    0005  ; This module provides the monitor's Command Line Interpreter (CLI)
                    0006  ; including the commands themselves.
                    0007  ;
                    0008  ; Public functions provided:
                    0009  ;   CLILoop               Command Line Interpreter main loop
                    0010  ;   CLIExecute            Offer string to command line interpreter
                    0011  ;   CLISkipNonDeli        Skip non-deliminaters in input line
                    0012  ;   CLISkipDelim          Skip deliminaters in input line
                    0013  ;   CLIGetHexParam        Get hex word/byte from input line
                    0014  
                    0015  
                    0016  ; **********************************************************************
                    0017  ; **  Constants                                                       **
                    0018  ; **********************************************************************
                    0019  
                    0020  ; Prompt character
002A:               0021  kPrompt:    .EQU '*'            ;Prompt character
                    0022  
                    0023  ; Error reporting flags
FEA1:               0024  kReportBad:
                    0024  kReportBad: .EQU 1              ;Report bad hex parameter
FEA1:               0025  kReportMis:
                    0025  kReportMis: .EQU 2              ;Report missing parameter
FEA1:               0026  kReportAny:
                    0026  kReportAny: .EQU 3              ;Report any error
                    0027  
                    0028  
                    0029  ; **********************************************************************
                    0030  ; **  Public functions                                                **
                    0031  ; **********************************************************************
                    0032  
                    0033              .CODE
                    0034  
                    0035  ; Command Line Interpreter: Main loop
0F72: CD E7 05      0036  CLILoop:    CALL StrInitDefault ;Initialise default string buffer
0F75: 3E 2A         0037              LD   A,kPrompt      ;Get prompt character
0F77: CD 0B 04      0038              CALL OutputChar     ;Output a prompt character
                    0039  #IFDEF      IncludeMiniTerm
0F7A: AF            0040              XOR  A              ;Flag command as
0F7B: 32 A3 FE      0041              LD   (iMiniTerm),A  ;  not from mini terminal
                    0042  #ENDIF
                    0043  #IFDEF      IncludeHexLoader
                    0044  ; Trap colon character and input intel hex record(s)
0F7E: CD 12 03      0045  @Preview:   CALL InputPreview   ;Preview first character in line
0F81: FE 3A         0046              CP   kColon         ;Start of Intel Hex record/line?
0F83: 20 08         0047              JR   NZ,@NotHex     ;No, so skip..
0F85: CD 84 1A      0048              CALL HexLoad        ;Load Intel Hex record
0F88: CD F3 02      0049              CALL InputInsert    ;Insert returned character as waiting
0F8B: 18 F1         0050              JR   @Preview       ;Go preview start of next line..
0000:               0051  @NotHex:
                    0052  #IFDEF      IncludeMiniTerm
                    0053  ; WARNING this should be outside IncludeHexLoader
0F8D: FE 2D         0054              CP   '-'
0F8F: 20 09         0055              JR   NZ,@NotMini
0F91: 32 A3 FE      0056              LD   (iMiniTerm),A
0F94: AF            0057              XOR   A
0F95: CD F3 02      0058              CALL InputInsert    ;Insert returned character as waiting
0F98: 18 E4         0059              JR   @Preview       ;Go preview start of next line..
0000:               0060  @NotMini:
                    0061  #ENDIF
                    0062  #ENDIF
                    0063  ; Input line and execute it
0F9A: CD 66 03      0064              CALL InputLine      ;Input a text line
0F9D: CD A2 0F      0065              CALL CLIExecute     ;Execute command line
0FA0: 18 D0         0066              JR   CLILoop        ;Go get next line
                    0067  
                    0068  
                    0069  ; Offer string to command line interpreter
                    0070  ;  On entry:  DE = Start of command string terminated in Null
                    0071  ;  On exit:   If command handled (blank line or executed command):
                    0072  ;               A = 0x00 and Z flagged
                    0073  ;             If command not handled:
                    0074  ;               A = 0xFF and NZ flagged
                    0075  ;             No register contents preserved
                    0076  ; If found the command is executed before return. HL points to
                    0077  ; start of parameter string when command code entered.
0FA2:               0078  CLIExecute:
                    0079              ;EI
0FA2: 1A            0080  @Leading:   LD   A,(DE)         ;Skip leading spaces...
0FA3: FE 20         0081              CP   kSpace         ;Space character?
0FA5: 20 03         0082              JR   NZ,@NotSpace   ;No, so continue to next char
0FA7: 13            0083              INC  DE             ;Increment string pointer
0FA8: 18 F8         0084              JR   @Leading       ;Go consider next character
0FAA: FE 00         0085  @NotSpace:  CP   kNull          ;Blank line?
0FAC: C8            0086              RET  Z              ;Yes, so exit with A=0x00 and Z flagged
                    0087  ; Optionally include scripting
                    0088  #IFDEF      IncludeScripting
                    0089  ; Scripting lines start with a numeric character
                    0090              CALL IsCharNumeric  ;Numeric character
                    0091              JR   NC,@NotNum     ;No, so skip..
                    0092              CALL CLIGetHexParam
                    0093              CALL ScrEdit        ;Send line to scripting editor
                    0094              JR   @Return
                    0095  @NotNum:
                    0096  #ENDIF
                    0097  ; Offer command string to system command line interpreter
                    0098  ; DE = Start of command string
0FAD: 21 28 13      0099              LD   HL,CmdNameList
0FB0: 01 5D 13      0100              LD   BC,CmdAddressList
0FB3: CD 7D 07      0101              CALL SearchStringList
                    0102  ;           JR   Z,@Error       ;Not found so return error
0FB6: 28 05         0103              JR   Z,@NotFound    ;Not found so try single letter
                    0104  ; Execute command
0FB8: 01 E0 0F      0105              LD   BC,@Return     ;Get return address
0FBB: C5            0106              PUSH BC             ;Push return address onto stack
0FBC: E9            0107              JP   (HL)           ;Execute address from table
0000:               0108  @NotFound
                    0109  ; Try looking for command in ROM filing system
                    0110  #IFDEF      IncludeRomFS
0FBD: D5            0111              PUSH DE
0FBE: CD 86 1B      0112              CALL RomExFile      ;Execute ROM file named at DE
0FC1: D1            0113              POP  DE
0FC2: C8            0114              RET  Z              ;Exit if command executed
                    0115  #ENDIF
                    0116  ; Try inserting space after first character in command string
                    0117  ; to allow single letter commands to be entered without space
                    0118  ; DE = Start of command string
0FC3: 62            0119              LD   H,D            ;Get start of command string..
0FC4: 6B            0120              LD   L,E
0FC5: 23            0121              INC  HL             ;Skip first character in string
0FC6: 0E 20         0122              LD   C,kSpace       ;Insert space
0FC8: 7E            0123  @Shift:     LD   A,(HL)         ;Get current character
0FC9: 71            0124              LD   (HL),C         ;Write new character
0FCA: 4F            0125              LD   C,A            ;Store previous character
0FCB: 7E            0126              LD   A,(HL)         ;Get character just written
0FCC: 23            0127              INC  HL             ;Point to next character
0FCD: B7            0128              OR   A              ;Null terminator?
0FCE: 20 F8         0129              JR   NZ,@Shift      ;No, so repeat
0FD0: 21 28 13      0130              LD   HL,CmdNameList
0FD3: 01 5D 13      0131              LD   BC,CmdAddressList
0FD6: CD 7D 07      0132              CALL SearchStringList
0FD9: 28 07         0133              JR   Z,@Error       ;Not found so return error
                    0134  ; Execute single letter command
0FDB: 01 E0 0F      0135              LD   BC,@Return     ;Get return address
0FDE: C5            0136              PUSH BC             ;Push return address onto stack
0FDF: E9            0137              JP   (HL)           ;Execute address from table
                    0138  ; Return here after executing the command
0FE0: AF            0139  @Return:    XOR  A              ;Return A = 0x00 and Z flagged
0FE1: C9            0140              RET
                    0141  ; Bad command
0FE2: 3E 20         0142  @Error:     LD   A,kMsgBadCmd   ;Message = Bad command
0FE4: CD 23 02      0143              CALL OutputMessage  ;Output message
0FE7: 3E FF         0144              LD   A,0xFF         ;No match found, so return:
0FE9: B7            0145              OR   A              ;  A = 0xFF and NZ flagged
0FEA: C9            0146              RET
                    0147  
                    0148  
                    0149  ; Skip non-deliminaters in input line
                    0150  ;   On entry: DE = Start address in input line
                    0151  ;   On exit:  A = Character at returned address
                    0152  ;             DE = Address of first non-delimiter character
                    0153  ;             BC HL IX IY preserved
                    0154  ; Delimiters are spaces or tabs (actually any control character other than null)
                    0155  ; Input line must be null terminated
                    0156  ; The return address can be that of the null terminator
0FEB:               0157  CLISkipNonDeli:
0FEB: 1A            0158  @Loop:      LD   A,(DE)         ;Get character from input line
0FEC: B7            0159              OR   A              ;End of line (null)?
0FED: C8            0160              RET  Z              ;Yes, so exit
0FEE: FE 21         0161              CP   kSpace+1       ;Character > space?
0FF0: D8            0162              RET   C             ;No, so exit
0FF1: 13            0163              INC  DE             ;No, so non-delimiter
0FF2: 18 F7         0164              JR   @Loop          ;  and go try next character
                    0165  
                    0166  
                    0167  ; Skip deliminaters in input line
                    0168  ;   On entry: DE = Start address in input line
                    0169  ;   On exit:  If end of line (null) found:
                    0170  ;               A = Zero and Z flagged
                    0171  ;               DE = Address of null character
                    0172  ;             If non-delimiter character found:
                    0173  ;               A = Character found and NZ flagged
                    0174  ;               DE = Address of first non-delimiter character
                    0175  ;             BC HL IX IY preserved
                    0176  ; Delimiters are spaces or tabs (actually any control character other than null)
                    0177  ; Input line must be null terminated
                    0178  ; The return address can be that of the null terminator
0FF4:               0179  CLISkipDelim:
0FF4: 1A            0180  @Loop:      LD   A,(DE)         ;Get character from input line
0FF5: B7            0181              OR   A              ;End of line (null)?
0FF6: C8            0182              RET  Z              ;Yes, so exit
0FF7: FE 21         0183              CP   kSpace+1       ;Character > space?
0FF9: D0            0184              RET  NC             ;Yes, so exit
0FFA: 13            0185              INC  DE             ;No, so skip delimiter
0FFB: 18 F7         0186              JR   @Loop          ;  and go try next character
                    0187  
                    0188  
                    0189  ; Get hex word/byte from input line
                    0190  ;   On entry: A = Error reporting flags kReportXXX
                    0191  ;             DE = Location of parameter in input line
                    0192  ;             HL = Default value returned if no parameter found
                    0193  ;   On exit:  If a valid hex parameter:
                    0194  ;               A = 0x00 and Z flagged and C flagged
                    0195  ;               DE = Location after parameter in input line
                    0196  ;               HL = Hex byte/word from input line
                    0197  ;               Carry flagged if no parameter found
                    0198  ;             If no parameter if found:
                    0199  ;               A = 0x00 and Z flagged and NC flagged
                    0200  ;               DE = Location after parameter in input line
                    0201  ;               HL = Hex byte/word from input line
                    0202  ;               Carry flagged if no parameter found
                    0203  ;               An error message is may be shown
                    0204  ;             If an invalid hex parameter is found
                    0205  ;               A = 0xFF and NZ flagged and NC flagged
                    0206  ;               DE = Location after any valid characters
                    0207  ;               HL = Unspecified
                    0208  ;               An error message is may be shown
                    0209  ;             BC IX IY preserved
                    0210  ; If a non-hex character is encountered before a delimiter or null an error
                    0211  ; is reported.
                    0212  ; The hex conversion duplicates that in ConvertStringToNumber but we
                    0213  ; can't use that function here for hex as it requires leading numeric
                    0214  ; character ('0' to '9') for hex numbers:
                    0215  ; ... this is to stop the assembler getting confused between
                    0216  ; ... register names and constants which could be fixed by
                    0217  ; ... re-ordering the (dis)assebmer's instruction table
0FFD:               0218  CLIGetHexParam:
0FFD: C5            0219              PUSH BC             ;Preserve BC
0FFE: 4F            0220              LD   C,A            ;Store error report flags
0FFF: CD F4 0F      0221              CALL CLISkipDelim   ;Skip deliminater
1002: B7            0222              OR   A              ;End of line (null)? This clears carry flag
1003: 28 35         0223              JR   Z,@None        ;Yes, so exit as no parameter found
1005: CD 61 07      0224              CALL IsCharHex      ;Is first character hexadecimal ?
1008: 30 25         0225              JR   NC,@Other      ;No, so invalid hex character
100A: 21 00 00      0226              LD   HL,0           ;Clear result (from supplied default)
100D: 1A            0227  @Loop:      LD   A,(DE)         ;Get character from input line
                    0228  ;           OR   A              ;End of line (null)? This clears carry flag
                    0229  ;           JR   Z,@None        ;Yes, so exit as ????with A = 0x00 and Z flagged
100E: FE 21         0230              CP   kSpace+1       ;Character > space?
1010: 38 24         0231              JR   C,@Found       ;No, so go end with delimiter
1012: CD DE 06      0232              CALL ConvertCharToUCase
1015: CD BD 06      0233              CALL ConvertCharToNumber
1018: 20 29         0234              JR   NZ,@Bad        ;Bad hex parameter
101A: CB 25         0235              SLA  L              ;Rotate new nibble
101C: CB 14         0236              RL   H              ;  into 16 bit result...
101E: CB 25         0237              SLA  L
1020: CB 14         0238              RL   H
1022: CB 25         0239              SLA  L
1024: CB 14         0240              RL   H
1026: CB 25         0241              SLA  L
1028: CB 14         0242              RL   H
102A: B5            0243              OR   L
102B: 6F            0244              LD   L,A
102C: 13            0245              INC  DE
102D: 18 DE         0246              JR   @Loop
                    0247  ; Try all other format not starting with hex character
102F: CD E7 06      0248  @Other:     CALL ConvertStringToNumber
1032: 28 02         0249              JR   Z,@Found
1034: 18 0D         0250              JR   @Bad
                    0251  ; Valid hex parameter found
1036: C1            0252  @Found:     POP  BC             ;Retore BC
1037: AF            0253              XOR  A              ;Return with A = 0x00
1038: 37            0254              SCF                 ;  and Z flagged (= no bad hex)
1039: C9            0255              RET                 ;  and C flagged (= no error)
                    0256  ; Parameter error: no parameter found
103A: 79            0257  @None:      LD   A,C            ;Get error report flags
103B: E6 02         0258              AND  kReportMis     ;Missing parameter report flag?
103D: C4 4E 10      0259              CALL NZ,@Error      ;Yes, so report error
1040: C1            0260              POP  BC             ;Retore BC
1041: AF            0261              XOR  A              ;Return with A = 0x00
                    0262              ;  and Z flagged (= no bad hex)
1042: C9            0263              RET                 ;  and NC flagged (= error)
                    0264  ; Parameter error: non-hex digit found
1043: 79            0265  @Bad:       LD   A,C            ;Get error report flags
1044: E6 01         0266              AND  kReportBad     ;Bad parameter report flag?
1046: C4 4E 10      0267              CALL NZ,@Error      ;Yes, so report error
1049: C1            0268              POP  BC             ;Retore BC
104A: 3E FF         0269              LD   A,0xFF         ;;Return with A = 0xFF 
104C: B7            0270              OR   A              ;  and NZ flagged (= bad hex)
104D: C9            0271              RET                 ;  and NC flagged (= error)
                    0272  ; Report error
0000:               0273  @Error:     ;PUSH DE
104E: CD 53 10      0274              CALL CLIBadParam
                    0275              ;POP  DE
1051: 13            0276              INC  DE
1052: C9            0277              RET
                    0278

11C3: 18 E0         0542              JR   @NextParam
                    0543  ; New memory data is a string
11C5: 13            0544  @String:    INC  DE             ;Increment pointer to input line
11C6: 1A            0545              LD   A,(DE)         ;Get character from input line
11C7: B7            0546              OR   A              ;End of string?
11C8: 28 A5         0547              JR   Z,@NextLine    ;Yes, next line
11CA: 77            0548              LD   (HL),A         ;Store new byte at current location
11CB: 23            0549              INC  HL             ;Increment memory location
11CC: 18 F7         0550              JR   @String
                    0551  ; Back one location
11CE: 2B            0552  @Back:      DEC  HL             ;Back one memory location
11CF: 18 9E         0553              JR   @NextLine
                    0554  
                    0555  
                    0556  ; Command: Flag: F [<name>]
11D1: CD F4 0F      0557  CmdFlag:    CALL CLISkipDelim   ;Skip deliminater
11D4: 28 14         0558              JR   Z,@Results     ;No flag specified so display flags
                    0559  ; Flag name specified so set or clear flag
11D6: 21 E1 13      0560              LD   HL,FlagNames
11D9: 01 FB 13      0561              LD   BC,FlagLogic
11DC: CD 7D 07      0562              CALL SearchStringList
11DF: CA 53 10      0563              JP   Z,CLIBadParam  ;Report error if flag name not found
11E2: 3A 75 FE      0564              LD   A,(iAF)        ;Get flags register
11E5: A5            0565              AND  L              ;AND with value from list
11E6: B4            0566              OR   H              ;OR with value form list
11E7: 32 75 FE      0567              LD   (iAF),A        ;Get flags register
11EA: CD 11 08      0568  @Results:   CALL WrRegister1    ;Write registers line 1 to buffer
11ED: C3 04 06      0569              JP   StrPrint       ;Output registers (inc flags)
                    0570  
                    0571  
                    0572  ; Command: Fill <start> <end> <data>
11F0: 3E 03         0573  CmdFill:    LD   A,kReportAny   ;Report any error
11F2: CD FD 0F      0574              CALL CLIGetHexParam ;Start address
11F5: D0            0575              RET  NC             ;Abort if any error
11F6: 22 A1 FE      0576              LD   (iParam1),HL   ;Store start address
11F9: 3E 03         0577              LD   A,kReportAny   ;Report any error
11FB: CD FD 0F      0578              CALL CLIGetHexParam ;End address
11FE: D0            0579              RET  NC             ;Abort if any error
11FF: 44            0580              LD   B,H            ;Store last address
1200: 4D            0581              LD   C,L
1201: 3E 03         0582              LD   A,kReportAny   ;Report any error
1203: CD FD 0F      0583              CALL CLIGetHexParam ;Data byte
1206: D0            0584              RET  NC             ;Abort if any error
1207: 5D            0585              LD   E,L            ;Store data byte
1208: 2A A1 FE      0586              LD   HL,(iParam1)   ;Get start address
120B: 2B            0587              DEC  HL
120C: 23            0588  @Loop:      INC  HL             ;Point to next address to fill
120D: 73            0589              LD   (HL),E         ;Write date byte to memory
120E: 7D            0590              LD   A,L            ;Lo byte of current address
120F: B9            0591              CP   C              ;Same as last address to fill?
1210: 20 FA         0592              JR   NZ,@Loop       ;No, so continue
1212: 7C            0593              LD   A,H            ;Hi byte of current address
1213: B8            0594              CP   B              ;Same as last address to fill?
1214: 20 F6         0595              JR   NZ,@Loop       ;No, so continue
1216: C9            0596              RET
                    0597  
                    0598  
                    0599  ; Command: Go: G [<start address>]
1217: 3E 01         0600  CmdGo:      LD   A,kReportBad   ;Only report bad hex parameter
1219: CD FD 0F      0601              CALL CLIGetHexParam
121C: C0            0602              RET  NZ             ;Abort if bad hex in parameter
121D: 30 10         0603              JR   NC,@Restore    ;Skip if no address parameter
121F: 22 73 FE      0604              LD   (iPC),HL       ;Store supplied address as PC
1222: 21 C0 FC      0605              LD   HL,kSPUsr      ;Get top of user stack
1225: 11 03 00      0606              LD   DE,WarmStart   ;Get return address
1228: 2B            0607              DEC  HL             ;  and 'push' it onto
1229: 72            0608              LD   (HL),D         ;  user stack
122A: 2B            0609              DEC  HL
122B: 73            0610              LD   (HL),E
122C: 22 81 FE      0611              LD   (iSP),HL       ;Store as user stack pointer
                    0612  #IFDEF      IncludeBreakpoint
122F: C3 D2 0E      0613  @Restore:   JP   BPGo           ;Execute using stored register values
                    0614  #ELSE
                    0615  @Restore:   LD   HL,(iPC)       ;Get execution address
                    0616              JP   (HL)           ;Run from this address
                    0617  #ENDIF
                    0618  
                    0619  
                    0620  ; Command: Help: HELP or ?
                    0621  ;#IFDEF     IncludeHelp
1232: 3E 03         0622  CmdHelp:    LD   A,kMsgAbout    ;Message = about text
1234: CD 23 02      0623              CALL OutputMessage  ;Output message
1237: CD 20 04      0624              CALL OutputNewLine  ;Output new line
123A: 3E 26         0625              LD   A,kMsgHelp     ;Message = help text
                    0626              ;JP   OutputMessage ;Output message
123C: CD 23 02      0627              CALL OutputMessage  ;Output message
                    0628  ; Look for ROM filing system help extensions
                    0629  #IFDEF      IncludeRomFS
123F: CD 45 1B      0630              CALL RomSearchInit
1242: CD 4D 1B      0631  @Loop:      CALL RomSearchNext
1245: C0            0632              RET  NZ
1246: CD 16 1C      0633              CALL RomGetType
1249: FE 03         0634              CP   3
124B: 20 F5         0635              JR   NZ,@Loop
124D: CD FB 1B      0636              CALL RomGetHlp
1250: CD 28 04      0637              CALL OutputZString
1253: 18 ED         0638              JR   @Loop
                    0639  #ELSE
                    0640              RET
                    0641  #ENDIF
                    0642  ;#ENDIF
                    0643  
                    0644  
                    0645  ; Command: Input: I <port address>
1255: 3E 03         0646  CmdIn:      LD   A,kReportAny   ;Report any error
1257: CD FD 0F      0647              CALL CLIGetHexParam
125A: D0            0648              RET  NC             ;Abort if any error
125B: 4D            0649              LD   C,L
125C: ED 78         0650              IN   A,(C)          ;Input from specified port
125E: CD 4C 06      0651              CALL StrWrHexByte   ;Write hex byte to string
1261: CD 77 06      0652              CALL StrWrNewLine   ;Write new line to string
1264: C3 04 06      0653              JP   StrPrint       ;Output string
                    0654  
                    0655  
                    0656  ; Command: Memory display: M [<start address>]
1267: 2A A1 FE      0657  CmdMemory:  LD   HL,(iParam1)   ;Default parameter
126A: 3E 01         0658              LD   A,kReportBad   ;Only report bad hex parameter
126C: CD FD 0F      0659              CALL CLIGetHexParam
126F: C0            0660              RET  NZ             ;Abort if bad hex in parameter
1270: 54            0661  @More:      LD   D,H
1271: 5D            0662              LD   E,L
1272: 0E 08         0663              LD   C,8            ;Number of lines of hex dump
1274: CD E3 07      0664  @Line:      CALL WrMemoryDump   ;Build string of one line of dump
1277: CD 04 06      0665              CALL StrPrint       ;Print to output device
127A: 0D            0666              DEC  C              ;End of dump?
127B: 20 F7         0667              JR   NZ,@Line       ;No, so loop back for another line
127D: 62            0668              LD   H,D
127E: 6B            0669              LD   L,E
127F: 22 A1 FE      0670              LD   (iParam1),HL   ;Update default parameter
1282: CD F7 02      0671              CALL InputMore      ;Print more?
1285: 28 E9         0672              JR   Z,@More        ;Yes, so repeat
1287: C9            0673              RET
                    0674  
                    0675  
                    0676  ; Command: New script: NEW
                    0677  #IFDEF      IncludeScripting
                    0678  CmdNew:     JP   ScrNew         ;Script, new command
                    0679  #ENDIF
                    0680  
                    0681  
                    0682  ; Command: Old script: OLD
                    0683  #IFDEF      IncludeScripting
                    0684  CmdOld:     JP   ScrOld         ;Script, old command
                    0685  #ENDIF
                    0686  
                    0687  
                    0688  ; Command: Output: O <port address> <port data>
1288: 3E 03         0689  CmdOut:     LD   A,kReportAny   ;Report any error
128A: CD FD 0F      0690              CALL CLIGetHexParam
128D: D0            0691              RET  NC             ;Abort if any error
128E: 4D            0692              LD   C,L
128F: 3E 03         0693              LD   A,kReportAny   ;Report any error
1291: CD FD 0F      0694              CALL CLIGetHexParam
1294: D0            0695              RET  NC             ;Abort if any error
1295: 7D            0696              LD   A,L
1296: ED 79         0697              OUT  (C),A
1298: C9            0698              RET
                    0699  
                    0700  
                    0701  ; Command: Registers: R [<name>]
1299:               0702  CmdRegisters:
1299: CD F4 0F      0703              CALL CLISkipDelim   ;Skip deliminater
129C: 20 0C         0704              JR   NZ,@Param      ;Go handle parameter
                    0705  ; No parameters, so just output register values
129E: CD 11 08      0706  @Show:      CALL WrRegister1
12A1: CD 04 06      0707              CALL StrPrint
12A4: CD 1C 08      0708              CALL WrRegister2
12A7: C3 04 06      0709              JP   StrPrint
                    0710  ; Handle parameter
12AA: D5            0711  @Param:     PUSH DE             ;Preserve start address of parameter
12AB: 21 87 13      0712              LD   HL,RegisterNames
12AE: 01 B3 13      0713              LD   BC,RegisterAddresses
12B1: CD 7D 07      0714              CALL SearchStringList
12B4: D1            0715              POP  DE             ;Restore start address of parameter
12B5: CA 53 10      0716              JP   Z,CLIBadParam  ;Report error if reg name not found
                    0717  ; Edit specified register's value
12B8: CD E7 05      0718              CALL StrInitDefault ;Initialise default string buffer
12BB: 0E 00         0719              LD   C,0            ;Length of register name
12BD: 1A            0720  @NextChar:  LD   A,(DE)         ;Get character of register name
12BE: FE 1F         0721              CP   kSpace-1       ;End of register name?
12C0: 38 0A         0722              JR   C,@Colon       ;Yes, so write colon
12C2: CD DE 06      0723              CALL ConvertCharToUCase
12C5: CD 38 06      0724              CALL StrWrChar      ;Write character to string buffer
12C8: 0C            0725              INC  C              ;Count characters in name
12C9: 13            0726              INC  DE             ;Increment to next char of name
12CA: 18 F1         0727              JR   @NextChar      ;  and go get next character
12CC: 3E 3A         0728  @Colon:     LD   A,':'          ;Get colon character
12CE: CD 38 06      0729              CALL StrWrChar      ;Write colon to string buffer
12D1: CD 92 06      0730              CALL StrWrSpace     ;Write space to string buffer
12D4: 5E            0731              LD   E,(HL)         ;Get register contents
12D5: 23            0732              INC  HL
12D6: 56            0733              LD   D,(HL)
12D7: 2B            0734              DEC  HL
12D8: 79            0735              LD   A,C            ;Length of register name
12D9: FE 01         0736              CP   1              ;One character long?
12DB: 20 06         0737              JR   NZ,@Word
12DD: 7B            0738              LD   A,E            ;Get lo byte 
12DE: CD 4C 06      0739              CALL StrWrHexByte   ;Write hex byte value of register
12E1: 18 03         0740              JR   @Space
12E3: CD 6C 06      0741  @Word:      CALL StrWrHexWord   ;Write hex word value of register
12E6: 3E 02         0742  @Space:     LD   A,2
12E8: CD 9A 06      0743              CALL StrWrSpaces    ;Write 2 spaces to string buffer
12EB: CD 04 06      0744              CALL StrPrint       ;Print current string ot output device
                    0745  ; Input line. HL = Memory location of register
12EE: CD 66 03      0746              CALL InputLine      ;Input text line
12F1: C8            0747              RET  Z              ;No input, so we're done
                    0748  ; Extract new register value from input buffer
12F2: E5            0749              PUSH HL             ;Preserve memory location
12F3: 3E 03         0750              LD   A,kReportAny   ;Report any error
12F5: CD FD 0F      0751              CALL CLIGetHexParam
12F8: 5D            0752              LD   E,L            ;Get hex value input
12F9: 54            0753              LD   D,H
12FA: E1            0754              POP  HL             ;Restore memory location
12FB: D0            0755              RET  NC             ;If any error then we're done
12FC: 73            0756              LD   (HL),E         ;Store lo byte at register location
12FD: 79            0757              LD   A,C            ;Length of register name
12FE: FE 01         0758              CP   1              ;One character long?
1300: C8            0759              RET  Z              ;Yes, so we're done
1301: 23            0760              INC  HL             ;Increment memory location
1302: 72            0761              LD   (HL),D         ;Store hi byte at register location
1303: C9            0762              RET
                    0763  
                    0764  
                    0765  ; Command: Reset: RESET
0000:               0766  CmdReset:   .EQU ColdStart      ;Command reset = Cold start system
1304: F3            0767              DI                  ;Disable interrupts
1305: C3 00 00      0768              JP   ColdStart      ;Run as if hardware reset
                    0769

                    0830  ; Command summary
                    0831  ; Char/Cmd,  Description     ? Other possible commands
                    0832  ;  :         Download intel hex record
                    0833  ;  ?         Help
                    0834  ;  A         Assemble        ?
                    0835  ;  B         Breakpoint      ?
                    0836  ;  C                         ? Compare, Copy, CPM
                    0837  ;  CONSOLE   Console select  ?
                    0838  ;  D         Disassemble     ? Dump, Display
                    0839  ;  DEVICES   Device list     ?
                    0840  ;  DIR       Directory list  ?
                    0841  ;  E         Edit memory     ? Execute
                    0842  ;  F         Flags           ? 
                    0843  ;  FILL      Fill memory     ?
                    0844  ;  G         Go              ?
                    0845  ;  H                         ?
                    0846  ;  HELP      Help            ?
                    0847  ;  I         Input from port ?
                    0848  ;  J                         ? Jump
                    0849  ;  K                         ? 
                    0850  ;  L                         ? Load, List 
                    0851  ;  M         Memory          ? Modify, Move
                    0852  ;  N                         ? Next, New
                    0853  ;  NEW       New script      ?
                    0854  ;  O         Output to port  ? Old
                    0855  ;  OLD       Old srcipt      ?
                    0856  ;  P                         ?
                    0857  ;  Q                         ?
                    0858  ;  R         Registers       ? 
                    0859  ;  RESET     Reset           ?
                    0860  ;  ROM       Directory list  ?
                    0861  ;  S         Step            ? Save, Script
                    0862  ;  SCRIPT    Script          ?
                    0863  ;  T                         ? Transfer
                    0864  ;  U                         ?
                    0865  ;  V                         ? Verify
                    0866  ;  W                         ?
                    0867  ;  X                         ?
                    0868  ;  Y                         ?
                    0869  ;  Z                         ?
                    0870

                    0872  ;  B         Breakpoint
                    0873  ;  E         Edit memory     (mini version -E)
                    0874  ;  F         Flags           (mini version needed)
                    0875  ;  G         Go
                    0876  ;  I         Input from port (mini version needed)

13CD: 89 FE         0989              .DW  iHL2           ;HL'
13CF: 76 FE         0990              .DW  iAF+1          ;A
13D1: 75 FE         0991              .DW  iAF            ;F
13D3: 78 FE         0992              .DW  iBC+1          ;B
13D5: 77 FE         0993              .DW  iBC            ;C
13D7: 7A FE         0994              .DW  iDE+1          ;D
13D9: 79 FE         0995              .DW  iDE            ;E
13DB: 7C FE         0996              .DW  iHL+1          ;H
13DD: 7B FE         0997              .DW  iHL            ;L
13DF: 8E FE         0998              .DW  iIR+1          ;H
                    0999  
                    1000  
                    1001  ; Flag names list (bit 7 delimited)
13E1: D3            1002  FlagNames:  .DB  128+'S'        ;S    Bit 7, Flag S set to 1 (negative/minus)
13E2: CE 53         1003              .DB  128+'N','S'    ;NS   Bit 7, Flag S cleared to 0 (positive)
13E4: CD            1004              .DB  128+'M'        ;M    Bit 7, Flag S set to 1 (negative/minus)
13E5: D0            1005              .DB  128+'P'        ;P    Bit 7, Flag S cleared to 0 (positive)
13E6: DA            1006              .DB  128+'Z'        ;Z    Bit 6, Flag Z set to 1 (zero)
13E7: CE 5A         1007              .DB  128+'N','Z'    ;NZ   Bit 6, Flag Z cleared to 0 (not zero)
13E9: C8            1008              .DB  128+'H'        ;H    Bit 4, Flag H set to 1 (half carry)
13EA: CE 48         1009              .DB  128+'N','H'    ;NH   4it 4, Flag H cleared to 0 (not half carry)
13EC: D0 41         1010              .DB  128+'P','A'    ;Pa   Bit 2, Flag P/V set to 1 (even parity or overflow)
13EE: CE 50         1011              .DB  128+'N','P'    ;NP   Bit 2, Flag P/V set to 0 (odd parity or no overflow)
13F0: D0 45         1012              .DB  128+'P','E'    ;PE   Bit 2, Flag P/V set to 1 (even parity or overflow)
13F2: D0 4F         1013              .DB  128+'P','O'    ;Po   Bit 2, Flag P/V set to 0 (odd parity or no overflow)
13F4: CE            1014              .DB  128+'N'        ;N    Bit 1, Flag N set to 1 (subtract)
13F5: CE 4E         1015              .DB  128+'N','N'    ;NN   Bit 1, Flag N cleared to 0 (add)
13F7: C3            1016              .DB  128+'C'        ;C    Bit 0, Flag C set to 1 (carry)
13F8: CE 43         1017              .DB  128+'N','C'    ;NC   Bit 0, Flag C cleared to 0 (not carry)
13FA: 80            1018              .DB  128            ;List terminator
                    1019  ; Flag manipulation list 
                    1020  ; Lo byte is ANDed with flags register, then Hi byte is ORed with flags register
                    1021  ; Yes, we could drive this from one byte but we are using the existing list handlers
                    1022  ; which need words, so might as well specify both AND and OR values!
13FB: 7F 80         1023  FlagLogic:  .DB  0x7F,0x80      ;S    Bit 7, Flag S set to 1 (negative/minus)
13FD: 7F 00         1024              .DB  0x7F,0x00      ;NS   Bit 7, Flag S cleared to 0 (positive)
13FF: 7F 80         1025              .DB  0x7F,0x80      ;M    Bit 7, Flag S set to 1 (negative/minus)
1401: 7F 00         1026              .DB  0x7F,0x00      ;P    Bit 7, Flag S cleared to 0 (positive)
1403: BF 40         1027              .DB  0xBF,0x40      ;Z    Bit 6, Flag Z set to 1 (zero)
1405: BF 00         1028              .DB  0xBF,0x00      ;NZ   Bit 6, Flag Z cleared to 0 (not zero)
1407: EF 10         1029              .DB  0xEF,0x10      ;H    Bit 4, Flag H set to 1 (half carry)
1409: EF 00         1030              .DB  0xEF,0x00      ;NH   4it 4, Flag H cleared to 0 (not half carry)
140B: FB 04         1031              .DB  0xFB,0x04      ;Pa   Bit 2, Flag P/V set to 1 (even parity or overflow)
140D: FB 00         1032              .DB  0xFB,0x00      ;NP   Bit 2, Flag P/V set to 0 (odd parity or no overflow)
140F: FB 04         1033              .DB  0xFB,0x04      ;PE   Bit 2, Flag P/V set to 1 (even parity or overflow)
1411: FB 00         1034              .DB  0xFB,0x00      ;Po   Bit 2, Flag P/V set to 0 (odd parity or no overflow)
1413: FD 02         1035              .DB  0xFD,0x02      ;N    Bit 1, Flag N set to 1 (subtract)
1415: FD 00         1036              .DB  0xFD,0x00      ;NN   Bit 1, Flag N cleared to 0 (add)
1417: FE 01         1037              .DB  0xFE,0x01      ;C    Bit 0, Flag C set to 1 (carry)
1419: FE 00         1038              .DB  0xFE,0x00      ;NC   Bit 0, Flag C cleared to 0 (not carry)
                    1039  
                    1040  
                    1041  ; **********************************************************************
                    1042  ; **  Private workspace (in RAM)                                      **
                    1043  ; **********************************************************************
                    1044  
                    1045              .DATA
                    1046  
FEA1: 00 00         1047  iParam1:    .DW  0x0000         ;CLI parameter 1
                    1048

FEA3: 00            1050  iMiniTerm:  .DB  0x00           ;Mini terminal flag

                    0063  #ENDIF
                    0064  #IFDEF      IncludeDisassemble
                    0065  #INCLUDE    Monitor\Disassembler.asm  ;In-line disassembler
                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  Disassembler support                      by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **********************************************************************
                    0004  
                    0005  ; This module provides instruction disassembly support. There are two
                    0006  ; main public functions which disassemble an instruction and provide the
                    0007  ; result as a string in the current string buffer. These are:
                    0008  ;
                    0009  ; Function: DisWrInstruction
                    0010  ; This returns a string in the format: Address: Opcodes  Mnemonic
                    0011  ; eg.   0300: CD FB 01       CALL 0x1FB
                    0012  ; It also returns the length of the instruction in bytes and the 
                    0013  ; address of the next instruction.
                    0014  ;
                    0015  ; Function: DisWrMnemonic
                    0016  ; This returns a string in the format: Mnemonic only
                    0017  ; eg.   CALL 0x1FB
                    0018  ; It also returns the length of the instruction in bytes.
                    0019  ;
                    0020  ; This module also provides the public helper function:
                    0021  ;
                    0022  ; Function: DisGetNextAddress
                    0023  ; Returns address of next instruction to be executed. This is used by 
                    0024  ; the single stepping feature. It takes into account flags to determine
                    0025  ; outcome of conditional instructions.
                    0026  ;
                    0027  ; Further documentation and notes are at the end of this file.
                    0028  ;
                    0029  ; Public functions provided
                    0030  ;   DisWrInstruction      Returns string if full details
                    0031  ;   DisWrMnemonic         Returns string of mnemonic only
                    0032  ;   DisGetNextAddress     Returns address of next instruction
                    0033  ; Private support function (not intended to be called from outside)
                    0034  ;   DisWrOperand          Process suppied operand
                    0035  ;   DisGetOpcode          Get instruction opcode
                    0036  ;   DisWrChar             Write a character to the buffer with filters
                    0037  ;   DisWrString           Write a string to the buffer with filters
                    0038  
                    0039  
                    0040  ; **********************************************************************
                    0041  ; **  Constants                                                       **
                    0042  ; **********************************************************************
                    0043  
0005:               0044  kDisBrack:  .EQU 5              ;Bracket flag
0004:               0045  kDisImmed:  .EQU 4              ;Immediate value flag
0003:               0046  kDisWord:   .EQU 3              ;Immediate value is word (not byte) flag
141B:               0047  kDisLength:
                    0047  kDisLength: .EQU 2              ;Substite two characters (not one) flag
0003:               0048  kDisMask:   .EQU 0x03           ;Mask type 0=0x07,1=0x18,2=0x30,3=0x38
                    0049  ; Should create EQUates for all numeric values used below. It's the law!
                    0050  
                    0051  
                    0052  ; **********************************************************************
                    0053  ; **  Public functions                                                **
                    0054  ; **********************************************************************
                    0055  
                    0056              .CODE
                    0057  
                    0058  ; Disassembler: Write full disassembly to string buffer
                    0059  ;   On entry: HL = Start of instruction to be disassembled
                    0060  ;   On exit:  Address, opcodes and mnemonic in current string buffer
                    0061  ;             iDisIndex variable used
                    0062  ;             A = Length of instruction in bytes
                    0063  ;             HL = Start address of next instruction
                    0064  ;             BC DE IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
141B:               0065  DisWrInstruction:
141B: C5            0066              PUSH BC
141C: D5            0067              PUSH DE
141D: 3E 14         0068              LD   A,20           ;Select string for mnemonic...
141F: 11 D0 FD      0069              LD   DE,kStrBuffer+80 ;Length 20 at end of default string
1422: CD EE 05      0070              CALL StrInitialise  ;Initialise string for mnemonic
1425: CD 58 14      0071              CALL DisWrMnemonic  ;Disassemble to mnemonic string
1428: 4F            0072              LD   C,A            ;Store length of instruction in bytes
                    0073  ;           XOR  A              ;CLear A to zero
1429: CD E7 05      0074              CALL StrInitDefault ;Select default string for opcodes
142C: 54            0075              LD   D,H            ;Get start of instruction..
142D: 5D            0076              LD   E,L
142E: CD 21 06      0077              CALL StrWrAddress   ;Write address, colon and space
1431: 41            0078              LD   B,C            ;Get length of instruction
1432: 7E            0079  @Opcode:    LD   A,(HL)         ;Get instruction opcode
1433: CD 4C 06      0080              CALL StrWrHexByte   ;Write as hex byte
1436: CD 92 06      0081              CALL StrWrSpace     ;Write space
1439: 23            0082              INC  HL             ;Point to next byte 
143A: 10 F6         0083              DJNZ @Opcode        ;Loop until all hex bytes written
143C: 3E 13         0084              LD   A,19           ;Column number
143E: CD 7F 06      0085              CALL StrWrPadding   ;Pad with spaces to specified column
1441: 41            0086              LD   B,C            ;Get length of instruction
1442: 1A            0087  @Ascii:     LD   A,(DE)         ;Get instruction opcode
1443: CD 2F 06      0088              CALL StrWrAsciiChar ;Write as ASCII character
1446: 13            0089              INC  DE             ;Point to next byte 
1447: 10 F9         0090              DJNZ @Ascii         ;Loop until all characters written
1449: 3E 19         0091              LD   A,25           ;Column number
144B: CD 7F 06      0092              CALL StrWrPadding   ;Pad with spaces to specified column
144E: 11 D0 FD      0093  @Mnemonic:  LD   DE,kStrBuffer+80
1451: CD 87 05      0094              CALL StrAppend      ;Append disassembly string
                    0095  ;           CALL StrWrNewLine   ;Write new line to string buffer
1454: 79            0096              LD   A,C            ;Get length of instruction in bytes
1455: D1            0097              POP  DE
1456: C1            0098              POP  BC
1457: C9            0099              RET
                    0100  
                    0101  
                    0102  ; Disassembler: Write mnemonic only to string buffer
                    0103  ;   On entry: HL = Start of instruction to be disassembled
                    0104  ;   On exit:  Mnemonic is written to current string buffer
                    0105  ;             iDisIndex variable used
                    0106  ;             A = Length of instruction in bytes
                    0107  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1458:               0108  DisWrMnemonic:
1458: C5            0109              PUSH BC
1459: D5            0110              PUSH DE
145A: E5            0111              PUSH HL
145B: DD E5         0112              PUSH IX
145D: FD E5         0113              PUSH IY
                    0114  ; Prepare to disassemble 
                    0115  ; HL = Address of current instruction
145F: E5            0116              PUSH HL             ;Copy start address of instruction
1460: FD E1         0117              POP  IY             ;  to IY 
1462: DD 21 59 18   0118              LD   IX,DisInst     ;Start of instruction table
1466: AF            0119              XOR  A
1467: 32 A4 FE      0120              LD   (iDisIndex),A  ;Clear index instruction opcode
146A: 5F            0121              LD   E,A            ;Clear prefix for extended instructions
146B: 56            0122              LD   D,(HL)         ;Instruction's primary opcode 
146C: 47            0123              LD   B,A            ;Offset to instruction's primary opcode
                    0124  ; Check for index register instruction (IX or IY)
146D: 7A            0125              LD   A,D            ;Could have been written LD A,(IY+0)
146E: FE DD         0126              CP   0xDD           ;IX instruction?
1470: 28 04         0127              JR   Z,@Index       ;Yes, so skip
1472: FE FD         0128              CP   0xFD           ;IY instruction?
1474: 20 07         0129              JR   NZ,@NotIndex   ;No, so skip
1476: 32 A4 FE      0130  @Index:     LD   (iDisIndex),A  ;Store index instruction opcode
1479: 04            0131              INC  B              ;Increment offset to primary opcode
147A: FD 7E 01      0132              LD   A,(IY+1)       ;Get next opcode byte
0000:               0133  @NotIndex:
                    0134  ; Check for extended instruction
147D: FE CB         0135              CP   0xCB           ;Extended instruction?
147F: 28 04         0136              JR   Z,@Extend      ;Yes, so skip
1481: FE ED         0137              CP   0xED           ;Extended instruction?
1483: 20 0D         0138              JR   NZ,@NotExtend  ;No, so skip
1485: 5F            0139  @Extend:    LD   E,A            ;Store prefix for extended instructions
1486: 04            0140              INC  B              ;Increment offset to primary opcode
1487: 3A A4 FE      0141              LD   A,(iDisIndex)  ;Get index instruction opcode
148A: B7            0142              OR   A              ;Is this an index instruction?
148B: 78            0143              LD   A,B            ;Prepare to read primary opcode
148C: 28 01         0144              JR   Z,@ExNoIndx    ;No, so skip
148E: 3C            0145              INC  A              ;Yes, skip index displacement byte
148F: CD 54 16      0146  @ExNoIndx:  CALL DisGetOpcode   ;Get primary opcode
1492:               0147  @NotExtend:
1492: 57            0147  @NotExtend: LD   D,A            ;Remember instruction's primary opcode
1493: 32 A7 FE      0148              LD   (iDisOpcode),A ;Store primary opcode
                    0149  ; Locate instruction table entry for current instruction (pointer to by HL)
                    0150  ; BASIC: (i And iMask(n)) = (iValue(n) And iMask(n)) ?
1496: DD 7E 00      0151  @Table:     LD   A,(IX+0)       ;Get opcode value from table
1499: DD A6 01      0152              AND  (IX+1)         ;AND with opcode mask from table
149C: 4F            0153              LD   C,A            ;Store Value AND Mask
149D: DD 7E 01      0154              LD   A,(IX+1)       ;Get opcode mask from table
14A0: A2            0155              AND  D              ;AND with instruction being disassembled
14A1: B9            0156              CP   C              ;Is this the correct table entry?
14A2: 20 14         0157              JR   NZ,@NotFound   ;No, so this is not the correct table
                    0158  ; BASIC: ... AND (p = iPrecode(n)) ?
14A4: AF            0159              XOR  A              ;Default precode for comparison = 0x00
14A5: DD CB 03 7E   0160              BIT  7,(IX+3)       ;Precode (index or extended)?
14A9: 28 0A         0161              JR   Z,@GotPrCode   ;No, so skip
14AB: 3E CB         0162              LD   A,0xCB         ;Default precode for comparison = 0xCB
14AD: DD CB 03 76   0163              BIT  6,(IX+3)       ;Precode = 0xED?
14B1: 28 02         0164              JR   Z,@GotPrCode   ;No, so skip
14B3: 3E ED         0165              LD   A,0xED         ;Yes, so precode for comparison = 0xED
14B5:               0166  @GotPrCode:
14B5: BB            0166  @GotPrCode: CP   E              ;Compare table precode with instruction
14B6: 28 09         0167              JR   Z,@Found       ;Yes, so this is the correct table
14B8: C5            0168  @NotFound:  PUSH BC             ;Preserve BC
14B9: 01 05 00      0169              LD   BC,5           ;No, so try next table entry
14BC: DD 09         0170              ADD  IX,BC          ;Point to next table entry
14BE: C1            0171              POP  BC             ;Restore BC
14BF: 18 D5         0172              JR   @Table
                    0173  ; We now have the correct instruction table entry (pointer to by IX)
                    0174  ; BASIC: (p = iPrecode(n)) And (i And iMask(n)) = (iValue(n) And iMask(n))
14C1: DD 7E 02      0175  @Found:     LD   A,(IX+2)       ;Get operation string number
14C4: 32 A5 FE      0176              LD   (iDisOpStr),A  ;Store operation string number
14C7: CD 76 16      0177              CALL DisWrString    ;Write operation string
14CA: CD 92 06      0178              CALL StrWrSpace
                    0179  ; BASIC: Operand sString(iOperand1(n)), t
14CD: DD 7E 03      0180              LD   A,(IX+3)       ;Get operand #1 string number
14D0: 32 A6 FE      0181              LD   (iDisOp1Str),A ;Store opcode #1 string number
14D3: 4A            0182              LD   C,D            ;Get primary opcode value
14D4: CD BD 15      0183              CALL DisWrOperand
                    0184  ; BASIC: Operand sString(iOperand2(n)), t
14D7: DD 7E 04      0185              LD   A,(IX+4)       ;Get operand #2 string number
14DA: 3D            0186              DEC  A              ;Is is 1? (null string)
14DB: 28 0C         0187              JR   Z,@NoOp2       ;Yes, so skip this operand
14DD: 3E 2C         0188              LD   A,','          ;Get comma character
14DF: CD 38 06      0189              CALL StrWrChar      ;Write comma to string
14E2: DD 7E 04      0190              LD   A,(IX+4)       ;Get operand #2 string number
14E5: 4A            0191              LD   C,D            ;Get primary opcode value
14E6: CD BD 15      0192              CALL DisWrOperand
0000:               0193  @NoOp2:
                    0194  ; If relative jump show absolute address in brackets
14E9: 3A A5 FE      0195              LD   A,(iDisOpStr)  ;Get operation string number
14EC: FE 1B         0196              CP   kDisJR         ;JR instruction?
14EE: 28 04         0197              JR   Z,@Rel         ;Yes, so skip
14F0: FE 1C         0198              CP   kDisDJNZ       ;DJNZ instruction?
14F2: 20 24         0199              JR   NZ,@NotRel     ;No so skip
14F4: 11 CA 16      0200  @Rel:       LD   DE,szDisTo     ;String = "  (to "
14F7: CD 9A 05      0201              CALL StrAppendZ     ;Append zero terminated string
14FA: FD E5         0202              PUSH IY             ;Push address of instruction
14FC: E1            0203              POP  HL             ;POP address of instruction
14FD: 23            0204              INC  HL             ;Increment to
14FE: 23            0205              INC  HL             ;  end of the JR/DJNZ instruction
14FF: 3A A8 FE      0206              LD   A,(iDisImmed)  ;Get immediate value from instruction
1502: 5F            0207              LD   E,A            ;Get displacement lo (signed byte)
1503: 16 00         0208              LD   D,0            ;Default to hi byte = zero
1505: CB 7F         0209              BIT  7,A            ;Displacement negative?
1507: 28 01         0210              JR   Z,@JRadd       ;No, so skip
1509: 15            0211              DEC  D              ;Yes, so set hi byte to 0xFF
150A: 19            0212  @JRadd:     ADD  HL,DE          ;Add signed 16-bit displacement
150B: 54            0213              LD   D,H            ;Get destination address hi byte
150C: 5D            0214              LD   E,L            ;Get destination address lo byte
150D: CD CB 07      0215              CALL WrHexPrefix    ;Write hex prefix to string
1510: CD 6C 06      0216              CALL StrWrHexWord   ;Write hex word to string
1513: 3E 29         0217              LD   A,')'          ;Get close bracket character
1515: CD 38 06      0218              CALL StrWrChar      ;Write close bracket to string
0000:               0219  @NotRel:
                    0220  ; Finish building mnemonic string
1518: 78            0221              LD   A,B            ;Get offset into instruction
1519: 3C            0222              INC  A              ;Increment to give instruction length
151A: FD E1         0223              POP  IY
151C: DD E1         0224              POP  IX
151E: E1            0225              POP  HL
151F: D1            0226              POP  DE
1520: C1            0227              POP  BC
1521: C9            0228              RET
                    0229  
                    0230  
                    0231  ; Disassembler: Get address of next instruction
                    0232  ;   On entry: HL = Next address ignoring flow control instructions
                    0233  ;             Next instruction
                    0234  ;   On exit:  HL = Next address after consider flow instructions
                    0235  ;             AF BC DE IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0236  ; Just prior to this function the next instruction must have been
                    0237  ; disassembled so instruction details are available in iDisXXXX
                    0238  ; workspace. Also breakpoint register values must be in workspace.
1522:               0239  DisGetNextAddress:
1522: F5            0240              PUSH AF
1523: C5            0241              PUSH BC
1524: D5            0242              PUSH DE
                    0243  ; Determine if last instruction may have changed program flow
1525: 3A A5 FE      0244              LD   A,(iDisOpStr)  ;Get operation string number
1528: FE 1B         0245              CP   kDisFlowF      ;Compare with first flow control instr
152A: 38 5C         0246              JR   C,@Done2       ;Not a flow instruction so we're done
152C: FE 23         0247              CP   kDisFlowL+1    ;Compare with first flow control instr
152E: 30 58         0248              JR   NC,@Done2      ;Not a flow instruction so we're done
                    0249  ; The current instruction is a flow controlling instruction eg. CALL
                    0250  ; Consider if the instruction is conditional or not by looking at
                    0251  ; operand 1 string number to see if it is the operand substitution
                    0252  ; string "c" or "cc" (for conditions NZ,Z,NC,C,PO,PE,P,M)
1530: 3A A6 FE      0253              LD   A,(iDisOp1Str) ;Get operand 1 string number
1533: 06 18         0254              LD   B,kDisMskC     ;Prepare condition 'c' mask
1535: FE 0A         0255              CP   kDisSubC       ;Is operand 'c'? eg. 'Z' in 'JR Z,addr'
1537: 28 06         0256              JR   Z,@Condition   ;Yes, so go handle condition
1539: 06 38         0257              LD   B,kDisMskCC    ;Prepare condition 'cc' mask
153B: FE 0B         0258              CP   kDisSubCC      ;Is operand 'cc'? eg. 'PE' in 'JP PE,addr'
153D: 20 1B         0259              JR   NZ,@NoCond     ;No, so skip condition evaluation
                    0260  ; Evaluate condition. B is the conditions bit mask
153F:               0261  @Condition:
153F: 3A A7 FE      0261  @Condition: LD   A,(iDisOpcode) ;Get instruction's primary opcode
1542: A0            0262              AND  B              ;Mask to give condition bits, Cy=0
1543: 1F            0263              RRA                 ;Shift condition bits to bits 0 to 1
1544: 1F            0264              RRA                 ;  to give offset in table
1545: 1F            0265              RRA                 ;  for each coded condition
1546: 1F            0266              RRA                 ;  plus flag state in Carry
1547: 47            0267              LD   B,A            ;Set up table loop counter
                    0268  ; Condition table contains one byte for each flag condition:
                    0269  ; 0=NZ/Z, 1=NC/C,2=PO/PE, 3=P/M and C set for Z, C, PE and M
                    0270  ; The values in the table are bit masks for the flags register
                    0271  ; which is ORed with Flags register to test if flag is set, and 
                    0272  ; then exclusively ORed with flags register to test if not set.
1548: E5            0273              PUSH HL
1549: 21 EE 16      0274              LD   HL,DisConTab-1 ;Point to start of condition table -1
154C: 04            0275              INC  B              ;Increment loop counter
154D: 23            0276  @IncPtr:    INC  HL             ;Inc pointer into condition table
154E: 10 FD         0277              DJNZ @IncPtr        ;Go inc again until we are there
1550: 3A 75 FE      0278              LD   A,(iAF)        ;Get flags register value
1553: 38 01         0279              JR   C,@ConTest     ;Looking for flag low?
1555: AE            0280              XOR  (HL)           ;Yes, so invert required flag bit
1556: A6            0281  @ConTest:   AND   (HL)          ;Mask required flag bit (clears Cy)
1557: E1            0282              POP  HL
1558: 28 5F         0283              JR   Z,@Done        ;We're done if condition not met
                    0284  ; Determine address after executing the flow controlling instruction
                    0285  ; when the condition is met (if there is a condition)
155A: 3A A5 FE      0286  @NoCond:    LD   A,(iDisOpStr)  ;Get operation string number
155D: FE 1F         0287              CP   kDisCALL       ;Instruction = "CALL"
155F: 28 55         0288              JR   Z,@CALL
1561: FE 1C         0289              CP   kDisDJNZ       ;Instruction = "DJNZ"
1563: 28 11         0290              JR   Z,@DJNZ
1565: FE 1E         0291              CP   kDisJP         ;Instruction = "JP"
1567: 28 21         0292              JR   Z,@JP
1569: FE 1B         0293              CP   kDisJR         ;Instruction = "JR"
156B: 28 0F         0294              JR   Z,@JR
156D: FE 1D         0295              CP   kDisRST        ;Instruction = "RST"
156F: 28 3B         0296              JR   Z,@RST
                    0297  ; Instruction: RET or RET cc or RETI or RETN
1571: 2A 81 FE      0298  @RET:       LD   HL,(iSP)       ;Get stack pointer at breakpoint
1574: 18 30         0299              JR   @JP2           ;Go to addess pointed to by HL
                    0300  ; Instruction: DJNZ n
1576: 3A 78 FE      0301  @DJNZ:      LD   A,(iBC+1)      ;Get register B value
1579: 3D            0302              DEC   A             ;Will B reach zero?
157A: 28 3D         0303              JR   Z,@Done        ;Yes, so we're done
                    0304  ;           JR   @JR            ;No, so go treat as JR instruction
                    0305  ; Instruction: JR n or JR c,n
157C: 3A A8 FE      0306  @JR:        LD   A,(iDisImmed)  ;Get immediate value from instruction
157F: 5F            0307              LD   E,A            ;Get displacement lo (signed byte)
1580: 16 00         0308              LD   D,0            ;Default to hi byte = zero
1582: CB 7F         0309              BIT  7,A            ;Displacement negative?
1584: 28 01         0310              JR   Z,@JRadd       ;No, so skip
1586: 15            0311              DEC  D              ;Yes, so set hi byte to 0xFF
1587: 19            0312  @JRadd:     ADD  HL,DE          ;Add signed 16-bit displacement
1588: 18 2F         0313  @Done2:     JR   @Done
                    0314  ; Instruction: JP nn or JP cc,nn or JP HL or JP IX or JP IY
158A: 3A A6 FE      0315  @JP:        LD   A,(iDisOp1Str) ;Get operand 1 string number
158D: FE 0B         0316              CP   kDisSubCC      ;Is operand 'cc'? eg. 'PE' in 'JP PE,addr'
158F: 28 25         0317              JR   Z,@GOTOnn      ;Yes, so goto address nn
1591: FE 04         0318              CP   kDisSubNN      ;Instruction = JP nn ?
1593: 28 21         0319              JR   Z,@GOTOnn      ;Yes, so goto address nn
                    0320  ; Instruction: JP HL or JP IX or JP IY
1595: 21 7B FE      0321              LD   HL,iHL         ;Point to register storage for HL
1598: 3A A4 FE      0322              LD   A,(iDisIndex)  ;Index register instruction?
159B: B7            0323              OR   A              ;No, so go JP 
159C: 28 08         0324              JR   Z,@JP2
159E: 23            0325              INC  HL             ;Point to register storage for IX
159F: 23            0326              INC  HL
15A0: FE DD         0327              CP   0xDD           ;Instruction = JP IX
15A2: 28 02         0328              JR   Z,@JP2
15A4: 23            0329              INC  HL             ;Point to register storage for IX
15A5: 23            0330              INC  HL
                    0331  ; Go to the address pointed to by HL
15A6: 7E            0332  @JP2:       LD   A,(HL)         ;Get lo byte of new address
15A7: 23            0333              INC  HL             ;Point to hi byte
15A8: 66            0334              LD   H,(HL)         ;Get hi byte of new address
15A9: 6F            0335              LD   L,A            ;Get lo byte of newreturn address
15AA: 18 0D         0336              JR   @Done
                    0337  ; Instruction: RST n
15AC: 3A A7 FE      0338  @RST:       LD   A,(iDisOpcode) ;Get primary opcode
15AF: E6 38         0339              AND  A,kDisMskRST   ;Mask off unwanted bits
15B1: 6F            0340              LD   L,A            ;Store as lo byte of new address
15B2: 26 00         0341              LD   H,0            ;Clear hi byte of new address
15B4: 18 03         0342              JR   @Done
                    0343  ; Instruction: CALL nn or CALL cc,nn
0000:               0344  @CALL:      ;JR  @JP            ;Treat as JP instruction
                    0345  ; Instruction: CALL nn or CALL cc,nn or JP nn pr JP cc,nn
15B6: 2A A8 FE      0346  @GOTOnn:    LD   HL,(iDisImmed) ;Get immediate value from instruction
                    0347  ;           JR   @Done
15B9: D1            0348  @Done:      POP  DE
15BA: C1            0349              POP  BC
15BB: F1            0350              POP  AF
15BC: C9            0351              RET
