                    0359  ; Disassembler: Write operand to buffer
                    0360  ;   On entry: A = Operand string number
                    0361  ;             B = Offset to opcode from start of instruction
                    0362  ;             C = Primary op-code
                    0363  ;             IY = Start address of instruction
                    0364  ;   On exit:  A = Unspecified
                    0365  ;             B = Updated offset to opcode from start of instruction
                    0366  ;             C = Not specified
                    0367  ;             DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
15BD:               0368  DisWrOperand:
15BD: E6 3F         0369              AND  kDisOpMask     ;Mask off flag bits
15BF: FE 0E         0370              CP   kDisSubsL+1    ;Substitution operand string?
15C1: D2 76 16      0371              JP   NC,DisWrString ;No, so just write string
15C4:               0372  @DisSubStr:
15C4: D5            0372  @DisSubStr: PUSH DE
15C5: E5            0373              PUSH HL
                    0374  ; Calculate operand table location for this operand and get details
15C6: 21 CF 16      0375              LD   HL,DisOperandTable-2
15C9: 87            0376              ADD  A,A            ;Two bytes per entry
15CA: 85            0377              ADD  A,L            ;Add to start of table
15CB: 6F            0378              LD   L,A            ;Store updated lo byte
15CC: 30 01         0379              JR   NC,@NoOverFlo  ;Skip if no overflow
15CE: 24            0380              INC  H              ;Overflow so increment hi byte
15CF:               0381  @NoOverFlo:
15CF: 5E            0381  @NoOverFlo: LD   E,(HL)         ;Get substitution string number
15D0: 23            0382              INC  HL             ;Point to BIILMM bits
15D1: 56            0383              LD   D,(HL)         ;Get BIILMM function bits
15D2: D5            0384              PUSH DE             ;So we can use E for scratch reg
                    0385  ; Process this operand as detailed in DE, left bracket?
15D3: CB 6A         0386              BIT  kDisBrack,D    ;Bracket flagged?
15D5: 28 05         0387              JR   Z,@NoBracL     ;No, so skip
15D7: 3E 28         0388              LD   A,'('          ;Get left bracket character
15D9: CD 38 06      0389              CALL StrWrChar      ;Print left bracket
0000:               0390  @NoBracL:   
                    0391  ; Process this operand as detailed in DE, immediate value?
15DC: CB 62         0392              BIT  kDisImmed,D    ;Immediate value flagged?
15DE: 28 1F         0393              JR   Z,@NoImmedia   ;No, so skip
15E0: CD CB 07      0394              CALL WrHexPrefix    ;Print "0x" (or whatever we use)
15E3: 04            0395              INC  B              ;Increment offset to lo byte
15E4: 78            0396              LD   A,B            ;Offset to instruction byte
15E5: CD 54 16      0397              CALL DisGetOpcode   ;Get lo byte of immediate value
15E8: 32 A8 FE      0398              LD   (iDisImmed),A  ;Store lo byte of immediate value
15EB: 5F            0399              LD   E,A            ;Store lo byte of immediate value
15EC: CB 5A         0400              BIT  kDisWord,D     ;Immediate value is a word?
15EE: 28 0B         0401              JR   Z,@ImmedLo     ;No, so skip
15F0: 04            0402              INC  B              ;Increment offset to hi byte
15F1: 78            0403              LD   A,B            ;Offset to instruction byte
15F2: CD 54 16      0404              CALL DisGetOpcode   ;Get hi byte of immediate value
15F5: 32 A9 FE      0405              LD   (iDisImmed+1),A  ;Store hi byte of immediate value
15F8: CD 4C 06      0406              CALL StrWrHexByte   ;Print hi byte of immediate value
15FB: 7B            0407  @ImmedLo:   LD   A,E            ;Restore lo byte of immediate value
15FC: CD 4C 06      0408              CALL StrWrHexByte   ;Print lo byte of immediate value
15FF:               0409  @NoImmedia:
                    0410  ; Process this operand as detailed in DE, right bracket?
15FF: CB 6A         0411              BIT  kDisBrack,D    ;Bracket flagged?
1601: 28 05         0412              JR   Z,@NoBracR     ;No, so skip
1603: 3E 29         0413              LD   A,')'          ;Get right bracket character
1605: CD 38 06      0414              CALL StrWrChar      ;Print right bracket
0000:               0415  @NoBracR:   
                    0416  ; Process this operand as detailed in DE, substitution string?
1608: D1            0417              POP  DE             ;Restore details
1609: 7B            0418              LD   A,E            ;Get substitution string number
160A: B7            0419              OR   A              ;String specified?
160B: 28 44         0420              JR   Z,@SubEnd      ;No, so skip
160D: 7A            0421              LD   A,D            ;Get BIILMM function bits
160E: E6 03         0422              AND  A,kDisMask     ;Separate mask type bits
1610: 21 EB 16      0423              LD   HL,DisMaskTable  ;Point to table of mask bits
1613: 85            0424              ADD  A,L            ;Add to start of table
1614: 6F            0425              LD   L,A            ;Store updated lo byte
1615: 30 01         0426              JR   NC,@NoOFlow    ;Skip if no overflow
1617: 24            0427              INC  H              ;Overflow so increment hi byte
1618: 7E            0428  @NoOFlow:   LD   A,(HL)         ;Get bit mask
1619: A1            0429              AND  A,C            ;Mask primary opcode
161A: 4F            0430              LD   C,A            ;Store masked primary opcode
161B: 7E            0431              LD   A,(HL)         ;Get bit mask
                    0432  ; Now shift primary opcode (masked) to right the number of
                    0433  ; times it takes to shift mask byte right before bit 1 is set
161C:               0434  @SubsShift:
161C: CB 3F         0434  @SubsShift: SRL  A              ;Shift mask right
161E: 38 04         0435              JR   C,@DoneShift   ;Bit 1 was set so we're done
1620: CB 39         0436              SRL  C              ;Shift primary opcode (masked) right
1622: 18 F8         0437              JR   @SubsShift     ;Go repeat..
1624:               0438  @DoneShift:
1624: CB 52         0438  @DoneShift: BIT  kDisLength,D   ;Length bit flagged?
1626: 28 02         0439              JR   Z,@Single      ;No, so skip
1628: CB 21         0440              SLA  C              ;Double value for two bytes
                    0441  ; C is now the offset into the substitute string
162A: 7B            0442  @Single:    LD   A,E            ;Substitute string number
162B: 21 F3 16      0443              LD   HL,DisString   ;Start of string list
162E: CD 53 07      0444              CALL FindStringInList ;Get start of string (=HL)
1631: 79            0445              LD   A,C            ;Offset into string
1632: 85            0446              ADD  A,L            ;Add to start of string
1633: 6F            0447              LD   L,A            ;Store updated lo byte
1634: 30 01         0448              JR   NC,@NoOver     ;Skip if no overflow
1636: 24            0449              INC  H              ;Overflow so increment hi byte
1637: 7E            0450  @NoOver:    LD   A,(HL)         ;Get substitute character
1638: FE 2A         0451              CP   '*'            ;Code for 2 byte HL/IX/IY string
163A: 20 07         0452              JR   NZ,@NotStar    ;No, so skip
163C: 3E 18         0453              LD   A,24           ;String = "HL"
163E: CD 76 16      0454              CALL DisWrString    ;Print string with substitutions
1641: 18 0E         0455              JR   @SubEnd
1643: CD 63 16      0456  @NotStar:   CALL DisWrChar      ;Print character with filters
1646: CB 52         0457              BIT  kDisLength,D   ;Length bit flagged?
1648: 28 07         0458              JR   Z,@SubEnd      ;No, so skip
164A: 23            0459              INC  HL             ;Point to second substitute character
164B: 7E            0460              LD   A,(HL)         ;Get substitute character
164C: FE 2E         0461              CP   '.'            ;Do not print '.' character
164E: C4 63 16      0462              CALL NZ,DisWrChar   ;Print character with filters
1651: E1            0463  @SubEnd:    POP  HL
1652: D1            0464              POP  DE
1653: C9            0465              RET
                    0466  
                    0467  
                    0468  ; Disassembler: Get instruction opcode
                    0469  ;   On entry: A = Offset from start of instruction
                    0470  ;             IY = Start of instruction
                    0471  ;   On exit:  A = Opcode
                    0472  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1654:               0473  DisGetOpcode:
1654: C5            0474              PUSH BC
1655: FD E5         0475              PUSH IY
1657: 4F            0476              LD   C,A            ;Offset from start of instruction
1658: 06 00         0477              LD   B,0            ;Clear hi byte ready for addition
165A: FD 09         0478              ADD  IY,BC          ;Calculate location of opcode
165C: FD 7E 00      0479              LD   A,(IY+0)       ;Get opcode from memory
165F: FD E1         0480              POP  IY
1661: C1            0481              POP  BC
1662: C9            0482              RET
                    0483  
                    0484  
                    0485  ; Disassembler: Write character to string buffer
                    0486  ;   On entry: A = Character to write
                    0487  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0488  ; This version of write character removes bit 7 (the new string flag)
                    0489  ; and replaces "-" with "(HL)"
0000:               0490  DisWrChar:
1663: F5            0491              PUSH AF
1664: E6 7F         0492              AND  A,0x7F         ;Mask off bit 7 (string start bit)
1666: FE 2D         0493              CP   '-'            ;Code for "(HL)" ?
1668: 28 05         0494              JR   Z,@SubHL       ;Yes, so go write "(HL)" instead
166A: CD 38 06      0495              CALL StrWrChar      ;Print character
166D: 18 05         0496              JR   @Done
166F: 3E 15         0497  @SubHL:     LD   A,21           ;String number for "(HL)"
1671: CD 76 16      0498              CALL DisWrString    ;Write "(HL)" instead of "-"
1674: F1            0499  @Done:      POP  AF
1675: C9            0500              RET                 ;JP instead to save byte
                    0501  
                    0502  
                    0503  ; Write disassembler string
                    0504  ;   On entry: A = Disassembler data string number
                    0505  ;             B = Offset to current opcode from start of instruction
                    0506  ;             IY = Start address for current instruction
                    0507  ;             (iDisIndex) = Index instruction opcode or zero
                    0508  ;   On exit:  AF C DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0509  ;             B is incremented if (IX/IY+d) is substituted
                    0510  ; This version of write string removes bit 7 (the new string flag)
                    0511  ; If iDisTmp1 (the current index instruction opcode) is 0xDD or 0xFD, 
                    0512  ; is not zero then strings are replaced: 
                    0513  ;   HL is replaced with IX or IY
                    0514  ;   (HL) is replaced with (IX + d) or (IY + d) except for JP instruction
                    0515  ;   where is is just replaced by (IX) or (IY)
1676:               0516  DisWrString:
1676: F5            0517              PUSH AF
1677: E5            0518              PUSH HL
1678: 6F            0519              LD   L,A            ;Store string number
1679: FE 15         0520              CP   kDisBracHL     ;String = (HL) ?
167B: 28 04         0521              JR   Z,@Subs        ;Yes, so go do substitution
167D: FE 18         0522              CP   kDisHL         ;String = HL ?
167F: 20 0C         0523              JR   NZ,@GotString  ;No, so just write the string
                    0524  ; Substitute IX/IY in HL string or (IX/IY+d) in (HL) string
1681: 3A A4 FE      0525  @Subs:      LD   A,(iDisIndex)  ;Get index instruction opcode
1684: B7            0526              OR   A              ;Index instruction?
1685: 28 06         0527              JR   Z,@GotString   ;No, so skip substitutions
1687: 2C            0528              INC  L              ;Increment to IX string number
1688: FE DD         0529              CP   0xDD           ;IX instruction?
168A: 28 01         0530              JR   Z,@GotString   ;Yes, so go write it
168C: 2C            0531              INC  L              ;Increment to IY string
168D:               0532  @GotString:
168D: 7D            0532  @GotString: LD   A,L            ;Get string number
168E: 21 F3 16      0533              LD   HL,DisString   ;Start of string list
1691: CD 53 07      0534              CALL FindStringInList ;Find start of string A
                    0535  ; HL now points to disassembler string
1694: 7E            0536  @Char:      LD   A,(HL)         ;Get character from string
1695: E6 7F         0537              AND  0x7F           ;Mask off string start bit
1697: FE 2B         0538              CP   '+'            ;Is it a '+' sign (displacement) ?
1699: 28 05         0539              JR   Z,@Plus        ;No, so skip to next character
169B: CD 38 06      0540              CALL StrWrChar      ;Write character
169E: 18 22         0541              JR   @Next          ;No, so skip to next character
                    0542  ; Encountered a plus sign so expecting to show a displacement
16A0: 3A A5 FE      0543  @Plus:      LD   A,(iDisOpStr)  ;Get instruction string
16A3: FE 1E         0544              CP   kDisJP         ;JP instruction?
16A5: 20 07         0545              JR   NZ,@Displace   ;No, so go show displacement
16A7: 3E 29         0546              LD   A,')'          ;Yes, so just terminate with ')'
16A9: CD 38 06      0547              CALL StrWrChar      ;Write close bracket character
16AC: 18 19         0548              JR   @End
                    0549  ; Show displacement in (IX+...) and (IY+...) instructions
16AE: 3E 2B         0550  @Displace:  LD   A,'+'
16B0: CD 38 06      0551              CALL StrWrChar      ;Write plus character
16B3: CD CB 07      0552              CALL WrHexPrefix
16B6: FD 7E 02      0553              LD   A,(IY+2)       ;Get index instruction displacement
16B9: CD 4C 06      0554              CALL StrWrHexByte   ;Write displacement in hex
16BC: 3E 29         0555              LD   A,')'
16BE: CD 38 06      0556              CALL StrWrChar      ;Write close bracket character
16C1: 04            0557              INC  B              ;Increment opcode offset
                    0558  ; Consider next character in disassembler string
16C2: 23            0559  @Next:      INC  HL             ;Point to next character
16C3: CB 7E         0560              BIT  7,(HL)         ;Start of new string?
16C5: 28 CD         0561              JR   Z,@Char        ;No, so go get next character
16C7: E1            0562  @End:       POP  HL
16C8: F1            0563              POP  AF
16C9: C9            0564              RET

                    0572  ; Strings
16CA: 20 20 28 74   0573  szDisTo:    .DB  "  (to ",kNull
16CE: 6F 20 00      5814  
                    0574  
                    0575  
                    0576  ; Operand table:
                    0577  ;   Index into table is the operand string number 1 to 13)
                    0578  ;   Contents: Substitution string number, function bits BIILMM
                    0579  ;
                    0580  ; Op  String  Offset  Bracket  Immediate  Substitue     subsLen  subsMask  ->  BIILMM
                    0581  ;  1  =""     +0      No       No   (00)  No                n/a  n/a           000000
                    0582  ;  2  ="n"    +1      No       Byte (10)  No                n/a  n/a           010000
                    0583  ;  3  ="(n)"  +1      Yes      Byte (10)  No                n/a  n/a           110000
                    0584  ;  4  ="nn"   +2      No       Word (11)  No                n/a  n/a           011000
                    0585  ;  5  ="(nn)" +2      Yes      Word (11)  No                n/a  n/a           111000
                    0586  ;  6  ="r"    +0      No       No   (00)  "BCDEHL-A"          1  0x38 (11)     000011
                    0587  ;  7  ="r2"   +0      No       No   (00)  "BCDEHL-A"          1  0x07 (00)     000000
                    0588  ;  8  ="dd"   +0      No       No   (00)  "BCDEHLSP"          2  0x30 (10)     000110
                    0589  ;  9  ="qq"   +0      No       No   (00)  "BCDEHLAF"          2  0x30 (10)     000110
                    0590  ; 10  ="c"    +0      No       No   (00)  "NZZ NCC "          2  0x18 (01)     000101
                    0591  ; 11  ="cc"   +0      No       No   (00)  "NZZ NCC POPEP M "  2  0x38 (11)     000111
                    0592  ; 12  ="t"    +0      No       No   (00)  "0008101820283038"  2  0x38 (11)     000111
                    0593  ; 13  ="b"    +0      No       No   (00)  "01234567"          1  0x38 (11)     000011
                    0594  ; Each table entry is coded with the string number plus a byte containing BIILMM bits
                    0595  ; Length bit is hi for strings with two character substitutions
16D1:               0596  DisOperandTable:
16D1: 00 00         0597              .DB  0x00,0x00      ;0b000000 ;Operand  1 = ""
16D3: 00 10         0598              .DB  0x00,0x10      ;0b010000 ;Operand  2 = "n"
16D5: 00 30         0599              .DB  0x00,0x30      ;0b110000 ;Operand  3 = "(n)"
16D7: 00 18         0600              .DB  0x00,0x18      ;0b011000 ;Operand  4 = "nn"
16D9: 00 38         0601              .DB  0x00,0x38      ;0b111000 ;Operand  5 = "(nn)"
16DB: 0E 03         0602              .DB  0x0E,0x03      ;0b000011 ;Operand  6 = "r"
16DD: 0E 00         0603              .DB  0x0E,0x00      ;0b000000 ;Operand  7 = "r2"
16DF: 0F 06         0604              .DB  0x0F,0x06      ;0b000110 ;Operand  8 = "dd"
16E1: 10 06         0605              .DB  0x10,0x06      ;0b000110 ;Operand  9 = "qq"
16E3: 11 05         0606              .DB  0x11,0x05      ;0b000101 ;Operand 10 = "c"
16E5: 12 07         0607              .DB  0x12,0x07      ;0b000111 ;Operand 11 = "cc"
16E7: 13 07         0608              .DB  0x13,0x07      ;0b000111 ;Operand 12 = "t"
16E9: 14 03         0609              .DB  0x14,0x03      ;0b000011 ;Operand 13 = "b"
                    0610  
                    0611  ; Mask table
                    0612  ; These are the masks used to separate token values such as register "BCDEHL-A"
                    0613  ; The index into the table is coded in the two mask bits from the above table.
16EB:               0614  DisMaskTable:
16EB: 07            0615              .DB  0x07           ;Mask type 0
16EC: 18            0616              .DB  0x18           ;Mask type 1
16ED: 30            0617              .DB  0x30           ;Mask type 2
16EE: 38            0618              .DB  0x38           ;Mask type 3
                    0619  
                    0620  
                    0621  ; Condition mask table
                    0622  ; The condition mask table contains one byte for each condition flag:
                    0623  ; NZ,Z,NC,C,PO,PE,P,M where offset into table is 0 for Z/NZ, 1 for C/NC, etc
                    0624  ; The value of each table entry is a bit mask, which when exclusively for the flags register
                    0625              ;SZ-H-PNC   Condition, Flag, Description
16EF: 40            0626  DisConTab:  .DB  0x40           ;xZxxxxxx   NZ,        Z=0,  Not Zero
16F0: 01            0627              .DB  0x01           ;xxxxxxxC   NC,        C=0,  No Carry
16F1: 04            0628              .DB  0x04           ;xxxxxxxC   PO,        P=0,  Parity Odd
16F2: 80            0629              .DB  0x80           ;xxxxxxxC   PO,        S=0,  Positive
                    0630  
                    0631  
                    0632  ; Include the data tables  DisString: and DisInst:
                    0633  ;
                    0634  ; Disassembler string table: (DisString)
                    0635  ; This contains many string which are not null terminated and have no length 
                    0636  ; value. Instead they are tightly packed with the start of each string 
                    0637  ; indicated by the first character having bit 7 set.
                    0638  ;
                    0639  ; Instruction table: (DisInst)
                    0640  ; The instruction table definition of the processor's instruction set.
                    0641  ; Each instruction is described by 5 bytes:
                    0642  ;    Byte 0:  Opcode value
                    0643  ;    Byte 1:  Opcode mask
                    0644  ;    Byte 2:  Operation string number
                    0645  ;    Byte 3:  Operand #1 string number, plus bits 6-7 define precode
                    0646  ;    Byte 4:  Operand #2 string number
                    0647  ; The precode values code in bits 6-7 are:
                    0648  ;    00xx xxxx = No precode
                    0649  ;    10xx xxxx = Precode 0xCB
                    0650  ;    11xx xxxx = Precode 0xED
                    0651  ; Precodes are used by the processor's extended instructions
                    0652  #INCLUDE    Monitor\DisData.asm
000D:               0001  kDisSubsL:	.EQU 13	;Last operand substitution string
0004:               0002  kDisSubNN:	.EQU 4	;Operand substitution string 'nn'
000A:               0003  kDisSubC:	.EQU 10	;Operand substitution string 'c'
000B:               0004  kDisSubCC:	.EQU 11	;Operand substitution string 'cc'
16F3:               0005  kDisBracHL:
                    0005  kDisBracHL:	.EQU 21	;Bracketed HL
0018:               0006  kDisHL:	.EQU 24	;HL
001B:               0007  kDisFlowF:	.EQU 27	;First flow control instructions
0022:               0008  kDisFlowL:	.EQU 34	;Last flow control instructions
001B:               0009  kDisJR:	.EQU 27	;Operation string 'JR'

                    0655  ; **********************************************************************
                    0656  ; **  Private workspace (in RAM)                                      **
                    0657  ; **********************************************************************
                    0658  
                    0659              .DATA
                    0660  
FEA4: 00            0661  iDisIndex:  .DB  0x00           ;Index instruction opcode
FEA5: 00            0662  iDisOpStr:  .DB  0x00           ;Operation string number
FEA6:               0663  iDisOp1Str:
FEA6: 00            0663  iDisOp1Str: .DB  0x00           ;Operand 1 string number
FEA7:               0664  iDisOpcode:
FEA7: 00            0664  iDisOpcode: .DB  0x00           ;Primary instruction opcode
FEA8: 00 00         0665  iDisImmed:  .DW  0x0000         ;Immediate value
                    0666  
                    0667  
                    0668  ; Disassembler: How it works...
                    0669  ; Solution is to use data table to define functions for each operand (see below)
                    0670  ; Decoding table: where the table entry is determined by the operand string number
                    0671  ; String numbers 0 to 12:
                    0672  ;   If bracket flagged print "("
                    0673  ;   If immediate byte flagged print hex byte at PC+Offset+1, Offset += 1
                    0674  ;   If immediate word flagged print word byte at PC+Offset+1 and PC+Offset+2, Offset += 2
                    0675  ;   If bracket flagged print ")"
                    0676  ;   If substitution string specified: (non-zero value)
                    0677  ;     n = opcode and SubsMask
                    0678  ;     n = n >> x, where x is the number of right shifts of the mask til bit 0 is a 1
                    0679  ;     If subsLen is 2 then n = n << 1
                    0680  ;     c =  character at (start of Substitution String + n + 0)
                    0681  ;     if = "-" then print "(HL)" else print character c
                    0682  ;     If SubsLen = 2 then
                    0683  ;       print character at (start of Substitution String + n + 1)
                    0684  ;     Endif
                    0685  ;   End if
                    0686  
                    0687  
                    0688  ; Single stepping
                    0689  ; Instructions which can change flow of code (ie. alter PC)
                    0690  ;   DJNZ d          10 nn      0001 0000
                    0691  ;   JR   d          18 nn      0001 1000
                    0692  ;   JR   c,  d      xx nn      001c c000
                    0693  ;   JP   nn         C3 nn nn   1100 0011
                    0694  ;   JP   cc, nn     xx nn nn   11cc c010
                    0695  ;   JP   HL         E9         1110 1001
                    0696  ;   JP   IX         DD E9      1110 1001
                    0697  ;   JP   IY         FD E9      1110 1001
                    0698  ;   CALL nn         CD nn nn   1100 1101
                    0699  ;   CALL cc, nn     xx nn nn   11cc c100
                    0700  ;   RET             C9         1100 1001
                    0701  ;   RET  cc         xx         11cc c000
                    0702  ;   RETI            ED 4D      0100 1101
                    0703  ;   RETN            ED 45      0100 0101
                    0704  ;   RST  aa         xx         11tt t111
                    0705  ; Also an interrupt or reset signal changes PC
                    0706  ; The above instructions are trapped after disassembly and the next instruction
                    0707  ; determined. The breakpoint is then placed here to allow single stepping.
                    0708  
                    0709  
                    0710  ; **********************************************************************
                    0711  ; **  End of Disassembler support module                              **
                    0712  ; **********************************************************************
                    0713

                    0067  #IFDEF      IncludeHexLoader
                    0068  #INCLUDE    Monitor\HexLoader.asm ;Intel hex loader
                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  Hex file loader                           by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **********************************************************************
                    0004  
                    0005  ; This module loads an Intel Hex file from the current input device.
                    0006  ;
                    0007  ; An Intel Hex file is a text file where each line is a record.
                    0008  ;
                    0009  ; A record starts with a colon (':') and ends with CR and/or LF.
                    0010  ;
                    0011  ; The next two characters form a hex byte which is the number of data
                    0012  ; bytes in the record.
                    0013  ;
                    0014  ; The next four characters form a hex word which is the start address 
                    0015  ; for the record's data bytes. High byte first.
                    0016  ;
                    0017  ; The next two characters form a hex byte which describes the type of
                    0018  ; record. 0x00 is a normal data record. 0x01 is an end of file marker.
                    0019  ;
                    0020  ; Then follows the specified number of data bytes, each written as two 
                    0021  ; character hex number.
                    0022  ;
                    0023  ; Finally there is a checksum byte in the form of a two character hex
                    0024  ; number. 
                    0025  ;
                    0026  ; To test the checksum simply add up all bytes in the record, including
                    0027  ; the checksum (but not the colon), and test to see if it is zero.
                    0028  ;
                    0029  ; The checksum is calculated by adding together all bytes in the record
                    0030  ; except the checksum byte and the colon character, ANDing with 0xFF 
                    0031  ; and then subtracting from 0x100. 
                    0032  ;
                    0033  ; Example record:
                    0034  ;   :0300300002337A1E 
                    0035  ; Record Length: 03 (3 bytes of data)
                    0036  ; Address: 0030 (the 3 bytes will be stored at 0030, 0031, and 0032)
                    0037  ; Record Type: 00 (normal data)
                    0038  ; Data: 02, 33, 7A
                    0039  ; Checksum: 1E (03 + 00 + 30 + 00 + 02 + 33 + 7A = E2, 100 - E2 = 1E)
                    0040  ;
                    0041  ; The last line of the file is an end marker with 00 data bytes and 
                    0042  ; record type 01, and so is:
                    0043  ;   :00000001FF
                    0044  ;
                    0045  ; Test file: (loads data 0x03 0x02 0x01 to address 0x4000)
                    0046  ;   :03400000030201B7
                    0047  ;   :00000001FF
                    0048  ;
                    0049  ; Public functions provided
                    0050  ;   HexLoad               Load hex file from the current console input
                    0051  
                    0052  
                    0053  ; **********************************************************************
                    0054  ; **  Public functions                                                **
                    0055  ; **********************************************************************
                    0056  
                    0057              .CODE
                    0058  
                    0059  ; HexLoader: Load an intel hex file from the current console input
                    0060  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0061  ;   On exit:  IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1A84: 0E 00         0062  HexLoad:    LD   C,0            ;Clear checksum of this whole file
1A86: CD 25 03      0063  @Line:      CALL InputChar      ;Get first character in record/line
1A89: FE 20         0064              CP   kSpace         ;Control character?
1A8B: 38 F9         0065              JR   C,@Line        ;Yes, so discard it
1A8D: FE 3A         0066              CP   kColon         ;Colon?
1A8F: C0            0067              RET  NZ             ;No, so return with this character
                    0068  ;           LD   C,0            ;Clear checksum for this line only
                    0069  ; Get number of data bytes in this record
1A90: CD D0 1A      0070              CALL HexGetByte     ;Get number of data bytes
1A93: 47            0071              LD   B,A            ;Store number of data bytes in record
1A94: 81            0072              ADD  A,C            ;Add to checksum
1A95: 4F            0073              LD   C,A
                    0074  ; Get start address for this record
1A96: CD D0 1A      0075              CALL HexGetByte     ;Get address hi byte
1A99: 57            0076              LD   D,A            ;Store address hi byte
1A9A: 81            0077              ADD  A,C            ;Add to checksum
1A9B: 4F            0078              LD   C,A
1A9C: CD D0 1A      0079              CALL HexGetByte     ;Get address lo byte
1A9F: 5F            0080              LD   E,A            ;Store address lo byte
1AA0: 81            0081              ADD  A,C            ;Add to checksum
1AA1: 4F            0082              LD   C,A
                    0083  ; Get record type
1AA2: CD D0 1A      0084              CALL HexGetByte     ;Get record type
1AA5: 67            0085              LD   H,A            ;Store record type
1AA6: 81            0086              ADD  A,C            ;Add to checksum
1AA7: 4F            0087              LD   C,A
                    0088  ; Input any data bytes in this record
1AA8: 78            0089              LD   A,B            ;Get number of bytes in record
1AA9: B7            0090              OR   A              ;Zero?
1AAA: 28 09         0091              JR   Z,@Check       ;Yes, so skip..
1AAC: CD D0 1A      0092  @Data:      CALL HexGetByte     ;Get data byte
1AAF: 12            0093              LD   (DE),A         ;Store data byte in memory
1AB0: 13            0094              INC  DE             ;Point to next memory location
1AB1: 81            0095              ADD  A,C            ;Add to checksum
1AB2: 4F            0096              LD   C,A
1AB3: 10 F7         0097              DJNZ @Data
                    0098  ; Get checksum byte for this record
1AB5: CD D0 1A      0099  @Check:     CALL HexGetByte     ;Get checksum byte
1AB8: 81            0100              ADD  A,C            ;Add to checksum
1AB9: 4F            0101              LD   C,A
                    0102  ; Should now test checksum for this line, but instead keep a checksum 
                    0103  ; for the whole file and test only at the end. This avoids having to 
                    0104  ; store a failure flag (no registers left) whilst still allowing this
                    0105  ; function to flush all lines of the file.
                    0106  ;Test for end of file
1ABA: 7C            0107              LD   A,H            ;Get record type
1ABB: FE 01         0108              CP   1              ;End of file?
1ABD: 20 C7         0109              JR   NZ,@Line       ;No, so repeat for next record
                    0110  ; End of file so test checksum
1ABF: 79            0111              LD   A,C            ;Get checksum
1AC0: B7            0112              OR   A              ;It should be zero?
1AC1: 3E 28         0113              LD   A,kMsgReady    ;Prepare for checksum ok message
1AC3: 28 02         0114              JR   Z,@Result      ;Skip if checksum ok
1AC5: 3E 29         0115              LD   A,kMsgFileEr   ;File error message number
1AC7: CD 69 08      0116  @Result:    CALL MonOutputMsg   ;Output message #A
1ACA: AF            0117              XOR  A              ;Return null character
1ACB: 3E 05         0118              LD   A,kNewLine
1ACD: 3E 0D         0119              LD   A,kReturn
1ACF: C9            0120              RET
                    0121  
                    0122  
                    0123  ; **********************************************************************
                    0124  ; **  Private functions                                               **
                    0125  ; **********************************************************************
                    0126  
                    0127  ; HexLoader: Get byte from two hex characters from current console input
                    0128  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0129  ;   On exit:  A = Bytes received
                    0130  ;             BC DE H IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1AD0:               0131  HexGetByte:
1AD0: CD 25 03      0131  HexGetByte: CALL InputChar      ;Get character from input device
1AD3: CD BD 06      0132              CALL ConvertCharToNumber
1AD6: 07            0133              RLCA
1AD7: 07            0134              RLCA
1AD8: 07            0135              RLCA
1AD9: 07            0136              RLCA
1ADA: 6F            0137              LD   L,A            ;Store result hi nibble
1ADB: CD 25 03      0138              CALL InputChar      ;Get character from input device
1ADE: CD BD 06      0139              CALL ConvertCharToNumber
1AE1: B5            0140              OR   A,L            ;Get result byte
1AE2: C9            0141              RET
                    0142  
                    0143  
                    0144  ; **********************************************************************
                    0145  ; **  End of Hex file loader module                                   **
                    0146  ; **********************************************************************
                    0147  
                    0148  
                    0149  
                    0069  #ENDIF
                    0070  #IFDEF      IncludeScripting
                    0071  #INCLUDE    Monitor\Script.asm  ;Simple scripting language
                    0072  #ENDIF
                    0073  
                    0074  ; Extensions
                    0075  #IFDEF      IncludeTrace
                    0076  #INCLUDE    Monitor\Trace.asm   ;Trace execution (needs disassembler)
                    0077  #ENDIF
                    0078  #IFDEF      IncludeRomFS
                    0079  #INCLUDE    Monitor\RomFS.asm   ;ROM filing system
                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  ROM Filing System module                  by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **********************************************************************
                    0004  ;
                    0005  ; This module provides a simple read only filing system designed to
                    0006  ; allow code and data to be stored in ROM, including banked ROM.
                    0007  
                    0008  ; For a system with multiple ROM banks, each 16k bytes long and 
                    0009  ; starting at address 0x0000, the first reference is 0x3FF0, the second 
                    0010  ; at 0x3FE0, and so on.
                    0011  ;
                    0012  ; WARNING: Supports a maximum of 15 files in each ROM bank
                    0013  ;
                    0014  ; Each file reference contains the following bytes:
                    0015  ; Offset    Description
                    0016  ; +0x00     Reference identifier byte 0x55
                    0017  ; +0x01     Reference identifier byte 0xAA
                    0018  ; +0x02     File name, 8 characters, padded with trailing spaces
                    0019  ; +0x0A     File type and flags (see below)
                    0020  ; +0x0B     File destination (see below)
                    0021  ; +0x0C     File start address, 16-bit, low byte first
                    0022  ; +0x0E     File length, 16-bit, low byte first
                    0023  ;
                    0024  ; The file name contains eight ASCII characters. File names shorter than 
                    0025  ; eight characters long have the unused trailing bytes filled with ASCII 
                    0026  ; spaces (0x20). The file name can contain characters A to Z, a to z, 0 
                    0027  ; to 9, underscore, but no spaces other than the trailing bytes.
                    0028  ; File names must be at least two characters long so they do not 
                    0029  ; conflict with single letter monitor commands.
                    0030  ;
                    0031  ; File types and flags:
                    0032  ;  +-------+-------+-------+-------+-------------------------------+
                    0033  ;  | Auto  | Move  | 0     | 0     | File type (0 to 15)           |
                    0034  ;  +-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+
                    0035  ;  | Bit 7 | Bit 6 | Bit 5 | Bit 4 | Bit 3 | Bit 2 | Bit 1 | Bit 0 |
                    0036  ;  +-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+-------+
                    0037  ;  File flags:                       File types:
                    0038  ;  Bit 7 = Auto run                  0 = .DAT  Unspecified
                    0039  ;  Bit 6 = Move* to RAM and run      1 = .COM  Monitor command
                    0040  ;  Bit 5 = Reserved (zero)           2 = .EXE  Executable
                    0041  ;  Bit 4 = Reserved (zero)           3 = .HLP  Help
                    0042  ;  * = see 'destination'             4 = .TXT  Text
                    0043  ;
                    0044  ; Type 0 files: (Unspecified)
                    0045  ; The contents of these files is ignored by the Monitor.
                    0046  ;
                    0047  ; Type 1 files: (Monitor commands)
                    0048  ; These are commands that can be issued from the Monitor and require 
                    0049  ; the Monitor in order to function.
                    0050  ; These files can not be executed from other ROM banks.
                    0051  ;
                    0052  ; Type 2 files: (Executable)
                    0053  ; These are commands that can be issued by the Monitor but do no
                    0054  ; require the Monitor to function.
                    0055  ; These files can not be executed from other ROM banks.
                    0056  ; Type 2 files can also be bootable ROMs which make no use of the 
                    0057  ; Monitor.
                    0058  ;
                    0059  ; Type 3 files: (Help)
                    0060  ; These are simple text files, which are output at the end of the 
                    0061  ; Monitor own help text.
                    0062  ; Typically these are used to describe executable code (type 1 or 2)
                    0063  ; which can be run from the Monitor's command line.
                    0064  ; Help text should be kept short, being made up of one or more lines
                    0065  ; each terminated with CR (0x0D) and LF (0x0A). The end of the help
                    0066  ; text is terminated with a Null (0x00).
                    0067  ;
                    0068  ; Type 4 files: (Text)
                    0069  ; Not currently used by the Monitor
                    0070  ;
                    0071  ; The destination byte, if required, is the hi byte of the address
                    0072  ; to which the file is copied. If the 'move' flag is not set then this
                    0073  ; byte should be 0x00.
                    0074  ;
                    0075  ; The start word is the address is the absolute address in the CPU's
                    0076  ; memory map when the host ROM bank is paged in. 
                    0077  ; To allow easy testing the Monitor removes 'bank select' bits from
                    0078  ; the address. For example, an address from start of 32K ROM of 0x7000
                    0079  ; is treated as 0x3000, as this is the address it will be once paged
                    0080  ; into the CPU's memory map.
                    0081  ;
                    0082  ; The file length is only currently used for files that need to be 
                    0083  ; copied to RAM. However, all files should include this to support
                    0084  ; future uses which may rely on knowing the length of any file.
                    0085  ;
                    0086  ; Example file reference:
                    0087  ;           .DW  0xAA55         ;Identifier
                    0088  ;           .DB  "TEST    "     ;File name ("TEST.COM")
                    0089  ;           .DB  0x41           ;File type 2 = Command, moved to RAM
                    0090  ;           .DB  0xF0           ;Move code to 0xF000 to run it
                    0091  ;           .DW  CmdTest        ;Start address
                    0092  ;           .DW  CmdTestEnd-CmdTest ;Length
                    0093  
                    0094  
                    0095  ; **********************************************************************
                    0096  ; **  Constants                                                       **
                    0097  ; **********************************************************************
                    0098  
                    0099  ; Fixed address to allow external code to use this data
1AE3:               0100  kTrFileRe2:
                    0100  kTrFileRe2: .EQU 0xFFD0         ;Transient file reference
1AE3:               0101  kTrFileRef:
                    0101  kTrFileRef: .EQU 0xFFE0         ;Transient file reference
                    0102  
                    0103  
                    0104  ; **********************************************************************
                    0105  ; **  Public functions                                                **
                    0106  ; **********************************************************************
                    0107  
                    0108              .CODE
                    0109  
                    0110  ; Initialise the ROM filing system
                    0111  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0112  ;   On exit:  IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1AE3:               0113  RomInitialise:
1AE3: CD 14 1F      0114              CALL RomPageInit    ;Initialise ROM paging
1AE6: AF            0115              XOR  A
1AE7: 32 AC FE      0116              LD   (iRomMap),A    ;Clear echoed bank flags
1AEA: CD FE 1A      0117              CALL RomEcho        ;Mask out ROM banks which are echos
                    0118  ; Auto run any suitably flagged files
1AED: CD 45 1B      0119              CALL RomSearchInit
1AF0: CD 4D 1B      0120  @Loop:      CALL RomSearchNext
1AF3: C0            0121              RET  NZ
1AF4: 3A EA FF      0122              LD   A,(kTrFileRef+0x0A)
1AF7: CB 7F         0123              BIT  7,A            ;Autorun?
1AF9: C4 CA 1B      0124              CALL NZ,RomRun      ;Execute this file
1AFC: 18 F2         0125              JR   @Loop
                    0126  
                    0127  
                    0128  ; Determine which ROM banks are real and which are echos
                    0129  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0130  ;   On exit:  IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0131  ; This table shows the possible ROM banks and any echoed copies
                    0132  ;  +--------------------+-----------+-----------+-----------+-----------+
                    0133  ;  | Configuration reg  | 64k EPROM | 32k EPROM | 32k EEPRM | 16k EPROM |
                    0134  ;  | ROM Bank selection | A15=ROS1  | A15=n/a   | A15=n/a   | A15=n/a   |
                    0135  ;  |          ROS1 ROS0 | A14=ROS0  | A14=ROS0  | A14=ROS1  | A14=n/a   |
                    0136  ;  +--------------------+-----------+-----------+-----------+-----------+
                    0137  ;  | Bank 0     0    0  |  Bank 0   |  Bank 0   |  Bank 0   |  Bank 0   |
                    0138  ;  | Bank 1     0    1  |  Bank 1   |  Bank 1   |  Echo 0 - |  Echo 0 - |
                    0139  ;  | Bank 2     1    0  |  Bank 2   |  Echo 0 - |  Bank 1   |  Echo 0 - |
                    0140  ;  | Bank 3     1    1  |  Bank 3   |  Echo 1 - |  Echo 1 - |  Echo 0 - |
                    0141  ;  +--------------------+-----------+-----------+-----------+-----------+
                    0142  ; To eliminate bank echos:
                    0143  ; If bank 0 = bank 1 then bank 1 is echo and bank 3 is echo
                    0144  ; If bank 0 = bank 2 then bank 2 is echo and bank 3 is echo
1AFE: 3E 00         0145  RomEcho:    LD   A,0            ;Bank zero
1B00: 2E F0         0146              LD   L,0xF0         ;First reference in bank
1B02: CD 23 1C      0147              CALL RomGetRef      ;Read reference to RAM
1B05: 21 E0 FF      0148              LD   HL,kTrFileRef  ;Copy file reference to
1B08: 11 D0 FF      0149              LD   DE,kTrFileRe2  ;  second reference buffer
1B0B: 01 10 00      0150              LD   BC,16          ;Length of file reference
1B0E: ED B0         0151              LDIR                ;Copy (HL) to (DE) and repeat x BC
1B10: 3E 01         0152              LD   A,1            ;Bank one
1B12: 2E F0         0153              LD   L,0xF0         ;First reference in bank
1B14: CD 23 1C      0154              CALL RomGetRef      ;Read reference to RAM
1B17: CD 35 1B      0155              CALL @RomComp       ;Compare banks
1B1A: 20 05         0156              JR   NZ,@Test2      ;Not the same so skip
1B1C: 3E 0A         0157              LD   A,0b00001010   ;Banks 1 and 3 are echos
1B1E: 32 AC FE      0158              LD   (iRomMap),A    ;Store echoed bank flags
1B21: 3E 02         0159  @Test2:     LD   A,2            ;Bank two
1B23: 2E F0         0160              LD   L,0xF0         ;First reference in bank
1B25: CD 23 1C      0161              CALL RomGetRef      ;Read reference to RAM
1B28: CD 35 1B      0162              CALL @RomComp       ;Compare banks
1B2B: C0            0163              RET  NZ             ;Not the same so finished
1B2C: 3A AC FE      0164              LD   A,(iRomMap)    ;Get echoed flag bits so far
1B2F: F6 0C         0165              OR   0b00001100     ;Banks 2 and 3 are echos
1B31: 32 AC FE      0166              LD   (iRomMap),A    ;Store echoed bank flags
1B34: C9            0167              RET
                    0168  ; Compare file reference from two banks to check for ROM echo
1B35: 06 10         0169  @RomComp:   LD   B,16           ;Compare 16 bytes
1B37: 21 E0 FF      0170              LD   HL,kTrFileRef  ;First file reference
1B3A: 11 D0 FF      0171              LD   DE,kTrFileRe2  ;Second file reference
1B3D: 1A            0172  @Loop:      LD   A,(DE)         ;Compare byte from each...
1B3E: BE            0173              CP   (HL)
1B3F: C0            0174              RET  NZ             ;Not the same so return NZ flagged
1B40: 23            0175              INC  HL
1B41: 13            0176              INC  DE
1B42: 10 F9         0177              DJNZ @Loop          ;Repeat for all 16 bytes
1B44: C9            0178              RET                 ;Same so return Z flagged
                    0179  
                    0180  
                    0181  ; Initialise file reference search
                    0182  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0183  ;   On exit:  iRomBank and iRomRefLo are the 'previous' reference
                    0184  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0185  ; First call RomSearchInit to initialise a file reference search
                    0186  ; Then repeatedly call RomSearchNext to read file references
1B45:               0187  RomSearchInit:
1B45: AF            0188              XOR  A
1B46: 32 AA FE      0189              LD   (iRomBank),A   ;Start with bank zero
1B49: 32 AB FE      0190              LD   (iRomRefLo),A  ;Start with first reference
1B4C: C9            0191              RET
                    0192  
                    0193  
                    0194  ; Get next file reference from ROM
                    0195  ;   On entry: iRomBank and iRomRefLo are the previous reference
                    0196  ;   On exit:  iRomBank and iRomRefLo are the current reference
                    0197  ;             If a valid reference found Z is flagged
                    0198  ;             DE IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0199  ; First call RomSearchInit to initialise a file reference search
                    0200  ; Then repeatedly call RomSearchNext to read file references
1B4D:               0201  RomSearchNext:
1B4D: 21 AB FE      0202              LD   HL,iRomRefLo   ;Point to lo byte of ref address
1B50: 7E            0203              LD   A,(HL)         ;Get current reference address lo
1B51: D6 10         0204              SUB  16             ;Move down to next reference
1B53: 77            0205              LD   (HL),A         ;Update current reference address lo
                    0206  ; Get current file reference
1B54: 6F            0207              LD   L,A            ;Store current reference lo byte
1B55: 3A AA FE      0208              LD   A,(iRomBank)   ;Get current ROM bank
1B58: D5            0209              PUSH DE
1B59: CD 23 1C      0210              CALL RomGetRef      ;Get the file reference
1B5C: D1            0211              POP  DE
1B5D: 2A E0 FF      0212              LD   HL,(kTrFileRef)  ;Get reference identifier
1B60: 7D            0213              LD   A,L            ;For valid ref L = 0x55
1B61: AC            0214              XOR  H              ;For valid ref H = 0xAA
1B62: 3C            0215              INC  A              ;Valid file?
1B63: C8            0216              RET  Z              ;Yes, so return with Z flagged
                    0217  ; Select next ROM bank
1B64: 3A AA FE      0218  @NextBank:  LD   A,(iRomBank)   ;Get current ROM bank
1B67: 3C            0219              INC  A              ;Point to next ROM bank
1B68: 47            0220              LD   B,A            ;Remember ROM bank for later
1B69: 32 AA FE      0221              LD   (iRomBank),A   ;Store new ROM bank
1B6C: E6 01         0222              AND  A,kROMBanks    ;Finished all ROM banks?
1B6E: C0            0223              RET  NZ             ;Yes, so end with NZ flagged
1B6F: 0E 01         0224              LD   C,0x01         ;Prepare bit mask for this bank
1B71: CB 01         0225  @Rotate:    RLC  C              ;Rotate bit mask 'B' times
1B73: 10 FC         0226              DJNZ @Rotate        ;  Bit 1 set for bank 1 etc
1B75: 3A AC FE      0227              LD   A,(iRomMap)    ;Bit maps of ROM bank echos
1B78: A1            0228              AND  C              ;Test if current bank is an echo
1B79: 20 E9         0229              JR   NZ,@NextBank   ;Skip this bank if it is an echo
1B7B: AF            0230              XOR  A              ;First reference in ROM
1B7C: 32 AB FE      0231              LD   (iRomRefLo),A  ;Store new reference address
1B7F: 18 CC         0232              JR   RomSearchNext  ;Go consider next file reference
                    0233  
                    0234  
                    0235  ; Get pointer to current position in command line string
                    0236  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0237  ;   On exit:  DE = Address of string typically in command line
                    0238  ;             AF BC HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0239  ; This function is called by the API
                    0240  ; Used by monitor command files to read command parameters
1B81: ED 5B AD FE   0241  RomGetPtr:  LD   DE,(iRomTemp)  ;Get pointer to command line 
1B85: C9            0242              RET
                    0243  
                    0244  
                    0245  ; Execute file in ROM matching named pointed to be DE
                    0246  ;   On entry: DE = Address of string typically in command line
                    0247  ;   On exit:  If command executed A = 0x00 and Z flagged
                    0248  ;             IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1B86: ED 53 AD FE   0249  RomExFile:  LD   (iRomTemp),DE  ;Store start of command string
1B8A: CD 45 1B      0250              CALL RomSearchInit
1B8D: CD 4D 1B      0251  @NextFile:  CALL RomSearchNext  ;Find next file reference
1B90: C0            0252              RET  NZ             ;End of ROM files so exit
1B91: 3A EA FF      0253              LD   A,(kTrFileRef+0x0A)  ;Get type
1B94: E6 0F         0254              AND  0x0F           ;Remove flag bits
1B96: FE 01         0255              CP   1              ;Monitor command?
1B98: 28 04         0256              JR   Z,@GotFile     ;Yes, so go check name
1B9A: FE 02         0257              CP   2              ;Executable code?
1B9C: 20 EF         0258              JR   NZ,@NextFile   ;No, so go look for next file
                    0259  ; Found executable file so see if the file name is a match
1B9E: ED 5B AD FE   0260  @GotFile:   LD   DE,(iRomTemp)  ;Get start of command string
1BA2: 21 E2 FF      0261              LD   HL,kTrFileRef+2  ;Get pointer to file name
1BA5: 06 08         0262              LD   B,8            ;Maximum length
1BA7: 7E            0263  @Compare:   LD   A,(HL)         ;Character from ROM
1BA8: FE 20         0264              CP   kSpace         ;Space?
1BAA: 28 15         0265              JR   Z,@Check       ;Yes, so possible match
1BAC: CD DE 06      0266              CALL ConvertCharToUCase
1BAF: 4F            0267              LD   C,A            ;Store char from ROM in UCase
1BB0: 1A            0268              LD   A,(DE)         ;Get char from command name
1BB1: FE 21         0269              CP   kSpace+1       ;<= Space?
1BB3: 38 D8         0270              JR   C,@NextFile    ;Yes, so too short
1BB5: CD DE 06      0271              CALL ConvertCharToUCase
1BB8: B9            0272              CP   C              ;Matching character?
1BB9: 20 D2         0273              JR   NZ,@NextFile   ;No, so no match
1BBB: 23            0274              INC  HL             ;Point ot next char in ROM
1BBC: 13            0275              INC  DE             ;Point ot next char in command
1BBD: 10 E8         0276              DJNZ @Compare       ;Loop until max characters
1BBF: 18 09         0277              JR   RomRun         ;Match, so run the file
                    0278  ; Check we are also at end of command name
1BC1: 1A            0279  @Check:     LD   A,(DE)         ;Get char from command name
1BC2: FE 21         0280              CP   kSpace+1       ;<= Space?
1BC4: 30 C7         0281              JR   NC,@NextFile   ;Yes, so too long
1BC6: ED 53 AD FE   0282              LD   (iRomTemp),DE  ;Update sommand line pointer
                    0283              ;JR   RomRun        ;Match, so run the file
                    0284  ;
                    0285  ; Found file so execute it
                    0286  ; WARNING: Call here RomInitialise for autorun function
1BCA: 3A EA FF      0287  RomRun:     LD   A,(kTrFileRef+0x0A)  ;Get file type
1BCD: CB 77         0288              BIT  6,A            ;Move code first
1BCF: 20 0C         0289              JR   NZ,@Move       ;No, so go move code
                    0290  ; Execute direct from ROM
1BD1: 3A AA FE      0291              LD   A,(iRomBank)   ;Get code's ROM bank
1BD4: ED 5B EC FF   0292              LD   DE,(kTrFileRef+0x0C) ;Get start address
1BD8: CD 15 1F      0293              CALL RomExec        ;Run the executable
1BDB: AF            0294  @Return:    XOR  A              ;Executed file, so return
1BDC: C9            0295              RET                 ;  A=0 and Z flagged
                    0296  ; Move code to RAM before running it
1BDD: 2A EC FF      0297  @Move:      LD   HL,(kTrFileRef+0x0C) ;Get start address
1BE0: ED 4B EE FF   0298              LD   BC,(kTrFileRef+0x0E) ;Get length address
1BE4: 3A EB FF      0299              LD   A,(kTrFileRef+0x0B)  ;Get destination MSB
1BE7: 57            0300              LD   D,A            ;Set destination MSB
1BE8: 1E 00         0301              LD   E,0            ;Set destination LSB to zero
1BEA: 3A AA FE      0302              LD   A,(iRomBank)   ;get code's ROM bank
1BED: CD 2C 1F      0303              CALL RomCopy        ;Move code to RAM
1BF0: 3A EB FF      0304              LD   A,(kTrFileRef+0x0B)  ;Get destination MSB
1BF3: 67            0305              LD   H,A            ;Set destination MSB
1BF4: 2E 00         0306              LD   L,0            ;Set destination LSB to zero
1BF6: 01 DB 1B      0307              LD   BC,@Return     ;Get return address
1BF9: C5            0308              PUSH BC             ;Push return address onto stack
1BFA: E9            0309              JP   (HL)           ;Execute at start address
                    0310  
                    0311  
                    0312  ; Get help from current file reference
                    0313  ;   On entry: File reference must be in RAM
                    0314  ;   On exit:  DE = Pointer to start of Help string
                    0315  ;             IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1BFB: 2A EC FF      0316  RomGetHlp:  LD   HL,(kTrFileRef+0x0C) ;Get start address
1BFE: ED 4B EE FF   0317              LD   BC,(kTrFileRef+0x0E) ;Get length address
1C02: 11 80 FD      0318              LD   DE,kStrBuffer  ;Copy help to string buffer
1C05: 3A AA FE      0319              LD   A,(iRomBank)   ;get code's ROM bank
1C08: CD 2C 1F      0320              CALL RomCopy        ;Move code to RAM
1C0B: 11 80 FD      0321              LD   DE,kStrBuffer  ;Copy help to string buffer
1C0E: C9            0322              RET
                    0323  
                    0324  
                    0325  ; Get pointer to file name
                    0326  ;   On entry: File reference must be in RAM
                    0327  ;   On exit:  DE = Pointer to start of Help string
                    0328  ;             BC HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0329  ; Returns a string with length as first byte and no Null terminator
1C0F:               0330  RomGetName:
1C0F: 3E 08         0330  RomGetName: LD   A,8            ;Length of file name
1C11: 11 E1 FF      0331              LD   DE,kTrFileRef+1  ;Location in file reference
1C14: 12            0332              LD   (DE),A         ;Store length as start of name
1C15: C9            0333              RET
                    0334  
                    0335  
                    0336  ; Get file type 
                    0337  ;   On entry: File reference must be in RAM
                    0338  ;   On exit:  A = File type and flags for current file reference
                    0339  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1C16:               0340  RomGetType:
1C16: 3A EA FF      0340  RomGetType: LD   A,(kTrFileRef+10)
1C19: C9            0341              RET
                    0342  
                    0343  
                    0344  ; Get file info 
                    0345  ;   On entry: File reference must be in RAM
                    0346  ;   On exit:  DE = File's start address in the ROM
                    0347  ;             BC = File's length
                    0348  ;             AF HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1C1A:               0349  RomGetInfo:
1C1A: ED 5B EC FF   0349  RomGetInfo: LD   DE,(kTrFileRef+0x0C) ;Start address
1C1E: ED 4B EE FF   0350              LD   BC,(kTrFileRef+0x0E) ;File length
1C22: C9            0351              RET
                    0352  
                    0353  
                    0354  ; Get file reference from ROM
                    0355  ;   On entry: A = ROM bank number (0 to 3)
                    0356  ;             L = Lo byte of file reference address
                    0357  ;   On exit:  IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0358  ; A file reference is copied from ROM to a fixed location in RAM
1C23: 26 3F         0359  RomGetRef:  LD   H,kROMTop      ;Hi byte of reference address
1C25: 11 E0 FF      0360              LD   DE,kTrFileRef  ;Transient storage for reference
1C28: 01 10 00      0361              LD   BC,16          ;Length of file reference
1C2B: CD 2C 1F      0362              CALL RomCopy        ;Go copy ref from ROM to RAM
                    0363  ; Ensure start address is the absolute address in the memory map
1C2E: 3A ED FF      0364              LD   A,(kTrFileRef+0x0D)  ;Get start address hi byte
1C31: E6 3F         0365              AND  kROMTop        ;Mask with top of ROM
1C33: 32 ED FF      0366              LD   (kTrFileRef+0x0D),A  ;Store start address hi byte
1C36: C9            0367              RET
                    0368  
                    0369  
                    0370  ; **********************************************************************
                    0371  ; **  Private workspace (in RAM)                                      **
                    0372  ; **********************************************************************
                    0373  
                    0374              .DATA
                    0375  
FEAA: 00            0376  iRomBank:   .DB  0x00
FEAB: 00            0377  iRomRefLo:  .DB  0x00
FEAC: 00            0378  iRomMap:    .DB  0x00           ;One bit for each bank, 1=Echo
FEAD: 00 00         0379  iRomTemp:   .DW  0x0000
                    0380  
                    0381  ; **********************************************************************
                    0382  ; **  End of ROM Filing System module                                 **
                    0383  ; **********************************************************************
                    0384  
                    0080  #ENDIF
