                    0015  #INCLUDE    Hardware\RC2014\!Manager.asm
                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  Hardware Manager                          by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **  Hardware:  RC2014                                               **      
                    0004  ; **                   16550 (ACE) driver added by Colin C MacArthur  **
                    0005  ; **               Front Panel Board 0xFF added by Colin C MacArthur  **
                    0006  ; **********************************************************************
                    0007  
                    0008  ; This module is responsible for:
                    0009  ;   Any optional hardware detection
                    0010  ;   Setting up drivers for all hardware
                    0011  ;   Initialising hardware
                    0012  
                    0013  ; Global constants
0080:               0014  kSIO2:      .EQU 0x80           ;Base address of SIO/2 chip
0080:               0015  kACIA1:     .EQU 0x80           ;Base address of serial ACIA #1
0040:               0016  kACIA2:     .EQU 0x40           ;Base address of serial ACIA #2
0088:               0017  kACE1:      .EQU 0x88           ;Base address of serial ACE #1
0000:               0018  kPrtIn:     .EQU 0x00           ;General input port
0000:               0019  kPrtOut:    .EQU 0x00           ;General output port
                    0020  ;kPrtIn:     .EQU 0xFF           ;Front Panel Board designed for RC2014 added by Colin C MacArthur  
                    0021  ;kPrtOut:    .EQU 0xFF           ;Front Panel Board designed for RC2014
                    0022  
                    0023  ; Include device modules
                    0024  #INCLUDE    Hardware\RC2014\Serial6850.asm
                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  Device Driver                             by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **  Hardware:  RC2014                                               **
                    0004  ; **  Interface: Serial 6850 ACIA                                     **
                    0005  ; **********************************************************************
                    0006  
                    0007  ; This module is the driver for the RC2014 serial I/O interface which is
                    0008  ; based on the 6850 Asynchronous Communications Interface Adapter (ACIA)
                    0009  ;
                    0010  ; Base addresses for ACIA externally defined. eg:
                    0011  ;kACIA1:    .EQU 0x80           ;Base address of serial ACIA #1
                    0012  ;kACIA2:    .EQU 0x40           ;Base address of serial ACIA #2
                    0013  ;
                    0014  ; RC2014 addresses for 68B50 number 2:
                    0015  ; 0x40   Control registers (read and write)
                    0016  ; 0x41   Data registers (read and write)
                    0017  ;
                    0018  ; Control registers (read and write)
                    0019  ; Bit   Control write              Control read
                    0020  ;  0    Counter divide select 1    Receive data register full
                    0021  ;  1    Counter divide select 2    Transmit data register empty
                    0022  ;  2    Word select 1              Data carrier detect (/DCD) input
                    0023  ;  3    Word seelct 2              Clear to send (/CTS) input
                    0024  ;  4    Word select 3              Framing error
                    0025  ;  5    Transmit contol 1          Receiver overrun
                    0026  ;  6    Transmit control 2         Parity error
                    0027  ;  7    Receive interrupt enable   Interrupt request
                    0028  ;
                    0029  ; Control register write
                    0030  ; Bit   7   6   5   4   3   2   1   0
                    0031  ;       |   |   |   |   |   |   |   |
                    0032  ;       |   |   |   |   |   |   0   0     Clock divide 1
                    0033  ;       |   |   |   |   |   |   0   1     Clock divide 16
                    0034  ; >     |   |   |   |   |   |   1   0  >  Clock divide 64
                    0035  ;       |   |   |   |   |   |   1   1     Master reset
                    0036  ;       |   |   |   |   |   |
                    0037  ;       |   |   |   0   0   0     7 data bits, even parity, 2 stop bits
                    0038  ;       |   |   |   0   0   1     7 data bits, odd parity,  2 stop bits
                    0039  ;       |   |   |   0   1   0     7 data bits, even parity, 1 stop bit
                    0040  ;       |   |   |   0   1   1     7 data bits, odd parity,  1 stop bit
                    0041  ;       |   |   |   1   0   0     8 data bits, no parity,   2 stop bits
                    0042  ;       |   |   |   1   0   1  >  8 data bits, no parity,   1 stop bit
                    0043  ;       |   |   |   1   1   0     8 data bits, even parity, 1 stop bit
                    0044  ;       |   |   |   1   1   1     8 data bits, odd parity,  1 stop bit
                    0045  ;       |   |   |
                    0046  ;       |   0   0  >  /RTS = low (ready), tx interrupt disabled
                    0047  ;       |   0   1     /RTS = low (ready), tx interrupt enabled
                    0048  ;       |   1   0     /RTS = high (not ready), tx interrupt disabled 
                    0049  ;       |   1   1     /RTS = low, tx break, tx interrupt disabled
                    0050  ;       |
                    0051  ;       0  >  Receive interrupt disabled
                    0052  ;       1     Receive interrupt enabled
                    0053  ;
                    0054  ; Control register read
                    0055  ; Bit   7   6   5   4   3   2   1   0
                    0056  ;       |   |   |   |   |   |   |   |
                    0057  ;       |   |   |   |   |   |   |   +-------  Receive data register full
                    0058  ;       |   |   |   |   |   |   +-------  Transmit data register empty
                    0059  ;       |   |   |   |   |   +-------  Data carrier detect (/DCD)
                    0060  ;       |   |   |   |   +-------  Clear to send (/CTS)
                    0061  ;       |   |   |   +-------  Framing error
                    0062  ;       |   |   +-------  Receiver overrun 
                    0063  ;       |   +-------  Parity error
                    0064  ;       +-------  Interrupt request
                    0065  
                    0066  ; 6850 #1 registers derived from base address (above)
FEAF:               0067  kACIA1Cont:
                    0067  kACIA1Cont: .EQU kACIA1+0       ;I/O address of control register
FEAF:               0068  kACIA1Data:
                    0068  kACIA1Data: .EQU kACIA1+1       ;I/O address of data register
                    0069  ; 6850 #2 registers derived from base address (above)
FEAF:               0070  kACIA2Cont:
                    0070  kACIA2Cont: .EQU kACIA2+0       ;I/O address of control register
FEAF:               0071  kACIA2Data:
                    0071  kACIA2Data: .EQU kACIA2+1       ;I/O address of data register
                    0072  
                    0073  ; Control register values
FEAF:               0074  k6850Reset:
                    0074  k6850Reset: .EQU 0b00000011     ;Master reset
0016:               0075  k6850Init:  .EQU 0b00010110     ;No int, RTS low, 8+1, /64
                    0076  
                    0077  ; Status (control) register bit numbers
FEAF:               0078  k6850RxRdy:
                    0078  k6850RxRdy: .EQU 0              ;Receive data available bit number
FEAF:               0079  k6850TxRdy:
                    0079  k6850TxRdy: .EQU 1              ;Transmit data empty bit number
                    0080  
                    0081  ; Device detection, test 1
                    0082  ; This test just reads from the devices' status (control) register
                    0083  ; and looks for register bits in known states:
                    0084  ; /CTS input bit = low
                    0085  ; /DCD input bit = low
                    0086  ; WARNING
                    0087  ; Sometimes at power up the Tx data reg empty bit is zero, but
                    0088  ; recovers after device initialised. So test 1 excludes this bit.
FEAF:               0089  k6850Mask1:
                    0089  k6850Mask1: .EQU  0b00001100    ;Mask for known bits in control reg
FEAF:               0090  k6850Test1:
                    0090  k6850Test1: .EQU  0b00000000    ;Test value following masking
                    0091  
                    0092  ; Device detection, test 2
                    0093  ; This test just reads from the devices' status (control) register
                    0094  ; and looks for register bits in known states:
                    0095  ; /CTS input bit = low
                    0096  ; /DCD input bit = low
                    0097  ; Transmit data register empty bit = high
FEAF:               0098  k6850Mask2:
                    0098  k6850Mask2: .EQU  0b00001110    ;Mask for known bits in control reg
FEAF:               0099  k6850Test2:
                    0099  k6850Test2: .EQU  0b00000010    ;Test value following masking
                    0100  
                    0101  
                    0102              .CODE
                    0103  
                    0104  
                    0105  ; RC2014 serial 6850 initialise
                    0106  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0107  ;   On exit:  Z flagged if device is found and initialised
                    0108  ;             AF BC DE HL not specified
                    0109  ;             IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0110  ; If the device is found it is initialised
1C37:               0111  RC2014_SerialACIA1_Initialise:
                    0112  ; First look to see if the device is present
                    0113  ; Test 1, just read from chip, do not write anything
1C37: DB 80         0114              IN   A,(kACIA1Cont) ;Read status (control) register
1C39: E6 0C         0115              AND  k6850Mask1     ;Mask for known bits in control reg
1C3B: FE 00         0116              CP   k6850Test1     ;and check for known values
1C3D: C0            0117              RET  NZ             ;If not found return with NZ flag
                    0118  ; Attempt to initialise the chip
1C3E: 3E 03         0119              LD   A,k6850Reset   ;Master reset
1C40: D3 80         0120              OUT  (kACIA1Cont),A ;Write to ACIA control register
1C42: 3E 16         0121              LD   A,k6850Init    ;No int, RTS low, 8+1, /64
1C44: D3 80         0122              OUT  (kACIA1Cont),A ;Write to ACIA control register
                    0123  ; Test 2, perform tests on chip following initialisation
1C46: DB 80         0124              IN   A,(kACIA1Cont) ;Read status (control) register
1C48: E6 0E         0125              AND  k6850Mask2     ;Mask for known bits in control reg
1C4A: FE 02         0126              CP   k6850Test2     ;Test value following masking
                    0127  ;           RET  NZ             ;Return not found NZ flagged
1C4C: C9            0128              RET                 ;Return Z if found, NZ if not
                    0129  
1C4D:               0130  RC2014_SerialACIA2_Initialise:
                    0131  ; First look to see if the device is present
                    0132  ; Test 1, just read from chip, do not write anything
1C4D: DB 40         0133              IN   A,(kACIA2Cont) ;Read status (control) register
1C4F: E6 0C         0134              AND  k6850Mask1     ;Mask for known bits in control reg
1C51: FE 00         0135              CP   k6850Test1     ;and check for known values
1C53: C0            0136              RET  NZ             ;If not found return with NZ flag
                    0137  ; Attempt to initialise the chip
1C54: 3E 03         0138              LD   A,k6850Reset   ;Master reset
1C56: D3 40         0139              OUT  (kACIA2Cont),A ;Write to ACIA control register
1C58: 3E 16         0140              LD   A,k6850Init    ;No int, RTS low, 8+1, /64
1C5A: D3 40         0141              OUT  (kACIA2Cont),A ;Write to ACIA control register
                    0142  ; Test 2, perform tests on chip following initialisation
1C5C: DB 40         0143              IN   A,(kACIA2Cont) ;Read status (control) register
1C5E: E6 0E         0144              AND  k6850Mask2     ;Mask for known bits in control reg
1C60: FE 02         0145              CP   k6850Test2     ;Test value following masking
                    0146  ;           RET  NZ             ;Return not found NZ flagged
1C62: C9            0147              RET                 ;Return Z if found, NZ if not
                    0148  
                    0149  
                    0150  ; RC2014 serial 6850 input character
                    0151  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0152  ;   On exit:  A = Character input from the device
                    0153  ;             NZ flagged if character input
                    0154  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0155  ; This function does not return until a character is available
1C63:               0156  RC2014_SerialACIA1_InputChar:
1C63: DB 80         0157              IN   A,(kACIA1Cont) ;Address of status register
1C65: CB 47         0158              BIT  k6850RxRdy,A   ;Receive byte available
1C67: C8            0159              RET  Z              ;Return Z if no character
1C68: DB 81         0160              IN   A,(kACIA1Data) ;Read data byte
1C6A: C9            0161              RET                 ;NZ flagged if character input
                    0162  
1C6B:               0163  RC2014_SerialACIA2_InputChar:
1C6B: DB 40         0164              IN   A,(kACIA2Cont) ;Address of status register
1C6D: CB 47         0165              BIT  k6850RxRdy,A   ;Receive byte available
1C6F: C8            0166              RET  Z              ;Return Z if no character
1C70: DB 41         0167              IN   A,(kACIA2Data) ;Read data byte
1C72: C9            0168              RET                 ;NZ flagged if character input
                    0169  
                    0170  
                    0171  ; RC2014 serial 6850 output character
                    0172  ;   On entry: A = Character to be output to the device
                    0173  ;   On exit:  If character output successful (eg. device was ready)
                    0174  ;               NZ flagged and A != 0
                    0175  ;             If character output failed (eg. device busy)
                    0176  ;               Z flagged and A = Character to output
                    0177  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1C73:               0178  RC2014_SerialACIA1_OutputChar:
1C73: C5            0179              PUSH BC
1C74: 0E 80         0180              LD   C,kACIA1Cont   ;ACIA control register
1C76: ED 40         0181              IN   B,(C)          ;Read ACIA control register
1C78: CB 48         0182              BIT  k6850TxRdy,B   ;Transmit register full?
1C7A: C1            0183              POP  BC
1C7B: C8            0184              RET  Z              ;Return Z as character not output
1C7C: D3 81         0185              OUT  (kACIA1Data),A ;Write data byte
1C7E: F6 FF         0186              OR   0xFF           ;Return success A=0xFF and NZ flagged
1C80: C9            0187              RET
                    0188  
1C81:               0189  RC2014_SerialACIA2_OutputChar:
1C81: C5            0190              PUSH BC
1C82: 0E 40         0191              LD   C,kACIA2Cont   ;ACIA control register
1C84: ED 40         0192              IN   B,(C)          ;Read ACIA control register
1C86: CB 48         0193              BIT  k6850TxRdy,B   ;Transmit register full?
1C88: C1            0194              POP  BC
1C89: C8            0195              RET  Z              ;Return Z as character not output
1C8A: D3 41         0196              OUT  (kACIA2Data),A ;Write data byte
1C8C: F6 FF         0197              OR   0xFF           ;Return success A=0xFF and NZ flagged
1C8E: C9            0198              RET
                    0199  
                    0200  
                    0201  ; **********************************************************************
                    0202  ; **  End of driver: RC2014, Serial 6850 ACIA                         **
                    0203  ; **********************************************************************
                    0204  
                    0205  
                    0206  
                    0207  
                    0208  
                    0025  #INCLUDE    Hardware\RC2014\SerialSIO2.asm
                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  Device Driver                             by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **  Hardware:  RC2014                                               **
                    0004  ; **  Interface: Serial SIO/2                                         **
                    0005  ; **********************************************************************
                    0006  
                    0007  ; This module is the driver for Z80 SIO/2 serial modules. It supports
                    0008  ; both Grant's original and Spencer's official addresing schemes.
                    0009  ;
                    0010  ; WARNING: the official SIO/2 module is not compatible with Grant
                    0011  ; Searle's original design (later used by Dr. S Baker's module).
                    0012  ; Addresses used:
                    0013  ; Original: SIOA-C=0x82, SIOA-D=0x80, SIOB-C=0x83, SIOB-D=0x81
                    0014  ; Official: SIOA-C=0x80, SIOA-D=0x81, SIOB-C=0x82, SIOB-D=0x83
                    0015  ; Address signals:
                    0016  ; Original: SIO C/D line = A1,  SIO B/A line = A0
                    0017  ; Official: SIO C/D line = /A0, SIO B/A line = A1
                    0018  ;
                    0019  ; RC2014 standard addresses for Grant's original SIO/2: (type 1)
                    0020  ; 0x82   Channel A control registers (read and write)
                    0021  ; 0x80   Channel A data registers (read and write)
                    0022  ; 0x83   Channel B control registers (read and write)
                    0023  ; 0x81   Channel B data registers (read and write)
                    0024  ;
                    0025  ; RC2014 standard addresses for Spencer's official SIO/2: (type 2)
                    0026  ; 0x80   Channel A control registers (read and write)
                    0027  ; 0x81   Channel A data registers (read and write)
                    0028  ; 0x82   Channel B control registers (read and write)
                    0029  ; 0x83   Channel B data registers (read and write)
                    0030  ;
                    0031  ; Too complex to reproduce technical info here. See SIO datasheet
                    0032  
                    0033  ; Base address for SIO externally defined. eg:
                    0034  ;kSIO2:     .EQU 0x80           ;Base address of SIO/2 chip
                    0035  
                    0036  ; SIO/2 type 1 registers derived from base address (above)
1C8F:               0037  kSIOAConT1:
                    0037  kSIOAConT1: .EQU kSIO2+2        ;I/O address of control register A
1C8F:               0038  kSIOADatT1:
                    0038  kSIOADatT1: .EQU kSIO2+0        ;I/O address of data register A
1C8F:               0039  kSIOBConT1:
                    0039  kSIOBConT1: .EQU kSIO2+3        ;I/O address of control register B
1C8F:               0040  kSIOBDatT1:
                    0040  kSIOBDatT1: .EQU kSIO2+1        ;I/O address of data register B
                    0041  ;
                    0042  ; SIO/2 type 2 registers derived from base address (above)
1C8F:               0043  kSIOAConT2:
                    0043  kSIOAConT2: .EQU kSIO2+0        ;I/O address of control register A
1C8F:               0044  kSIOADatT2:
                    0044  kSIOADatT2: .EQU kSIO2+1        ;I/O address of data register A
1C8F:               0045  kSIOBConT2:
                    0045  kSIOBConT2: .EQU kSIO2+2        ;I/O address of control register B
1C8F:               0046  kSIOBDatT2:
                    0046  kSIOBDatT2: .EQU kSIO2+3        ;I/O address of data register B
                    0047  
                    0048  ; Status (control) register bit numbers
0000:               0049  kSIORxRdy:  .EQU 0              ;Receive data available bit number
0002:               0050  kSIOTxRdy:  .EQU 2              ;Transmit data empty bit number
                    0051  
                    0052  ; Device detection, test 1
                    0053  ; This test just reads from the devices' status (control) register
                    0054  ; and looks for register bits in known states:
                    0055  ; CTS input bit = high
                    0056  ; DCD input bit = high
                    0057  ; Transmit data register empty bit = high
002C:               0058  kSIOMask1:  .EQU  0b00101100    ;Mask for known bits in control reg
002C:               0059  kSIOTest1:  .EQU  0b00101100    ;Test value following masking
                    0060  
                    0061  
                    0062              .CODE
                    0063  
                    0064  ; **********************************************************************
                    0065  ; **  Type 1 (Grant's original addressing scheme)                     **
                    0066  ; **********************************************************************
                    0067  
                    0068  
                    0069  ; RC2014 type 1 serial SIO/2 initialise
                    0070  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0071  ;   On exit:  Z flagged if device is found and initialised
                    0072  ;             AF BC DE HL not specified
                    0073  ;             IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0074  ; If the device is found it is initialised
1C8F:               0075  RC2014_SerialSIO2_Initialise_T1:
                    0076  ; First look to see if the device is present
1C8F: DB 82         0077              IN   A,(kSIOAConT1) ;Read status (control) register A
1C91: E6 2C         0078              AND  kSIOMask1      ;Mask for known bits in control reg
1C93: FE 2C         0079              CP   kSIOTest1      ;Test value following masking
1C95: C0            0080              RET  NZ             ;Return not found NZ flagged
1C96: DB 83         0081              IN   A,(kSIOBConT1) ;Read status (control) register B
1C98: E6 2C         0082              AND  kSIOMask1      ;Mask for known bits in control reg
1C9A: FE 2C         0083              CP   kSIOTest1      ;Test value following masking
1C9C: C0            0084              RET  NZ             ;Return not found NZ flagged
                    0085  ; Device present, so initialise A
1C9D: 0E 82         0086              LD   C,kSIOAConT1   ;SIO/2 channel A control port
1C9F: CD 17 1D      0087              CALL RC2014_SerialSIO2_IniSend
1CA2: 0E 83         0088              LD   C,kSIOBConT1   ;SIO/2 channel B control port
1CA4: C3 17 1D      0089              JP   RC2014_SerialSIO2_IniSend
                    0090  
                    0091  
                    0092  ; RC2014 type 1 serial SIO/2 channel A & B input character
                    0093  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0094  ;   On exit:  A = Character input from the device
                    0095  ;             NZ flagged if character input
                    0096  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1CA7:               0097  RC2014_SerialSIO2A_InputChar_T1:
1CA7: DB 82         0098              IN   A,(kSIOAConT1) ;Address of status register
1CA9: CB 47         0099              BIT  kSIORxRdy,A    ;Receive byte available
1CAB: C8            0100              RET  Z              ;Return Z if no character
1CAC: DB 80         0101              IN   A,(kSIOADatT1) ;Read data byte
1CAE: C9            0102              RET
1CAF:               0103  RC2014_SerialSIO2B_InputChar_T1:
1CAF: DB 83         0104              IN   A,(kSIOBConT1) ;Address of status register
1CB1: CB 47         0105              BIT  kSIORxRdy,A    ;Receive byte available
1CB3: C8            0106              RET  Z              ;Return Z if no character
1CB4: DB 81         0107              IN   A,(kSIOBDatT1) ;Read data byte
1CB6: C9            0108              RET
                    0109  
                    0110  
                    0111  ; RC2014 type 1 serial SIO/2 channel A & B output character
                    0112  ;   On entry: A = Character to be output to the device
                    0113  ;   On exit:  If character output successful (eg. device was ready)
                    0114  ;               NZ flagged and A != 0
                    0115  ;             If character output failed (eg. device busy)
                    0116  ;               Z flagged and A = Character to output
                    0117  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1CB7:               0118  RC2014_SerialSIO2A_OutputChar_T1:
1CB7: C5            0119              PUSH BC
1CB8: 0E 82         0120              LD   C,kSIOAConT1   ;SIO control register
1CBA: ED 40         0121              IN   B,(C)          ;Read SIO control register
1CBC: CB 50         0122              BIT  kSIOTxRdy,B    ;Transmit register full?
1CBE: C1            0123              POP  BC
1CBF: C8            0124              RET  Z              ;Return Z as character not output
1CC0: D3 80         0125              OUT  (kSIOADatT1),A ;Write data byte
1CC2: F6 FF         0126              OR   0xFF           ;Return success A=0xFF and NZ flagged
1CC4: C9            0127              RET
1CC5:               0128  RC2014_SerialSIO2B_OutputChar_T1:
1CC5: C5            0129              PUSH BC
1CC6: 0E 83         0130              LD   C,kSIOBConT1   ;SIO control register
1CC8: ED 40         0131              IN   B,(C)          ;Read SIO control register
1CCA: CB 50         0132              BIT  kSIOTxRdy,B    ;Transmit register full?
1CCC: C1            0133              POP  BC
1CCD: C8            0134              RET  Z              ;Return Z as character not output
1CCE: D3 81         0135              OUT  (kSIOBDatT1),A ;Write data byte
1CD0: F6 FF         0136              OR   0xFF           ;Return success A=0xFF and NZ flagged
1CD2: C9            0137              RET
                    0138  
                    0139  
                    0140  ; **********************************************************************
                    0141  ; **  Type 2 (Spencer's original addressing scheme)                   **
                    0142  ; **********************************************************************
                    0143  
                    0144  ; RC2014 type 2 serial SIO/2 initialise
                    0145  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0146  ;   On exit:  Z flagged if device is found and initialised
                    0147  ;             AF BC DE HL not specified
                    0148  ;             IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0149  ; If the device is found it is initialised
1CD3:               0150  RC2014_SerialSIO2_Initialise_T2:
                    0151  ; First look to see if the device is present
1CD3: DB 80         0152              IN   A,(kSIOAConT2) ;Read status (control) register A
1CD5: E6 2C         0153              AND  kSIOMask1      ;Mask for known bits in control reg
1CD7: FE 2C         0154              CP   kSIOTest1      ;Test value following masking
1CD9: C0            0155              RET  NZ             ;Return not found NZ flagged
1CDA: DB 82         0156              IN   A,(kSIOBConT2) ;Read status (control) register B
1CDC: E6 2C         0157              AND  kSIOMask1      ;Mask for known bits in control reg
1CDE: FE 2C         0158              CP   kSIOTest1      ;Test value following masking
1CE0: C0            0159              RET  NZ             ;Return not found NZ flagged
                    0160  ; Device present, so initialise 
1CE1: 0E 80         0161              LD   C,kSIOAConT2   ;SIO/2 channel A control port
1CE3: CD 17 1D      0162              CALL RC2014_SerialSIO2_IniSend
1CE6: 0E 82         0163              LD   C,kSIOBConT2   ;SIO/2 channel B control port
1CE8: C3 17 1D      0164              JP   RC2014_SerialSIO2_IniSend
                    0165  
                    0166  
                    0167  ; RC2014 type 2 serial SIO/2 channel A & B input character
                    0168  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0169  ;   On exit:  A = Character input from the device
                    0170  ;             NZ flagged if character input
                    0171  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1CEB:               0172  RC2014_SerialSIO2A_InputChar_T2:
1CEB: DB 80         0173              IN   A,(kSIOAConT2) ;Address of status register
1CED: CB 47         0174              BIT  kSIORxRdy,A    ;Receive byte available
1CEF: C8            0175              RET  Z              ;Return Z if no character
1CF0: DB 81         0176              IN   A,(kSIOADatT2) ;Read data byte
1CF2: C9            0177              RET
1CF3:               0178  RC2014_SerialSIO2B_InputChar_T2:
1CF3: DB 82         0179              IN   A,(kSIOBConT2) ;Address of status register
1CF5: CB 47         0180              BIT  kSIORxRdy,A    ;Receive byte available
1CF7: C8            0181              RET  Z              ;Return Z if no character
1CF8: DB 83         0182              IN   A,(kSIOBDatT2) ;Read data byte
1CFA: C9            0183              RET
                    0184  
                    0185  
                    0186  ; RC2014 type 2 serial SIO/2 channel A & B output character
                    0187  ;   On entry: A = Character to be output to the device
                    0188  ;   On exit:  If character output successful (eg. device was ready)
                    0189  ;               NZ flagged and A != 0
                    0190  ;             If character output failed (eg. device busy)
                    0191  ;               Z flagged and A = Character to output
                    0192  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1CFB:               0193  RC2014_SerialSIO2A_OutputChar_T2:
1CFB: C5            0194              PUSH BC
1CFC: 0E 80         0195              LD   C,kSIOAConT2   ;SIO control register
1CFE: ED 40         0196              IN   B,(C)          ;Read SIO control register
1D00: CB 50         0197              BIT  kSIOTxRdy,B    ;Transmit register full?
1D02: C1            0198              POP  BC
1D03: C8            0199              RET  Z              ;Return Z as character not output
1D04: D3 81         0200              OUT  (kSIOADatT2),A ;Write data byte
1D06: F6 FF         0201              OR   0xFF           ;Return success A=0xFF and NZ flagged
1D08: C9            0202              RET
1D09:               0203  RC2014_SerialSIO2B_OutputChar_T2:
1D09: C5            0204              PUSH BC
1D0A: 0E 82         0205              LD   C,kSIOBConT2   ;SIO control register
1D0C: ED 40         0206              IN   B,(C)          ;Read SIO control register
1D0E: CB 50         0207              BIT  kSIOTxRdy,B    ;Transmit register full?
1D10: C1            0208              POP  BC
1D11: C8            0209              RET  Z              ;Return Z as character not output
1D12: D3 83         0210              OUT  (kSIOBDatT2),A ;Write data byte
1D14: F6 FF         0211              OR   0xFF           ;Return success A=0xFF and NZ flagged
1D16: C9            0212              RET
                    0213  
                    0214  
                    0215  ; **********************************************************************
                    0216  ; **  Private functions                                               **
                    0217  ; **********************************************************************
                    0218  
                    0219  
                    0220  ; RC2014 serial SIO/2 write initialisation data 
                    0221  ;   On entry: C = Address of SIO control register
                    0222  ;   On exit:  DE IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0223  ; Send initialisation data to specied control register
1D17:               0224  RC2014_SerialSIO2_IniSend:
1D17: 21 20 1D      0225              LD   HL,@SIOIni     ;Point to initialisation data
1D1A: 06 09         0226              LD   B,@SIOIniEnd-@SIOIni ;Length of ini data
1D1C: ED B3         0227              OTIR                ;Write data to output port C
1D1E: AF            0228              XOR  A              ;Return Z flag as device found
1D1F: C9            0229              RET
                    0230  ; SIO channel initialisation data
1D20: 18            0231  @SIOIni:    .DB  0b00011000     ; Wr0 Channel reset
                    0232  ;           .DB  0b00000010     ; Wr0 Pointer R2
                    0233  ;           .DB  0x00           ; Wr2 Int vector
1D21: 14            0234              .DB  0b00010100     ; Wr0 Pointer R4 + reset ex st int
1D22: C4            0235              .DB  0b11000100     ; Wr4 /64, async mode, no parity
1D23: 03            0236              .DB  0b00000011     ; Wr0 Pointer R3
1D24: C1            0237              .DB  0b11000001     ; Wr3 Receive enable, 8 bit 
1D25: 05            0238              .DB  0b00000101     ; Wr0 Pointer R5
                    0239  ;           .DB  0b01101000     ; Wr5 Transmit enable, 8 bit 
1D26: EA            0240              .DB  0b11101010     ; Wr5 Transmit enable, 8 bit, flow ctrl
1D27: 11            0241              .DB  0b00010001     ; Wr0 Pointer R1 + reset ex st int
1D28: 00            0242              .DB  0b00000000     ; Wr1 No Tx interrupts
1D29:               0243  @SIOIniEnd:
                    0244  
                    0245  
                    0246  ; **********************************************************************
                    0247  ; **  End of driver: RC2014, Serial SIO/2                             **
                    0248  ; **********************************************************************

                    0026  #INCLUDE    Hardware\RC2014\Serial16550.asm
                    0001  ; **********************************************************************
                    0002  ; **  Device Driver                             by Stephen C Cousins  **
                    0003  ; **  Hardware:  RC2014            16550 driver by Colin C MacArthur  **
                    0004  ; **  Interface: Serial 16550 ACE                                     **
                    0005  ; **********************************************************************
                    0006  
                    0007  ; This module is the driver for the RC2014 serial I/O interface which is
                    0008  ; based on the 16550 Asynchronous Communications Element (ACE)
                    0009  ;
                    0010  ; Base addresses for ACE externally defined. eg:
                    0011  ;kACE1:     .EQU 0xC0           ;Base address of serial ACE #1
                    0012  ;
                    0013  ; DLAB (Line Control Register Bit 7 = 1)
                    0014  ; 0xC0   Data registers (read and write)
                    0015  ; 0xC1   Interrupt Enable Register Control register (read and write)
                    0016  ;
                    0017  ; DLAB (Line Control Register Bit 7 = 0)
                    0018  ; 0xC0   Data registers (read and write)
                    0019  ; 0xC1   Interrupt Enable Register Control register (read and write)
                    0020  ; 0xC2   FIFO Control     Register Control register (write)
                    0021  ; 0xC2   Interrupt Ident. Register Control register (read)
                    0022  ; 0xC3   Line Control     Register (read and write)
                    0023  ; 0xC4   Modem Control    Register (read and write)
                    0024  ; 0xC5   Line Status      Register (read)
                    0025  ; 0xC6   Modem Status     Register (read)
                    0026  ; 0xC7   Scratch          Register (read and write)
                    0027  ;
                    0028  ;
                    0029  ;kACE1:    .EQU 0xC0           ; Base address of serial ACE #1
0088:               0030  kACE1_R0   .EQU     kACE1 + 0  ; Data             Register (read and write)
0089:               0031  kACE1_R1   .EQU     kACE1 + 1  ; Interrupt Enable Register Control register (read and write)
008A:               0032  kACE1_R2   .EQU     kACE1 + 2  ; FIFO Control     Register Control register (write)
                    0033  ;                              ; Interrupt Ident. Register Control register (read)
008B:               0034  kACE1_R3   .EQU     kACE1 + 3  ; Line Control     Register (read and write)
008C:               0035  kACE1_R4   .EQU     kACE1 + 4  ; Modem Control    Register (read and write)
008D:               0036  kACE1_R5   .EQU     kACE1 + 5  ; Line Status      Register (read)
008E:               0037  kACE1_R6   .EQU     kACE1 + 6  ; Modem Status     Register (read)
008F:               0038  kACE1_R7   .EQU     kACE1 + 7  ; Scratch          Register (read and write)
                    0039  
                    0040  ; Status (control) register bit numbers
0000:               0041  kACERxRdy:  .EQU 0              ; Receive data available bit number
0006:               0042  kACETxRdy:  .EQU 6              ; Transmit data empty bit number
                    0043  
                    0044              .CODE
                    0045  
                    0046  ; RC2014 serial ACE initialise
                    0047  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0048  ;   On exit:  Z flagged if device is found and initialised
                    0049  ;             AF BC DE HL not specified
                    0050  ;             IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0051  ; If the device is found it is initialised
                    0052  ; First look to see if the device is present
                    0053  ; SEE IF UART IS THERE BY CHECKING DLAB FUNCTIONALITY
1D29:               0054  RC2014_SerialACE1_Initialise:
1D29: 3E 80         0055              LD	A,080H          ;DLAB BIT ON
1D2B: D3 8B         0056              OUT (kACE1_R3),A    ;OUTPUT TO LCR (DLAB REGS NOW ACTIVE)
1D2D: 3E 04         0057              LD	A,04H           ;DIVISOR_LOW
1D2F: D3 88         0058              OUT (kACE1_R0),A    ;OUTPUT TO DLM
1D31: 3E 00         0059              LD	A,00H           ;DIVISOR_HIGH
1D33: D3 89         0060              OUT (kACE1_R1),A    ;OUTPUT DIVISOR_HIGH
1D35: DB 88         0061              IN  A,(kACE1_R0)    ;READ IT BACK
1D37: FE 04         0062              CP  04H			;CHECK VALUE
1D39: C0            0063              RET  NZ             ;Return not found NZ flagged Device NOT found
1D3A: 3E 03         0064              LD	A,03H           ;DIVISOR LOAD DISABLE Bit 7=0, 8 bits, No Parity, 1 stopbit
1D3C: D3 8B         0065              OUT (kACE1_R3),A    ;OUTPUT TO LCR (DLAB REGS NOW INACTIVE)
1D3E: 3E 02         0066              LD  A,02H           ; Enable RTS 
1D40: D3 8C         0067              OUT (kACE1_R4),A    ; Modem Control    Register (read and write)
1D42: 3E 87         0068              LD  A,087H          ; 06-no FIFO, 07-enable FIFO, bits 6,7 - fifo trigger 1(00) or 4(01) or 8(10) or 16(11)
1D44: D3 8A         0069              OUT (kACE1_R2),A    ; IFO Control     Register Control register (write)
1D46: 3E 00         0070              LD  A,00H           ; xxx1B=RXBUF int, xx1xB=TXBUF int, x1xxB=RXCHR int, 00H=POLLING
1D48: D3 89         0071              OUT (kACE1_R1),A    ; Interrupt Enable Register Control register (read and write)
1D4A: AF            0072              XOR  A              ; Return success A=0 and Z flagged
1D4B: C9            0073              RET
                    0074  
                    0075  ; RC2014 serial ACE #1 input character
                    0076  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0077  ;   On exit:  A = Character input from the device
                    0078  ;             NZ flagged if character input
                    0079  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1D4C:               0080  RC2014_SerialACE1_InputChar:
1D4C: DB 8D         0081              IN   A,(kACE1_R5)   ; Line Status      Register (read)
1D4E: CB 47         0082              BIT  kACERxRdy,A    ; Receive byte available
1D50: C8            0083              RET  Z              ; Return Z if no character
1D51: DB 88         0084              IN   A,(kACE1_R0)   ; Read data byte
1D53: C9            0085              RET
                    0086  
                    0087  
                    0088  ; RC2014 serial ACE #1 output character
                    0089  ;   On entry: A = Character to be output to the device
                    0090  ;   On exit:  If character output successful (eg. device was ready)
                    0091  ;               NZ flagged and A != 0
                    0092  ;             If character output failed (eg. device busy)
                    0093  ;               Z flagged and A = Character to output
                    0094  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1D54:               0095  RC2014_SerialACE1_OutputChar:
1D54: C5            0096              PUSH BC
1D55: 0E 8D         0097              LD   C,kACE1_R5     ; Line Status      Register (read)
1D57: ED 40         0098              IN   B,(C)          ;Read SIO control register
1D59: CB 70         0099              BIT  kACETxRdy,B    ;Transmit register full?
1D5B: C1            0100              POP  BC
1D5C: C8            0101              RET  Z              ;Return Z as character not output
1D5D: D3 88         0102              OUT  (kACE1_R0),A   ;Write data byte
1D5F: F6 FF         0103              OR   0xFF           ;Return success A=0xFF and NZ flagged
1D61: C9            0104              RET
                    0105  
                    0106  ; Hardware: Set baud rate
                    0107  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0108  ;   On entry: A = Baud rate code
                    0109  ;             C = Console device number (1 to 6)
                    0110  ;   On exit:  IF successful: (ie. valid device and baud code)
                    0111  ;               A != 0 and NZ flagged
                    0112  ;             BC HL not specified
                    0113  ;             DE? IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0114  ; A test is made for valid a device number and baud code.
                    0115  ;  +----------+--------------+---------------+---------------+
                    0116  ;  |  Serial  |   Baud rate  |    DIVISOR    |    DIVISOR    |
                    0117  ;  |    Baud  |        code  |     HIGH      |      LOW      |
                    0118  ;  +----------+--------------+---------------+---------------+
                    0119  ;  |  230400  |   1 or 0x23  |           00  |           02  |
                    0120  ;  |  115200  |   2 or 0x11  |           00  |           04  |
                    0121  ;  |   57600  |   3 or 0x57  |           00  |           08  |
                    0122  ;  |   38400  |   4 or 0x38  |           00  |           0C  |
                    0123  ;  |   19200  |   5 or 0x19  |           00  |           18  |
                    0124  ;  |   14400  |   6 or 0x14  |           00  |           20  |
                    0125  ;  |    9600  |   7 or 0x96  |           00  |           30  |
                    0126  ;  |    4800  |   8 or 0x48  |           00  |           60  |
                    0127  ;  |    2400  |   9 or 0x24  |           00  |           C0  |
                    0128  ;  |    1200  |  10 or 0x12  |           01  |           80  |
                    0129  ;  |     600  |  11 or 0x60  |           03  |           00  |
                    0130  ;  |     300  |  12 or 0x30  |           06  |           00  |
                    0131  ;  +----------+--------------+---------------+---------------+
1D62:               0132  RC2014_SerialACE1_Hardware_BaudSet:
                    0133  ; Search for baud rate in table
                    0134  ; A = Baud rate code  (not verified)
                    0135  ; C = Console device number (1 to 6)  (not verified)
1D62: 21 83 1D      0136              LD   HL,Hardware_BaudTable
1D65: 06 0C         0137              LD   B,12           ;Number of table entries
1D67: BE            0138  @Search:    CP   (HL)           ;Record for required baud rate?
1D68: 23            0139              INC  HL             ;point to DIVISOR_HIGH
1D69: 28 06         0140              JR   Z,@Found       ;Yes, so Set Baud Rate
1D6B: 23            0141              INC  HL             ;point to DIVISOR_LOW
1D6C: 23            0142              INC  HL             ;Point to next record
1D6D: 10 F8         0143              DJNZ @Search        ;Repeat until end of table
1D6F: AF            0144  @Failed:    XOR  A              ;Return failure (A=0 and Z flagged)
1D70: C9            0145              RET                 ;Abort as invalid baud rate
                    0146  ; Found location in table
                    0147  ; B = Baud code (1 to 11)  (verified)
                    0148  ; C = Console device number (1 to 6)  (not verified)
                    0149  ; (HL)   = Divisor High  (verified) 
                    0150  ; (HL)+1 = Divisor Low   (verified)
0000:               0151  @Found:
1D71: 3E 80         0152              LD	A,080H          ;DLAB BIT ON
1D73: D3 8B         0153              OUT (kACE1_R3),A    ;OUTPUT TO LCR (DLAB REGS NOW ACTIVE)
1D75: 7E            0154              LD	A,(HL)          ;DIVISOR_HIGH FROM Hardware_BaudTable
1D76: D3 89         0155              OUT (kACE1_R1),A    ;OUTPUT DIVISOR_HIGH
1D78: 23            0156              INC HL              ; POINT TO DIVISOR_LOW
1D79: 7E            0157              LD	A,(HL)          ;DIVISOR_LOW FROM Hardware_BaudTable
1D7A: D3 88         0158              OUT (kACE1_R0),A    ;OUTPUT TO DLM
1D7C: 3E 03         0159              LD	A,03H           ;DIVISOR LOAD DISABLE Bit 7=0, 8 bits, No Parity, 1 stopbit
1D7E: D3 8B         0160              OUT (kACE1_R3),A    ;OUTPUT TO LCR (DLAB REGS NOW INACTIVE)
1D80: F6 FF         0161              OR   0xFF           ;Return success (A=0xFF and NZ flagged)
1D82: C9            0162              RET
                    0163  ;
                    0164  ; 16550 (ACE) Baud DIVISOR HIGH / LOW FOR 7.3734 MHz Clock
                    0165  ; Baud rate table 
                    0166  ; Position in table matches value of short baud rate code (1 to 11)
                    0167  ; First column in the table is the long baud rate code
                    0168  ; Second column is the Divisor (16 bit)
                    0169  ;Hardware_BaudTable:
                    0170  ;            .DB  0x30,0x06,0x00     ;12 =    300 baud
                    0171  ;            .DB  0x60,0x03,0x00     ;11 =    600 baud
                    0172  ;            .DB  0x12,0x01,0x80     ;10 =   1200 baud
                    0173  ;            .DB  0x24,0x00,0xC0     ; 9 =   2400 baud
                    0174  ;            .DB  0x48,0x00,0x60     ; 8 =   4800 baud
                    0175  ;            .DB  0x96,0x00,0x30     ; 7 =   9600 baud
                    0176  ;            .DB  0x14,0x00,0x20     ; 6 =  14400 baud
                    0177  ;            .DB  0x19,0x00,0x18     ; 5 =  19200 baud
                    0178  ;            .DB  0x38,0x00,0x0C     ; 4 =  38400 baud
                    0179  ;            .DB  0x57,0x00,0x08     ; 3 =  57600 baud
                    0180  ;            .DB  0x11,0x00,0x04     ; 2 = 115200 baud
                    0181  ;            .DB  0x23,0x00,0x02     ; 1 = 230400 baud
                    0182  
                    0183  ;
                    0184  ; 16550 (ACE) Baud DIVISOR HIGH / LOW FOR 1,8432 MHz Clock
1D83:               0185  Hardware_BaudTable:
1D83: 30 01 80      0186              .DB  0x30,0x01,0x80     ;12 =    300 baud
1D86: 60 00 C0      0187              .DB  0x60,0x00,0xC0     ;11 =    600 baud
1D89: 12 00 60      0188              .DB  0x12,0x00,0x60     ;10 =   1200 baud
1D8C: 24 00 30      0189              .DB  0x24,0x00,0x30     ; 9 =   2400 baud
1D8F: 48 00 18      0190              .DB  0x48,0x00,0x18     ; 8 =   4800 baud
1D92: 96 00 0C      0191              .DB  0x96,0x00,0x0C     ; 7 =   9600 baud
1D95: 14 00 08      0192              .DB  0x14,0x00,0x08     ; 6 =  14400 baud
1D98: 19 00 06      0193              .DB  0x19,0x00,0x06     ; 5 =  19200 baud
1D9B: 38 00 03      0194              .DB  0x38,0x00,0x03     ; 4 =  38400 baud
1D9E: 57 00 02      0195              .DB  0x57,0x00,0x02     ; 3 =  57600 baud
1DA1: 11 00 01      0196              .DB  0x11,0x00,0x01     ; 2 = 115200 baud
1DA4: 00 00 00      0197              .DB  0x00,0x00,0x00     ; 1 = 230400 baud N/A
                    0198  ;
                    0199

                    0032  
                    0033              .CODE
                    0034  
                    0035  ; Startup message
1DA7: 52 43 32 30   0036  szStartup:  .DB "RC2014",kNull
1DAB: 31 34 00      7475  
                    0037  
                    0038  
                    0039  ; Hardware initialise
                    0040  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0041  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL not specified
                    0042  ;             IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0043  ; Identify and initialise console devices:
                    0044  ;   Console device 1 = Serial device at $80 (SIO port A or ACIA #1)
                    0045  ;   Console device 2 = Serial device at $80 (SIO port B)
                    0046  ;   Console device 3 = Serial device at $40 (ACIA #2)
                    0047  ;   Console device 3 = Serial device at $C0 (ACE #1) added by Colin C MacArthur
                    0048  ; Sets up hardware device flags:
                    0049  ;   Bit 0 = Serial 6850 ACIA #1 detected at $80
                    0050  ;   Bit 1 = Serial Z80 SIO   #1 detected at $80
                    0051  ;   Bit 2 = Serial 6850 ACIA #2 detected at $40
                    0052  ;   Bit 3 = Serial 16550 ACE #1 detected at $C0 added by Colin C MacArthur
1DAE:               0053  Hardware_Initialise:
1DAE: AF            0054              XOR  A
1DAF: 32 AF FE      0055              LD   (iHwFlags),A   ;Clear hardware flags
                    0056  ; Look for SIO2 type 2 (official addressing scheme)
1DB2: CD D3 1C      0057              CALL RC2014_SerialSIO2_Initialise_T2
1DB5: 20 0A         0058              JR   NZ,@NoSIO2T2   ;Skip if SIO2 not found
1DB7: 21 AF FE      0059              LD   HL,iHwFlags    ;Get hardware flags
1DBA: CB CE         0060              SET  1,(HL)         ;Set SIO2 present flag
1DBC: 21 1F 1E      0061              LD   HL,@PtrSIO2T2  ;Pointer to vector list
1DBF: 18 0D         0062              JR   @Serial4       ;Set up serial vectors
0000:               0063  @NoSIO2T2:
                    0064  ; Look for SIO2 type 1 (original addressing scheme)
1DC1: CD 8F 1C      0065              CALL RC2014_SerialSIO2_Initialise_T1
1DC4: 20 0C         0066              JR   NZ,@NoSIO2T1   ;Skip if SIO2 not found
1DC6: 21 AF FE      0067              LD   HL,iHwFlags    ;Get hardware flags
1DC9: CB CE         0068              SET  1,(HL)         ;Set SIO2 present flag
1DCB: 21 17 1E      0069              LD   HL,@PtrSIO2T1  ;Pointer to vector list
1DCE: 06 04         0070  @Serial4:   LD   B,4            ;Number of jump vectors
1DD0: 18 0F         0071              JR   @Serial        ;Set up serial vectors
0000:               0072  @NoSIO2T1:
                    0073  ; Look for 6850 ACIA #1
1DD2: CD 37 1C      0074              CALL RC2014_SerialACIA1_Initialise
1DD5: 20 0F         0075              JR   NZ,@NoACIA1    ;Skip if 6850 not found
1DD7: 21 AF FE      0076              LD   HL,iHwFlags    ;Get hardware flags
1DDA: CB C6         0077              SET  0,(HL)         ;Set 6850 present flag
1DDC: 21 2B 1E      0078              LD   HL,@PtrACIA1   ;Pointer to vector list
1DDF: 06 02         0079              LD   B,2            ;Number of jump vectors
                    0080              ;JR   @Serial       ;Set up serial vectors
                    0081  ; Set up jump table for serial device #1 or #1+#2
1DE1: 3E 10         0082  @Serial:    LD   A,kFnDev1In    ;First device jump entry
1DE3: CD 5B 01      0083              CALL InitJumps      ;Set up serial vectors
0000:               0084  @NoACIA1:
                    0085  ; Look for 6850 ACIA #2
1DE6: CD 4D 1C      0086              CALL RC2014_SerialACIA2_Initialise
1DE9: 20 0F         0087              JR   NZ,@NoACIA2    ;Skip if 6850 not found
1DEB: 21 AF FE      0088              LD   HL,iHwFlags    ;Get hardware flags
1DEE: CB D6         0089              SET  2,(HL)         ;Set 6850 present flag
1DF0: 21 2F 1E      0090              LD   HL,@PtrACIA2   ;Pointer to vector list
1DF3: 06 02         0091              LD   B,2            ;Number of jump vectors
1DF5: 3E 14         0092              LD   A,kFnDev3In    ;First device jump entry
1DF7: CD 5B 01      0093              CALL InitJumps      ;Set up serial vectors
0000:               0094  @NoACIA2:
                    0095  ; Look for 16550 ACE #1                   added by Colin C MacArthur
1DFA: CD 29 1D      0096              CALL RC2014_SerialACE1_Initialise
1DFD: 20 0F         0097              JR   NZ,@NoACE1     ;Skip if 16550 not found
1DFF: 21 AF FE      0098              LD   HL,iHwFlags    ;Get hardware flags
1E02: CB DE         0099              SET  3,(HL)         ;Set 16550 present flag
1E04: 21 27 1E      0100              LD   HL,@PtrACE1    ;Pointer to vector list
1E07: 06 02         0101              LD   B,2            ;Number of jump vectors
1E09: 3E 10         0102              LD   A,kFnDev1In    ;First device jump entry
1E0B: CD 5B 01      0103              CALL InitJumps      ;Set up serial vectors
0000:               0104  @NoACE1:
                    0105  ; Test if any console devices have been found
1E0E: 3A AF FE      0106              LD   A,(iHwFlags)   ;Get device detected flags
1E11: B7            0107              OR   A              ;Any found?
1E12: C0            0108              RET  NZ             ;Yes, so return
                    0109  ; Indicate failure by turning on Bit 0 LED at the default port
1E13: AF            0110              XOR  A              ;Output bit number zero (A=0)
1E14: C3 B4 04      0111              JP   PrtOSet        ;Turn on specified output bit
                    0112  ; Jump table enties
1E17:               0113  @PtrSIO2T1:
                    0113  @PtrSIO2T1: ; Device #1 = Serial SIO/2 channel A
1E17: A7 1C         0114              .DW  RC2014_SerialSIO2A_InputChar_T1
1E19: B7 1C         0115              .DW  RC2014_SerialSIO2A_OutputChar_T1
                    0116              ; Device #2 = Serial SIO/2 channel B
1E1B: AF 1C         0117              .DW  RC2014_SerialSIO2B_InputChar_T1
1E1D: C5 1C         0118              .DW  RC2014_SerialSIO2B_OutputChar_T1
1E1F:               0119  @PtrSIO2T2:
                    0119  @PtrSIO2T2: ; Device #1 = Serial SIO/2 channel A
1E1F: EB 1C         0120              .DW  RC2014_SerialSIO2A_InputChar_T2
1E21: FB 1C         0121              .DW  RC2014_SerialSIO2A_OutputChar_T2
                    0122              ; Device #2 = Serial SIO/2 channel B
1E23: F3 1C         0123              .DW  RC2014_SerialSIO2B_InputChar_T2
1E25: 09 1D         0124              .DW  RC2014_SerialSIO2B_OutputChar_T2
0000:               0125  @PtrACE1:   ; Device #1 = Serial ACE #1 module  added by Colin C MacArthur
1E27: 4C 1D         0126              .DW  RC2014_SerialACE1_InputChar
1E29: 54 1D         0127              .DW  RC2014_SerialACE1_OutputChar
0000:               0128  @PtrACIA1:  ; Device #1 = Serial ACIA #1 module
1E2B: 63 1C         0129              .DW  RC2014_SerialACIA1_InputChar
1E2D: 73 1C         0130              .DW  RC2014_SerialACIA1_OutputChar
0000:               0131  @PtrACIA2:  ; Device #3 = Serial ACIA #2 module
1E2F: 6B 1C         0132              .DW  RC2014_SerialACIA2_InputChar
1E31: 81 1C         0133              .DW  RC2014_SerialACIA2_OutputChar
                    0134  
                    0135  
                    0136  ; Hardware: Set baud rate
                    0137  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0138  ;   On entry: A = Baud rate code
                    0139  ;             C = Console device number (1 to 6)
                    0140  ;   On exit:  IF successful: (ie. valid device and baud code)
                    0141  ;               A != 0 and NZ flagged
                    0142  ;             BC HL not specified
                    0143  ;             DE? IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1E33:               0144  Hardware_BaudSet:
1E33: F5            0145              PUSH AF             ;                 added by Colin C MacArthur
1E34: 79            0146              LD A,C              ; Console device number 
1E35: FE 03         0147              CP 3                ; Device #3 = ACE
1E37: 28 03         0148              JR Z, Hardware_BaudSet3
1E39: F1            0149              POP AF
1E3A: AF            0150              XOR  A              ;Return failure (A=0 and Z flagged)
1E3B: C9            0151              RET                 ;Abort as invalid baud rate
1E3C:               0152  Hardware_BaudSet3:
1E3C: F1            0153              POP AF
1E3D: C3 62 1D      0154              JP RC2014_SerialACE1_Hardware_BaudSet
                    0155  
                    0156  ; Hardware: Poll timer
                    0157  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0158  ;   On exit:  If 1ms event to be processed NZ flagged and A != 0
                    0159  ;             BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0160  ; Poll software generated timer to see if a 1ms event has occurred.
                    0161  ; We have to estimate the number of clock cycles used since the last
                    0162  ; call to this routine. When the system is waiting for a console input
                    0163  ; character this will be the time it takes to call here plus the time 
                    0164  ; to poll the serial input device. Lets call this the loop time.
                    0165  ; The rest of the time we don't know so the timer events will probably 
                    0166  ; run slow.
                    0167  ; We generate a 1000 Hz event (every 1,000 micro seconds) by 
                    0168  ; counting processor clock cycles.
                    0169  ; With a 7.3728 Hz CPU clock, 1,000 micro seconds is 7,373 cycles
1E40:               0170  Hardware_PollTimer:
1E40: 3A B0 FE      0171              LD   A,(iHwIdle)    ;Get loop counter
1E43: C6 07         0172              ADD  A,7            ;Add to loop counter
1E45: 32 B0 FE      0173              LD   (iHwIdle),A    ;Store updated counter
1E48: 38 02         0174              JR   C,@RollOver    ;Skip if roll over (1ms event)
1E4A: AF            0175              XOR   A             ;No event so Z flagged and A = 0
1E4B: C9            0176              RET
1E4C: F6 FF         0177  @RollOver:  OR    0xFF          ;1ms event so NZ flagged and A != 0
1E4E: C9            0178              RET
                    0179  
                    0180  
                    0181  ; Hardware: Output signon info
                    0182  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0183  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL not specified
                    0184  ;             IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1E4F:               0185  Hardware_Signon:
1E4F: 11 55 1E      0186              LD   DE,@szHardware ;Pointer to start up message
1E52: C3 28 04      0187              JP   OutputZString  ;Output start up message
1E55:               0188  @szHardware:
1E55: 5A 38 30 20   0189              .DB  "Z80 based RC2014 systems",kNewLine,kNull
1E59: 62 61 ...     7628  
                    0190  
                    0191  
                    0192  ; Hardware: Output devices info
                    0193  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0194  ;   On exit:  AF BC DE HL not specified
                    0195  ;             IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1E6F:               0196  Hardware_Devices:
1E6F: 21 AF FE      0197              LD   HL,iHwFlags    ;Get hardware present flags
1E72: 11 93 1E      0198              LD   DE,@szHw6850   ;Serial 6850 message Device #1
1E75: CB 46         0199              BIT  0,(HL)         ;Serial 6850 present?
1E77: C4 28 04      0200              CALL NZ,OutputZString ;Yes, so list it
1E7A: 11 AD 1E      0201              LD   DE,@szHwSIO2   ;Serial SIO/2 message
1E7D: CB 4E         0202              BIT  1,(HL)         ;Serial SIO/2 present?
1E7F: C4 28 04      0203              CALL NZ,OutputZString ;Yes, so list it
1E82: 11 E0 1E      0204              LD   DE,@szHw6850B  ;Serial 6850 message
1E85: CB 56         0205              BIT  2,(HL)         ;Serial 6850 present?
1E87: C4 28 04      0206              CALL  NZ,OutputZString  ;Yes, so list it
1E8A: 11 FA 1E      0207              LD   DE,@szHw16550  ;Serial 16550 message   added by Colin C MacArthur
1E8D: CB 5E         0208              BIT  3,(HL)         ;Serial 16550 present?
1E8F: C4 28 04      0209              CALL NZ,OutputZString ;Yes, so list it
1E92: C9            0210              RET
1E93: 31 20 3D 20   0211  @szHw6850:  .DB  "1 = 6850 ACIA #1   (@80)",kNewLine,kNull
1E97: 36 38 ...     7650  
1EAD: 31 20 3D 20   0212  @szHwSIO2:  .DB  "1 = Z80 SIO port A (@80)",kNewLine
1EB1: 5A 38 ...     7651  
1EC6: 32 20 3D 20   0213              .DB  "2 = Z80 SIO port B (@82)",kNewLine,kNull
1ECA: 5A 38 ...     7652  
1EE0:               0214  @szHw6850B:
1EE0: 33 20 3D 20   0214  @szHw6850B: .DB  "3 = 6850 ACIA #2   (@40)",kNewLine,kNull
1EE4: 36 38 ...     7653  
1EFA:               0215  @szHw16550:
1EFA: 31 20 3D 20   0215  @szHw16550: .DB  "1 = 16550 ACE #1   (@C0)",kNewLine,kNull
1EFE: 31 36 ...     7654  
                    0216  
                    0217  
                    0218  
                    0219  ; Initialise ROM paging
                    0220  ;   On entry: No parameters required
                    0221  ;   On exit:  BC DE HL IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
1F14:               0222  RomPageInit:
1F14: C9            0223              RET
                    0224  
                    0225  ; Fixed address to allow external code to use it
1F15:               0226  kTransCode:
                    0226  kTransCode: .EQU 0xFF80         ;Transient code area
                    0227  
                    0228  ; Execute code in ROM bank
                    0229  ;   On entry: A = ROM bank number (0 to 3)
                    0230  ;             DE = Absolute address to execute
                    0231  ;   On exit:  IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0232  ; WARNING: Not safe against interrupt changing config register
                    0233  ; First copy required utility function to RAM and then run it
                    0234  ; The ROM bank is selected and the code executed
1F15: D5            0235  RomExec:    PUSH DE
1F16: 21 25 1F      0236              LD   HL,@TransExec  ;Source: start of code to copy
1F19: 11 80 FF      0237              LD   DE,kTransCode  ;Destination: transient code area
1F1C: 01 07 00      0238              LD   BC,@TransExecEnd-@TransExec  ;Length of copy
1F1F: ED B0         0239              LDIR                ;Copy (HL) to (DE) and repeat x BC
1F21: D1            0240              POP DE
1F22: C3 80 FF      0241              JP  kTransCode
                    0242  ; Transient code copied to RAM before being executed
1F25:               0243  @TransExec:
                    0243  @TransExec: ;RLCA               ;Shift requested ROM bank number
                    0244              ;RLCA               ;  from  0b000000NN
                    0245              ;RLCA               ;  to    0b00NN0000
                    0246              ;RLCA
                    0247              ;LD   B,A           ;Store new ROM bank bits
                    0248              ;LD   A,(iConfigCpy)  ;Get current config byte
                    0249              ;LD   (iConfigPre),A  ;Store as 'previous' config byte
                    0250              ;AND  0b11001111    ;Clear ROM bank bits
                    0251              ;OR   B             ;Include new ROM bank bits
                    0252              ;LD   (iConfigCpy),A  ;Write config byte to shadow copy
                    0253              ;OUT  (kConfigReg),A  ;Write config byte to register
1F25: 01 86 FF      0254              LD   BC,kTransCode+@TransRet-@TransExec
1F28: C5            0255              PUSH BC             ;Push return address onto stack
1F29: D5            0256              PUSH DE             ;Jump to DE by pushing on
1F2A: C9            0257              RET                 ;  to stack and 'returning'
0000:               0258  @TransRet:  ;LD   A,(iConfigPre)  ;Get previous ROM page
                    0259              ;LD   (iConfigCpy),A  ;Write config byte to shadow copy
                    0260              ;OUT  (kConfigReg),A  ;Write config byte to register
1F2B: C9            0261              RET
1F2C:               0262  @TransExecEnd:
                    0263  
                    0264  
                    0265  ; Copy from ROM bank to RAM
                    0266  ;   On entry: A = ROM bank number (0 to 3)
                    0267  ;             HL = Source start address (in ROM)
                    0268  ;             DE = Destination start address (in RAM)
                    0269  ;             BC = Number of bytes to copy
                    0270  ;   On exit:  IX IY I AF' BC' DE' HL' preserved
                    0271  ; WARNING: Not safe against interrupt changing config register
                    0272  ; First copy required utility function to RAM and then run it
1F2C: C5            0273  RomCopy:    PUSH BC
1F2D: D5            0274              PUSH DE
1F2E: E5            0275              PUSH HL
1F2F: 21 40 1F      0276              LD   HL,TransCopy   ;Source: start of code to copy
1F32: 11 80 FF      0277              LD   DE,kTransCode  ;Destination: transient code area
1F35: 01 03 00      0278              LD   BC,TransCopyEnd-TransCopy  ;Length of copy
1F38: ED B0         0279              LDIR                ;Copy (HL) to (DE) and repeat x BC
1F3A: E1            0280              POP  HL
1F3B: D1            0281              POP  DE
1F3C: C1            0282              POP  BC
1F3D: C3 80 FF      0283              JP   kTransCode
                    0284  ; Transient code copied to RAM before being executed
0000:               0285  TransCopy:  ;PUSH BC            ;Preserve number of bytes to copy
                    0286              ;RLCA               ;Shift requested ROM bank number
                    0287              ;RLCA               ;  from  0b000000NN
                    0288              ;RLCA               ;  to    0b00NN0000
                    0289              ;RLCA
                    0290              ;LD   B,A           ;Store new ROM bank bits
                    0291              ;LD   A,(iConfigCpy)  ;Get current config byte
                    0292              ;LD   C,A           ;Store as 'previous' config byte
                    0293              ;AND  0b11001111    ;Clear ROM bank bits
                    0294              ;OR   B             ;Include new ROM bank bits
                    0295              ;OUT  (kConfigReg),A  ;Write new config byte to register
                    0296              ;LD   A,C           ;Get 'previous' config byte
                    0297              ;POP  BC            ;Restore number of bytes to copy
1F40: ED B0         0298              LDIR                ;Copy (HL) to (DE) and repeat x BC
                    0299              ;OUT  (kConfigReg),A  ;Restore 'previous' config byte
1F42: C9            0300              RET
1F43:               0301  TransCopyEnd:
                    0302  
                    0303  
                    0304  ; **********************************************************************
                    0305  ; **  Public workspace (in RAM)                                       **
                    0306  ; **********************************************************************
                    0307  
                    0308              .DATA
                    0309  
                    0310  ; Hardware flags
                    0311  ; Bit 0 = Serial 6850 ACIA #1 detected
                    0312  ; Bit 1 = Serial Z80 SIO   #1 detected
                    0313  ; Bit 2 = Serial 6850 ACIA #2 detected
                    0314  ; Bit 3 = Serial 16550 ACE #1 detected
                    0315  ; Bit 4 to 7 = Not defined, all cleared to zero
FEAF: 00            0316  iHwFlags:   .DB  0x000          ;Hardware flags
                    0317  
FEB0: 00            0318  iHwIdle:    .DB  0              ;Poll timer count
